| Dollar base and premium states (rev 7, 1.6b) | `lnS_{j,b}`, `ln PT_b`, `fx_0`, `Frisk_b`, `z_{j,b}` and the trade `z_level_base` are captured by `WorldBlock::capture_bases` at the end of the last burn-in month; `qpsi`, `zeta`, the `UsHist.a` ring and `T_t` are zero at the base; `Frisk` runs from creation (shadow) while `world_link_dollar` is true |
| Dollar two phases (rev 7, 2.6) | phase A (premium, addend, projection) runs after `net::us_inputs` and before the bloc step; phase B (bilateral rates, `ln D`, `w.row.fx`) after it; both inside `world::step`, after the skipped `exchange_rate` slot and before `banking::set_rates` and `goods::clear` |
| Policy-surprise injection into `f_t` | done in `after_exog`, after `factors_step` has used `f_t` this month for `tp_level` and `risk_premium`; those two see it from the next month. |

`sim::PHASES` becomes 21 names with `"world"` inserted after `"fx"`; `step_timed` gets `[f64; 21]`; `world::after_exog` is called inside the existing `exog` entry (a closure) and `world::settle` inside the existing `firms.close` closure, so no further names are needed; `crates/econsim-cli/src/bin/profile.rs` prints indices `0..20` (it prints all but the metrics phase). `step` and `step_timed` must keep the same order.

| Id | File, anchor | Change (stage) |
|---|---|---|
| H1 | `lib.rs` | `pub mod world;` (1) |
| H2 | `sim.rs` `step`, `step_timed` | calls above; `monetary::exchange_rate` guarded by `!world::dollar_active(w)` (`dollar_active` is `#[inline(never)]`) (1; dollar_active used from 4) |
| H3 | `exog.rs` `step`, line `w.exog.oil_price = ...` | guard `if !crate::world::oil_active(w)`; the legacy oil draws (`rng::normal` on EXOG, `oil_month` with its two streams) are still taken so that every later EXOG draw (risk premium, TFP, tech regime) is unchanged (3) |
| H4 | `types.rs` | `World.world: Option<Box<WorldBlock>>`; no change to `Flows` except `imports_by_sector: [f64; N_SECTORS]` (stage 2, `#[serde(default)]`) |
| H5 | `monetary.rs` | `update_curve`: `tp_official` is folded into the base loop's `tp` sum, right after the `INFLATION_RISK_PREMIUM` term, as `let tp = if let Some(fo) = world_tp_fo(w, j) { tp + fo } else { tp };` (the later factor pass recomputes yields from `term_premium` and keeps it) (4); `pub(crate)` on `TP_SLOPE`, `trade_with_banks`, `trade_with`; **`expected_rate_path`** (1; all three branches, D33 fallback): `update_curve` stores its locals in a `Copy` struct `CurveInputs { gap_on: bool, base: f64, i_star: f64, i_inf: f64, flat: f64 }` on `w.monetary` (one `#[inline(never)] fn record_curve_inputs(w, ..)` call right after `i_star` is computed; no arithmetic change) and `pub fn expected_rate_path(w: &World, out: &mut [f64])` reads it (section 2.1); `rebalance_row` (rev 8, D45): `let (bonds, dep) = match crate::world::world_row_extra(w) { Some(x) => (w.row.bonds_value + x, w.row.deposits - x), None => (w.row.bonds_value, w.row.deposits) };` then `target = bonds / 12.0 + ROW_BUFFER_GDP_MONTHS * gdp` with `dep` in both comparisons (the rev-7 form shifted the target only); `world_tp_fo(w, j) -> Option<f64>` and `world_row_extra(w) -> Option<f64>` are `#[inline(never)]` wrappers in `world/mod.rs` that return `None` unless `finance_active(w)`; `rebalance_row` becomes `pub(crate)` for the tests (4) |
| H6 | `goods.rs` `clear`, the block that computes `imp_rel`, `terms_ratio`, `tr_now` | when `trade_active`: `imp_rel_s` comes from `world.out.imp_rel[s]` and `st.import_rel = Some(world.out.imp_rel_cpi)` (read by `firms.rs:940` for firm input prices and `metrics.rs:1738` for the import price index); `import_pass_through` is not called and `st.pt_z`, `st.fx_clear` are left alone (2). `terms_ratio` keeps `fxb.terms[s] * fxb.import_price`, the base sector price. The base-month legacy import level is the anchor of `imp_rel` (section 2.5), so the terms ratio is continuous at reported month 0 (S2-R1). Earlier text (removed): at the first reported world step `world::step` **overwrote** `w.monetary.fx_base = Some(FxBase { import_price: p_world / fx, terms: fx * P_s / p_world })` (the fields are `pub`; `FxBase::capture` is private) and sets `w.trade_import_z = None` (the lagged logs restart at the current value), so the terms ratio is 1 at reported month 0 when the blocs are at zero (D36); in dollar mode `exchange_rate` never runs, so it cannot capture the base (2; the dollar part 4). `world::step` no longer writes `fx_base` or `trade_import_z` (F1, F5). |
| H7 | `goods.rs` `clear`, step 8 exports | when `trade_active`: `exports[s] = world.out.exports[s]` (nominal, model units); `export_z`/`trade_export_z` untouched (2) |
| H8 | `goods.rs` `clear`, the line `let tariff = ...shocks.tariff` (joint3 line 614); `firms.rs` `goods_input_mult` (`tau_t`, line 995); `metrics.rs` `tariff_rate_pct` (line 1729) [verify@M3] | one helper `world::tau_for(w, Channel) -> f64` (`#[inline(never)]`): when `trade_active` it returns `world.out.tau_eff_hh` (the household duty in `clear`, and `tariff_rate_pct`) or `world.out.tau_eff_firm` (the `tariff` argument of `firm_stage`, and `firms.rs`), otherwise the literal `finite_or(w.shocks.tariff, 0.0).max(0.0)` expression of each site; without this the firms price imported inputs without the duty they pay under a bilateral `Tariff` (2) |

Both routings (`firm_stage` and b7-spikes F2 `route_pooled`) are covered; the test sums to `w.row.imports_this_month` (2) |

**Hook discipline (review problem 3).** Every new function called from an existing hot function (`world::tau_for`, `oil_active`, `trade_active`, `dollar_active`, `record_curve_inputs`, `world_tp_fo`, the H6/H7/H9 helpers) is `#[inline(never)]`: b7-transmission needed this on `lot_duration_ratio` and `pinned_expected` to keep the default md5. After **each** hook is added, run I1 (md5 of `run 5000 60 3 --json | tail -n 1` at RAYON 1 and 8) and make a WIP commit (`wip: hook H<n>`); the WIP commits are squashed into the stage's commits at the end. **Stop rule:** if I1 changes with no logic change, add `#[inline(never)]` to the function the hook calls before debugging anything else.

### 1.5 Config block (`// b8-world`, appended at the end of `Config` and `Default`; every doc comment names its source path)

Defaults are the pre-registered values; "mult" keys scale the research/P\* tables at run time (changing a structural coefficient needs `solve_blocs.py` and a regenerated JSON, section 1.7). Types: `bool`, `f64`, `String`.

| Key | Default | Stage | Meaning (source) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `world_block_enabled` | false | 1 | master switch |
| `world_link_trade` / `world_link_oil` / `world_link_dollar` / `world_link_finance` | false | 2 / 3 / 4 (4-1) / 4 (4-2) | link switches. `world_link_dollar` owns the dollar module, the `F` filters, the injections of the policy surprise, `RiskOff` and `BlocDemand{risk_off}` into `f_t`, and the dollar term of `UsCredibility`; `world_link_finance` owns official demand, `tp_official`, `OfficialTreasury` and the term-premium term of `UsCredibility` (rev 7, D42) |
| `world_retaliation`, `world_positions`, `world_fund_foreign_equity` | false | 4-3 / 4-3 / 4-4 | modules. `world_retaliation` needs `world_link_trade` (CLI exit 2, server 400); on with no flagged `Tariff` the output is byte-identical to off (D51, D54). `world_positions` is memo only (D52) |
| `world_spillback_off` | false | 4 | measurement switch for W4-18: the blocs' US inputs (`ius`, `ex`, `y_US`) and the policy-surprise injection are replaced by their no-shock values; all US-bound links stay on |
| `world_uae_in_opec` | true | 1 | D7 |
| `world_shock_scale` | 1.0 | 1 | multiplies every bloc shock sd (`blocs.<b>.shocks_monthly_sd.*`); 0 draws still taken |
| `world_theta_mult`, `world_lam_rp_mult`, `world_kus_mult`, `world_oil_is_mult` | 1.0 | 1 | runtime multipliers on P\* `theta`, `lambda_rp`, `k_us`, IS oil coefficients |
| `world_theta_f_ratio` | 0.5 | 4 | [assumed] share of the policy-channel loading `theta` applied per 13 VIX points of non-policy risk |
| `world_rho_f`, `world_rho_p`, `world_rho_oil`, `world_rho_qty` | 0.93, 0.95, 0.98, 0.97 | 1 | projection persistences (D17) |
| `world_okun_ratio` | 2.0 | 1 | D18 |
| `world_ius_offset` | -0.40 | 1 | pp; D28: the mean of `100 (policy_rate - i_inf)` over months 0-239 and 12 seeds of master runs, measured once by `scripts/world/measure_ius_offset.py` (-0.40, SE 0.09) [verify@stage1] |
| `world_opec_va_share` | 0.20 | 1 | [assumed] oil value added share of OPEC+ GDP for the production channel |
| `world_china_risk_pts_per_pct` | 6.0 | 4 | [assumed] VIX points added per 1% of China peak shortfall (BlocDemand CN with `risk_off`) |
| `world_origin_sigma_sr`, `world_origin_sigma_lr`, `world_origin_speed` | 0.5, 2.0, 0.05 | 2 | origin substitution (`elasticities.us_imports.origin_substitution_elasticity` 2.53 is the FGKK long run; 2.0 hits the Boehm et al. bilateral profile) |
| `world_dcp_kappa_usd`, `world_dcp_speed_usd` | 0.47, 0.08 | 2 | `pass_through.us_import_price_from_bloc_formula.G_usd_long_run`, `pass_through.us_nonfuel_import_price_to_broad_dollar.monthly_speed_to_long_run` |
| `world_dcp_kappa_nonusd`, `world_dcp_speed_nonusd` | 0.95, 0.9 | 2 | `...G_nonusd_long_run`; speed [assumed] (month-1 pass-through 0.86 vs 0.96) |
| `world_export_income_elast` | 1.4 | 2 | `elasticities.us_exports.foreign_income_elasticity` |
| `world_export_price_sr`, `world_export_price_lr`, `world_export_fx_sr`, `world_export_fx_lr`, `world_export_speed` | 0.10, 0.90, 0.15, 0.30, 0.04 | 2 | calibrated to W2-02 (hand calc -3.2/-4.5/-5.4, `scripts/world/trade_hand.py`); fx SR from `pass_through.dominant_currency_volume_response.bilateral_volume_elasticity_to_importer_usd_rate_impact`, LR [assumed] |
| `world_us_import_channel` | true | 2 | D27 |
| `world_oil_profile` | "default" | 3 | `default`, `supply_heavy`, `low_demand` (= `alternatives.low_demand_elasticity`), `high_demand` (= `alternatives.high_demand_elasticity`), `shale_boom`, `shale_discipline` (oil `alternatives.<name>.changes`) |
| `world_opec_regime` | "price_defence" | 3 | or `market_share` (oil `supply.opec.market_share_unwind_rate`) |
| `world_oil_exact_path` | true | 3 | legacy `OilPrice` presets pin the monthly-average price (section 2.4) |
| `world_oil_mean_offset` | 8.355 | 3 | D6 (`anchors.stochastic_mean_offset`) |
| `world_oil_sigma_mult` | 1.0 | 3 | multiplies all market shock scales; legacy `oil_sigma` is ignored |
| `world_oil_rho_q`, `world_oil_sig_i_mult` | 0.99, 1.0 | 3 | pre-authorised levers for `oil_lvl` (D5): `shocks.oil_specific.rho_q` (to 0.995) and the oil-specific shock scale `sig_I` (to 1.1). One spelling for the field and the JSON key (rev 6; was `world_oil_sigI_mult`) |
| `world_oil_feedback` | true | 3 | rev 6 (S3-R2): the market reads the blocs' output gaps and real exchange rates; false is the exact-path interface test only |
| `world_oil_shock_mask` | `IDO` | 3 | rev 6 (W3-19): the structural sources drawn into the market (I, D, O); the others are the variance-share cells |
| `world_oil_qty_offset` | -6.627 | 3 | rev 6 (S3-R8 option b): stochastic mean of the OPEC+ quantity input, subtracted when `stochastic_oil` is on (measured on seeds 1-200, checked on 201-400) |
| `world_oilcheck_activity_sigma`, `world_oilcheck_activity_rho` | 1.6657, 0.9943 | 3 | `shocks.activity.{sigma, rho_y}`; used by the isolated `oil-check` market only, drawn on `WORLD_OIL_S` (D5) |
| `world_vix_per_unit`, `world_vix_per_100bp` | 10.0, 13.0 | 4 | `global_financial_cycle.vix_points_per_unit_f`, `us_policy_spillovers.vix_response_pts_per_100bp` |
| `world_fx_risk_mult`, `world_fx_risk_rho`, `world_fx_risk_asym` | 1.0, 0.9, 0.588 | 4 | `dollar.risk_off_loading_gamma_pct_per_vix_pt`, `dollar.risk_premium_persistence_rho_monthly`, 1/`global_financial_cycle.dollar_vix_asymmetry_broad` (= 0.588); lever grid for `world_fx_risk_rho` {0.90, 0.93, 0.95} (D35) |
| `world_fx_rp_sd_mult` | 1.0 | 4 | scales bloc UIP premium innovations |
| `world_fx_idio_sd_ae`, `world_fx_idio_sd_eme`, `world_fx_idio_sd_cn`, `world_fx_idio_sd_opec`, `world_fx_idio_rho` | 1.5, 2.0, 0.3, 0.3, 0.99 | 4 | D15, % per month [assumed]; AE = EA, JP, UK, CA, AU; EME = MX, IN, KR, BR, RoW; CN and OPEC+ have their own (W4-06: CN band [0.3, 2.0]); the innovation sd of the AR(1) `zeta` (unconditional sd 10.6 % AE, 14.2 % EME, 2.1 % CN); `zeta` is zero at the base (1.6b) |
| `world_fo_theta_stock`, `world_fo_theta_trans`, `world_fo_trans_rho` | 13.0, 20.0, 0.944 | 4 | `treasury_demand.official_demand_term_premium_coefficients` (bp per 1% of GDP; monthly persistence of a 12-month half-life) |
| `world_fo_dollar_resp`, `world_fo_vix_resp`, `world_fo_trend_cn` | -0.0023, 1.05, -80.0 | 4 | `treasury_demand.reserve_manager_response_to_dollar...` (applied to the monthly change of ln D, D32), `global_financial_cycle.foreign_official_ust_holdings_per_vix_point_usd_bn_per_month`, `treasury_demand.china_secular_decline` ($bn per year, real-world units; capped by the CN tranche, D44) |
| `world_gdp_rw_bn` | 32560.0 | 4 | rev 7 (D39): real-world annualised GDP in $bn behind the unit conversions of 2.7 and 2.8 (finance `cross_border.aggregate_iip_2026Q2.niip_tn` unit text: $32.56tn) |
| `world_dollar_neutral` | true | 4-1b | rev 9 (D47): the dollar link reads each bloc's `z` against its conditional forecast `z_fc` (false: against its base value, the rev-7 form) |
| `world_rp_sat_knee`, `world_rp_sat_scale` | 0.02, 0.015 | 4-1b | rev 9 (D48): knee and scale of the bound on `ex[k] + lambda_rp gp[k]` in the bloc dollar equation, decimal a year (the M-F constants of D33); active with `world_link_dollar`; scale 0 = off; server and CLI refuse values outside [0, 0.2] |
| `world_ret_eps_sr`, `world_ret_eps_lr`, `world_ret_speed` | 1.0, 3.0, 0.10 | 4-3 | D9, D51; validated (rev 10): each elasticity 0 to 10 and the long run at least the short run, the speed 0 to 1; levers {2, 3, 4} and {0.05, 0.10, 0.20} (S4-k) |
| `world_import_gap_offset` | empty (profiles: twelve values, D57) | 5 | per-bloc centring constants of the coupled US import-demand term, pp, in bloc order EA CN JP UK CA MX IN KR BR AU OPEC+ RoW; subtracted from the clamped term once it is active (`TradeState::pre_bloc`); validated (empty or twelve finite values in [-10, 10]); the server answers 400, the CLI exits with status 2 |
| `world_import_rel_drift` | 0 (profiles: 0.0032, D62) | 5-T2 | drift of the block's import price against PT, decimal a year (positive: imports get cheaper against PT); `imp_rel_s` is multiplied by `exp(-g n / 12)`, `n` = reported months since the base; validated finite and within +-0.05; the server answers 400, the CLI exits with status 2 |
| `world_sleeve_target`, `world_sleeve_speed`, `world_sleeve_div_yield` | 0.172, 0.10, 0.02 | 4e | foreign equity / US equity value (14.7% of the portfolio [derived]), rebalancing speed [assumed], dividend yield [assumed] |


World profiles `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s4a.json` (4-1), `b8-world-s4a-mf.json` (adds `fx_rate_knee` 0.02 and `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0.015 only; W4-28), `b8-world-s4b.json` (4-2: `b8-world-s4a.json` plus `world_link_finance` true, the dollar link off, S4-R15), `b8-world-s4b-trend0.json` (plus `world_fo_trend_cn` 0, the S4-h lever) and `b8-world-s4c.json` (4-3: `b8-world-s4a.json` plus `world_retaliation` true and `world_positions` true, S4-R24) carry the stage-3 keys (`world_link_oil` false) and also set `fin_factor_rho` 0.83, `fin_factor_sigma` 0.30, `fin_jump_intensity` 0.06 (rev 7), `fin_jump_mean` 1.3, `fin_jump_sd` 0.5, `erp_fin_loading` 0.0028 (D21). These are ABM keys: every matched cell carries them (D41). Stage 1-3 profiles leave all ABM keys alone. Rev 9 (4-1b): `b8-world-s4d.json` = `b8-world-s4a.json` plus `world_link_dollar` true (the new keys at their defaults); `b8-world-s4d-mf.json` = that plus `fx_rate_knee` 0.02 and `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0.015 (W4-28); `legacy_s4a_dollar.json` = the two-key overlay `{"world_dollar_neutral": false, "world_rp_sat_scale": 0.0}` (the 4-1 link, identity I6 only). A stop rule of 4-1b ships `world_link_dollar` false in `b8-world-s4d.json` (it is then `b8-world-s4a.json`). **Rev 11 (D57, D58):** `b8-world-s5a.json` = `b8-world-s4c.json` plus the twelve `world_import_gap_offset` constants; `b8-world-s5b.json` = `b8-world-s4e.json` plus `fx_rate_knee` 0.02, `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0.015 and the constants; `b8-world-s5c.json` = `b8-world-s5b.json` plus `world_link_finance` true; fallbacks `b8-world-s5a-nc.json` (`world_us_import_channel` false), `b8-world-s5c-nd.json` (`b8-world-s5a.json` plus finance) and the reported lean arm `b8-world-s5a-lean.json` (`b8-world-s5a.json` without the six financial-factor keys); block-off references `b8-world-wabm.json`, `b8-world-wabm-mf.json`, `b8-world-wl.json`; `legacy_b8off.json` resets every ABM key a world profile moves (the financial-factor keys are zero in the J4 default).

### 1.6 Shocks, metrics, streams, init, determinism

**Shock variants** (all `#[serde(tag = "type")]`, bloc names `"EA","CN","JP","UK","CA","MX","IN","KR","BR","AU","OPEC+","RoW"`; each validated like the existing ones):

| Variant | Fields | Meaning | Stage |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BlocPolicy` | `bloc`, `bps`, `months` in {3, 6, 12, 18, 24} | rate held `bps` above the rule for `months`, then released; uses `pin` tables | 1 |
| `BlocDemand` | `bloc`, `pct`, `half_life_months`, `risk_off` (default false; true for CN adds a risk-off jump) | IS shock scaled so the isolated bloc's peak gap equals `pct` | 1 |
| `GlobalDemand` | `pct`, `half_life_months` | `BlocDemand` applied to all 12 blocs (never to the ABM) | 1 |
| `Tariff` | `partner: Option<bloc>`, `sector: Option<usize>`, `pct`, `retaliate` | bilateral partner-sector duty component, **added** on top of the set-level `ImportTariff` level (`w.shocks.tariff`, replaced not added, unchanged semantics, D34); `pct` in percentage points on the shock, decimal (0.25 = 25%) everywhere inside the block; with `world_retaliation` a component flagged `retaliate` drives the partner's duty on US exports (2.5) | 2 (retaliation 4-3) |
| `OilSupply` | `pct_of_world_supply` (loss > 0, SPR release < 0), `ramp_months`, `hold_months`, `recover_months` | disruption path `s_D` | 3 |
| `OpecCut` | `mbd` (cut > 0), `months` (phase-in, >= 1) | policy surprise `eps^O = -(mbd/103.1)*100 / months` % of Q in each of `months` months | 3 |
| `RiskOff` | `vix_points` | jump in `f_t` of `vix_points/world_vix_per_unit` | 4 |
| `UsCredibility` | `tp_bp`, `months`, `dollar_pct` | profile `p_t` = 1 for `months`, then half-life 3 months: term-premium shock `tp_bp * p_t TP_SLOPE[j]/TP_SLOPE[3]` on tenor `j` (`world_link_finance`, rev 8, D46) and `ln(1 - dollar_pct/100) * p_t` added to the common dollar premium `q_t` (`world_link_dollar`, D31) | 4 |
| `OfficialTreasury` | `bloc: Option` (a bloc name or null), `bn` (signed purchases, real-world units, in total), `months` | extra official flow of `bn/months` a month for `months` months from the shock month, to the bloc, or pro rata to the blocs' tranches when null; validated: bloc valid, `abs(bn)` <= 2000, `months` in [1, 60]; ignored with `world_link_finance` off (rev 8) | 4 |

Existing shocks keep their meaning (`Dollar` is the level addend of section 2.2, `OilPrice` the exact-path target).

**Metrics.** `Metrics.world: Option<WorldMetrics>`; absent from the JSON when the block is off (so the default `run 5000 60 3 --json` md5 is unchanged). Keys (all finite numbers; arrays in bloc order): `y_gap[12]` (% output gap), `pi_gap[12]` (annualised pp, deviation from the bloc's target), `i_gap[12]` (pp, deviation of the policy rate from its steady state; the gap the gates W1-13..W1-15 use), `z[12]` (% real exchange rate against the dollar, plus = depreciation), `infl[12]` (level, annualised pp = `pi*_j + pi_gap`), `policy[12]` (level, pp = `neutral_real_rate_j + pi*_j + i_gap`, floored at `policy_rate_floor`), `s_index[12]` (`exp(ln S_j - bases.ln_s[j])`, foreign currency per USD, **1.0 at the first reported step**), `p_index[12]` (`exp(ln P_j - bases.ln_p[j])`, 1.0 at the first reported step), `headline[12]` (level, annualised pp: `infl + 12 omega^E phi (0.6 dc_t + 0.4 dc_{t-1})`, equal to `infl` in stage 1 because the oil gap is 0), `u_gap[12]` (pp, Okun gap, from its own state); stage 2: `exports_bn[12]`, `imports_bn[12]` (annualised $bn, scaled), `r_import[12]`, `tau_eff_hh`, `tau_eff_firm`, `tau_by_origin[12]`, `import_volume_idx`, `import_price_idx`, `ret_tau[12]`, `us_import_gap[12]`; stage 3: `p_bar_pct`, `brent_usd` (2024 $), `oil_supply_pct`, `oil_demand_pct`, `opec_out_mbd`, `us_prod_mbd`, `stocks`, `spare_pct`; stage 4a (rev 7): `f_vix` (F_t, VIX points), `f_risk` (Frisk_t - Frisk_b), `fx_broad` (100 w.row.fx / fx_0 with or without the dollar link, so that the matched cell reports the ABM's own dollar; 100 at the base month and before it), `a_dollar` (100 a_t), `s_bil[12]` (exp(lnS_{j,t}) with premium and noise; 1.0 at the base month); stage 4b (rev 8): `fo_bn` (FO^rw_t, $bn rw), `fo_flow_bn` (executed flow of the month, $bn rw), `tp_fo_bp` and `tp_cred_bp` (the 10y terms in force in the month's curve, bp), `fo_extra` (cumulative net official sales at market value, model units), `f_ring_mean` (the six-month mean of F - Fbar, VIX points), `row_mv_gdp_pct`, `row_dep_gdp_pct` (RoW's bond market value and deposits, % of annualised model GDP), `fo_share_row_pct` (tranche over RoW's market value), `row_face_share_pct` (RoW's share of the face debt); stage 4c (rev 10): `ret_tau[12]` (the mean of `tau*_{j,s}` over the goods sectors Energy..Tech weighted by `x0_{s,j}`: the average retaliatory duty on US goods exports to the bloc, decimal; zero while the switch is off) and `exports_goods_bn[12]` (annualised $bn of Energy..Tech exports by destination bloc, always filled); stage 4d (rev 10): `niip_pct_gdp` (the memo net international investment position, % of annualised model GDP), `ca_pct_gdp` (the current account with the interest paid to RoW and the memo primary income, % of GDP), `ca_bilateral_bn[12]` (annualised $bn), `sfc_world_error` (the largest identity residual of the month over monthly GDP), all zero while `world_positions` is off; stage 4e: `sleeve_bn`. All are `#[serde(default)]`. Stage 1 ships only the first group.

**Streams.** `agent_rng(w.seed, w.tick, stream::WORLD_BLOC, bloc_index)` draws, in this fixed order, four standard normals per bloc and month (demand, cost-push, policy, risk-premium innovation) **whether or not the scale is zero**; oil: `WORLD_OIL_I` (oil-specific innovation, Student-t scale draw, jump count and sizes), `WORLD_OIL_S` (disruption jumps, OPEC+ surprise, activity not drawn: it comes from the blocs); dollar disconnect noise `WORLD_FX` (12 normals); the `f_t` draws stay on `FIN_FACTOR`. The world code is serial; no float reduction depends on thread count. The stream-collision test must pass with the new ids: `WORLD_BLOC = 0x574F_524C_0000_0009`, `WORLD_OIL_I = 0x574F_524C_0000_000A`, `WORLD_OIL_S = 0x574F_524C_0000_000B`, `WORLD_FX = 0x574F_524C_0000_000C` (every existing id differs from these at bit 40 or above, or in the low 20 bits; the four differ from each other in the low bits).

**Init.** `world::init_if_enabled` (after `calibrate_purchases`, hook H12) builds the state at tick 0: bloc states zero (`BlocHist` zero-filled), `UsHist.u` filled with `w.unemployment_rate()` three times and `UsHist.ius`, `y_us`, `a` with zeros, RoW Treasury and deposit splits from the ledger (stage 4). The block steps through the 144 burn-in months in shadow, so month 0 of the reported run is a stochastic state; tick-reset safe: no absolute ticks are stored (shock timers are counters). **Bases and burn-in: D36.** `ln P_j` and `ln S_j^bloc` accumulate from creation; nothing reads them before `bases` exists; `Bases` is captured once, at the first `world::step` with `reported(w)` (`bases.is_none()`).

**Clone and compare.** `World: Clone` carries the block; both arms of `compare` share the same draws.

### 1.6b Stage-4 continuity (S2-R1 and S3-R1 applied; rev 7, D37)

Stage 2 failed on a month-0 level reset and stage 3 needed a decaying splice. Stage 4 has neither, by one rule that every test below checks: **a link may add to an ABM quantity the change of a block state since the base month, never the level of a block state; a state that has a level is captured from the ABM's own values at the end of the last burn-in month (the base month b; `WorldBlock::capture_bases`, called where `tr.capture` is called today and as the first-step fallback of a world without burn-in), or it is an innovation-only state that is zero at the base.** The two arms of a matched pair (the shocked and the unshocked world of one seed, and the two arms of `compare` after the warm-up) share the burn-in, the warm-up and every draw, so every captured value and every state is bit-identical in the two arms up to the shock month; the shock enters additively through a named input and the pair difference is the response. No capture may depend on a shock (no lazy capture at the first shock). Measured at `ccdcc48` (24 seeds, 5000 households, shadow run, all links off, the pre-flight note (not published) section 3): the weighted bloc real exchange rate is +14.9 % (sd 11.2) above its steady state at the base month and unwinds over 60 months, so a dollar built on the level of `z` would jump by 15 % at month 0; the base-relative `ln D` moves 0.14 % (sd 0.65, max 2.13) in month 0 against -0.11 % (sd 1.65, max 3.5) for the ABM's own `ln fx`. No splice is needed: the time mean of the base-relative `ln D` over months 12-239 (-7.9 %) agrees with the ABM's own (-7.2 %).

| State | Level? | Rule | Pair property | Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| bilateral `lnS_j`, broad `ln D`, `w.row.fx` | yes (nominal) | `lnS_{j,b}`, `ln PT_b`, `fx_0` captured at the end of the last burn-in month by `capture_bases` (not in the stage-1 `Bases`, which is captured at the first reported step); `ln D_t = sum_j wH10_j lnS_{j,t}` with `lnS_j` built from the changes of `P_j`, `PT` since the base and from `z_j - z_fc,j` (2.6; rev 9: the change of `z` is read against its conditional forecast, D47, not against `z_b`) | `ln D` differs by the shock terms only | `dollar_base_captured_at_last_burn_in_month`, `dollar_first_month_is_continuous` |
| the forecast `z_fc,j(m)` (rev 9, D47) | path, shock-independent | computed once at the base capture from the captured state (`WorldBlock::forecast`: the settle branch of a world with a burn-in, the first-step fallback otherwise, never by a shock); `z - z_fc` is an innovation-only state, zero at the base | one array, shared by the clones (`Arc`) | `forecast_exists_only_with_the_link_and_neutral_on`, `forecast_reproduces_the_block`, `forecast_error_is_the_response_to_the_news_alone`, `forecast_is_shock_independent_and_shared`, `first_month_forecast_error_is_innovation_sized`, `step_month_passes_the_forecast_to_the_trade_module` |
| `qpsi` (psi premium and M-X `z`) | AR(1), unit-root-like | innovation-only, zero through burn-in (`fx::anchored_premium`; `fx_psi_state`, `fx_psi_component`, `fx_mp_prev` are 0 at the base): the master convention, unchanged | same psi draws; `z` is 0 without a `PolicyShift` | `dollar_psi_zero_through_burn_in` |
| `F_t` (VIX points) | the ABM's own `f_t` | none: `w.exog.fin_factor` is an ABM state through burn-in; levels enter only as `F - Fbar` (`Fex`, the VIX flow term) | same draws | `fin_factor_moments_with_world_profile` |
| `Frisk`, `g_j` | asymmetric filter; mean about +9 VIX points | the filter runs from creation, every month, while `world_link_dollar` is true (shadow in burn-in); `g_j = world_fx_risk_mult (gamma_j/100)(Frisk_t - Frisk_b)`; `Frisk_b` captured at the base. An anchored zero start would build the mean as a +1.7 % dollar drift over the first two years | `Frisk_t - Frisk_b` has mean about 0 in the baseline; a `RiskOff` jump adds `vix_points` to `e_t` | `frisk_runs_in_shadow_and_is_based` |
| `zeta_j` | AR(1), unconditional sd 10-14 % | zero at the base, starts at the first reported month; draws keyed `(seed, tick, WORLD_FX, j)` | same draws | `zeta_noise_ar1_sd` |
| `UsHist.a` ring | log points | zero through burn-in (the block sees `a = delta = 0`) | | `dollar_addend_seen_by_blocs` |
| trade `z~`, `zrel` in dollar mode | yes | `z_level_base_j = z_{j,b}` captured with the trade base (`TradeBase`); `zrel_j = z_j - z_{j,b} + 100 a_{j,t}`; rev 9 (D47): `zrel_j = z_j - z_fc,j + 100 a_{j,t}` (`TradeState::post_bloc_ref`) | | `trade_ztilde_dollar_mode_zero_at_base`, `trade_zrel_reads_the_forecast` |
| official tranche `H_j`, `k_FO`, `FO^rw` | yes | captured at the end of the last burn-in month from the ledger (`w.row.bonds_value`) and normalised to the sourced 3773.1 (2.7); kept as the memo path `h_j,b tau_t/tau_b` plus a deviation `dH_j` that is 0.0 until a flow executes; **not** captured at creation (RoW holds 30 % of the debt at creation and 37.6 % of the face debt, 42.8 % of GDP, at the base) | tranche and deviation identical in both arms | `fo_base_captured_and_normalised`, `fo_stock_ratio_constant_in_baseline` |
| `T_t`, `ln D_{t-1}` of the flow rule, `tau_{t-1}`, ring of 6 values of `F - Fbar` | | `T_b = 0`, `ln D_b = 0` (the ABM's own `ln(fx/fx_0)` with the dollar link off: the first reported month carries its month-0 step), `tau_{-1} = tau_b`; the ring is filled every month from the ABM's `f_t` (burn-in included) and includes the current month | | `fo_first_month_is_continuous`, `vix_ring_filled_in_shadow` |
| `tp_FO` | stock term relative to base | `stock = 100 sum dH_j/k_FO / GDP^rw`: the memo path and `GDP^rw` share `tau_t/tau_b`, so `tp_FO = 0.0` bit for bit at the base and without world flows | | `fo_stock_ratio_constant_in_baseline` |
| RoW book (deposits, bonds) and `extra` | yes (ABM state) | not captured: the ABM's own book runs through the burn-in; `extra = 0` at the base; `rebalance_row` works on `deposits - extra` and `bonds_value + extra`, so the private book is the same in both arms and the pair difference in RoW's bonds is the executed official flow (6.5 % of a sale was lost with the rev-7 hook) | identical up to the shock month | `rebalance_row_hook_keeps_extra_deposits`, `official_execute_is_ledger_consistent` |
| `UsCredibility` term-premium components, `OfficialTreasury` programmes | | registered in `after_exog` from `world_shocks` by a cursor of their own; none exists at the base | | `credibility_tp_part`, `official_treasury_shock_path` |
| memo positions (nine stocks) | yes | memo stocks = real-world value x `k_GDP,b`, `k_GDP,b = 12 tau_b / world_gdp_rw_bn`, `tau_b` the trend GDP at the capture (rev 10, D52), captured once at the end of the last burn-in month (the `base_month` branch of `settle`; a world without burn-in in `capture_bases`, where the first update has no previous marks and its residual is set to 0); the two ledger stocks are read from the ledger every month, not captured; `NIIP_{t-1}`, `ln fx_{t-1}`, `share_price_{t-1}` and RoW's bonds with their prices are captured at the same month; the accumulators of the ledger interest and of the auction purchases are reset every month | the same stocks and references in both arms; the noise `eta` is keyed by (seed, tick, `WORLD_FX`, 16 + j) | `positions_initial_ratios_and_memo_stocks_at_base`, `positions_burn_in_zero_world_captures_at_its_first_step`, `positions_pair_identical_before_shock` |
| retaliation `hist`, `tau*`, `zr`, `R` (rev 10) | yes (levels) | zero at creation and through the burn-in; the state steps only in reported months after the trade base exists and is a function of the active flagged `Tariff` components alone (fixed weights, D51); with none, `tau*` = `zr` = 0.0 and `R` = 1.0 exactly; nothing is captured | the components exist only after the flagged shock fires; before `T0` the two arms are bit-identical; at `T0` each export cell to the retaliator differs by `exp(-1.2 x_s)` | `retaliation_off_or_unflagged_is_bit_identical`, `retaliation_pair_identical_before_shock` |
| Fund sleeve units and price | yes | carved out of the Fund's existing equity at the base, no cash move (2.8) | identical | `sleeve_first_month_is_continuous` |

**Pair identity test** `dollar_pair_identical_before_shock` (and its 4-2..4-4 versions): two worlds, same seed and config, one with the stage-4 shock scheduled at month `T0 >= 1`; every stage-4 output and state (`w.row.fx`, `ln D`, `a`, `Frisk`, `FO`, `tp_FO`, positions) is bit-identical for months `< T0`, and at `T0` the difference equals the analytic shock term to 1e-12 (`RiskOff`: `F` jumps by exactly `vix_points`; `Dollar`: `delta` by `ln(1 + pct/100)`; `UsCredibility`: `cred` by `ln(1 - dollar_pct/100)`). The same test runs through `sim::compare` with `warmup_months` 12: the two arms are identical up to the shock month. The 4-2 versions are `finance_pair_identical_before_shock` (`OfficialTreasury` and `UsCredibility`; the difference at `T0` is the analytic flow 100.0 or term 22.0 bp) and the identity I5 (`finance_on_zero_drivers_equals_off`, and 8 long seeds in the brief: the finance link with every driver at zero changes no non-world key). The 4-3 versions are `retaliation_pair_identical_before_shock` (a flagged `Tariff`; the difference at `T0` is the factor `exp(-1.2 x_s)` on each export cell to the retaliator) and `positions_pair_identical_before_shock` (a `Dollar` shock; the difference of the memo valuation at `T0` is `-sum fc X D(ds) + (net equity exposure) D(r_us)`, computed in the test from the worlds' own `w.row.fx` and share prices), each also through `sim::compare` with `warmup_months` 12 where the brief says so, and the identity I7 (the two switches on with no flagged shock equal both off in every non-world key; 8 long seeds in the brief, the registered 64 in the measurement).

### 1.7 Parameter pipeline (no retyping)

`scripts/world/build_world_params.py` reads the four research `params.json` plus the CSVs listed in 1.1 and writes `world/data/world_params.json`; `scripts/world/solve_blocs.py` reads the same plus `scripts/world/pstar.json` (the P\* table, the lead modeller's `pstar_blocs.json`) and writes `world/data/bloc_solution.json` (K = 240 kernels, the 5 x 4 tail vectors, T = 720). Both write a `calibration_hash` (sha256 of the canonical input JSON) and the `--check` flag recomputes and compares it (a per-commit check, section 6). The map of embedded keys to research paths is `param_map`; the main paths:

| Embedded block | Research path (copy by path) |
|---|---|
| bloc steady states, monthly coefficients, shocks | `blocs.<id>.{steady_state, quarterly_coefficients_gpm_form, shocks_monthly_sd, oil.IS_oil_coefficient_monthly, oil.energy_cpi_weight, okun}` and `blocs.<id>.monthly_coefficients` (checked against the converter) |
| spillover and REER weights | `blocs/data/bloc_spillover_coeffs_2024.csv`, `bloc_trade_weights_2024.csv` |
| GDP weights (market, PPP) | `blocs.<id>.weights.share_world_pct_{usd,ppp}_2024` |
| P\* (financial channel, structure changes) | `scripts/world/pstar.json` (table 2.2.4); defaults `blocs.<id>.IS_curve.theta_global_financial_channel`, `FX.lambda_rp_on_G`, `policy_rule.k_us_policy_rate_passthrough` |
| trade matrices, invoicing, elasticities | `trade.sector_shares.matrices.{imports,exports}_base_C26_in_Tech.usd_bn_2024`, `bloc_goods_matrix.{matrix_2024,import_shares_2024}`, `invoicing.theta_usd_share_of_bloc_exports_to_US`, `pass_through.*`, `elasticities.*`, `retaliation.recommended_rule`, `tariffs.yale_partner_effective_rates_end_2026_pre_substitution_pct` |
| oil | `oil.{anchors, time_aggregation, demand_aggregate, supply, inventories, shocks, us_links, blocs.*, alternatives.*}` (bloc elasticity columns ignored, D12) |
| finance | `finance.{us_policy_spillovers, global_financial_cycle, treasury_demand, dollar, spillbacks, cross_border}` |

Bloc id mapping used by the script: oil `euro_area, China, Japan, UK, Canada, Mexico, India, Korea, Brazil, Australia, OPEC+, rest_of_world`; finance `euro_area, china, japan, uk, canada, mexico, india, korea, brazil, australia, opec_plus, rest_of_world`; trade retaliation `EU -> EA`; blocs/trade `EA, CN, JP, UK, CA, MX, IN, KR, BR, AU, OPEC+, RoW`.

### 1.8 Oil options and presets: on and off

| Existing element | Block off | Block on, `world_link_oil` on |
|---|---|---|
| `stochastic_oil`, `oil_ar`, `oil_sigma`, `oil_t_nu`, `oil_garch_alpha/beta`, `oil_jump_*` | master process | **ignored** (legacy draws still taken, results discarded). `stochastic_oil=false` -> no structural shocks and no mean offset |
| `OilPrice{pct, hl}` ("Oil +50%") | `oil_shock` log added to the AR(1) price | exact path: the monthly-average price of the shocked arm exceeds the unshocked structural price by exactly `100 ln(1+pct/100) 0.5^(t/hl)` (shadow copy, section 2.4) |
| `oil_base`, `oil_index_to_world`, fuel line, extraction labour, `world_energy_oil_elasticity` | unchanged | unchanged; `exog.oil_price = oil_base * oil_index * exp((p_bar - offset)/100)` |
| `exog.oil_ar`, `exog.oil_shock` | master states | `oil_ar := (p_bar_A - offset)/100`; `oil_shock` unchanged (display only) |
| WTI vs Brent | n/a | `brent_usd = 80.53 * exp(p_bar/100)` is reported; the ABM keeps its WTI-like `oil_base` $65 |
| Hormuz, SPR, OPEC+ cut, global demand | not available | `OilSupply`, `OpecCut`, `GlobalDemand` shocks (presets, section 5) |

### 1.9 Stage-1 definitions: schemas, types, accessors, tools

Everything a stage-1 engineer would otherwise have to guess. Bloc order everywhere: EA, CN, JP, UK, CA, MX, IN, KR, BR, AU, OPEC+, RoW (`NB = 12`); `K = 240`.

**JSON schemas** (written by `build_world_params.py` and `solve_blocs.py`, embedded with `include_str!`, read with serde).

`world_params.json`:
```
{ "meta":    { "calibration_hash": "<64 hex>", "k": 240, "k_tail": 480, "t_kernel": 720, "blocs": [12 names], "pstar_sha": "<16 hex>" },
  "blocs":   [ { "name": "EA", "inflation_target", "neutral_real_rate", "policy_rate_floor",               // blocs.<id>.steady_state.* (pp)
                 "monthly": { "b1","b2","b3","b4","b5","l1","l2","l3","g1","g2","g4","phi","uip_w","theta" },     // blocs.<id>.monthly_coefficients.* (research values; theta before the P* scale)
                 "pstar":   { "theta_scale","lam_rp","k_us","g_z","uip_w","oil_is_scale" },                       // pstar.json, uip_w resolved to a number
                 "sd":      { "demand","cost_push","policy","risk_premium" },                                      // blocs.<id>.shocks_monthly_sd.{sd_demand, sd_cost_push, sd_policy, sd_risk_premium}
                 "okun":    { "a1","a2" },                                                                         // blocs.<id>.okun.{alpha1_lag_monthly, alpha2_gap_monthly}; u_t = a1 u_{t-1} - a2 y_t
                 "oil":     { "is_coef","energy_cpi_weight","phi_fuel" },                                          // IS_oil_coefficient_monthly, energy_cpi_weight, oil blocs.<name>.crude_share_of_fuel_price_phi
                 "weight_ppp","weight_usd" } ],                                                                   // blocs.<id>.weights.share_world_pct_{ppp,usd}_2024 / 100
  "network": { "s_us": [12], "s_bb": [[12]x12], "w_us": [12], "w_bb": [[12]x12] },        // see "Orientation" below
  "financial": { "rho_g": 0.9283, "rho_rp": 0.9283 },                                        // 0.8^(1/3), blocs global.financial_channel.monthly, FX.risk_premium_shock_AR_quarterly
  "opec": { "uae_in": { ... }, "uae_out": { ... } } }                                        // stage 3
```
Effective coefficients at run time: `theta_i = monthly.theta * pstar.theta_scale * world_theta_mult`, `k_us,i = pstar.k_us * world_kus_mult`, `lambda_rp,i = pstar.lam_rp * world_lam_rp_mult`, `beta^oil_i = oil.is_coef * pstar.oil_is_scale * world_oil_is_mult`; the kernels depend only on `g_z` and `uip_w` (structural), never on these.

`bloc_solution.json`:
```
{ "meta":  { "calibration_hash", "k": 240, "k_tail": 480, "t_kernel": 720, "pstar_sha", "outputs": ["y","pi","i","z","mrr"],
             "state": "y lags 1..3, i lag 1, z lags 1..13, pi lags 1..13, mrr lags 1..3 (33)" },
  "blocs": { "EA": { "c":    [[33]x5],                    // rows = outputs, columns = state in the order above
                     "ir":   { "is": [[5]x240], "pc": [[5]x240], "rule": [[5]x240], "v": [[5]x240] },   // row k = period-0 response (y,pi,i,z,mrr) to a unit impulse at period k (v = UIP equation, solver key "S")
                     "tail": { "is": [5], "pc": [5], "rule": [5], "v": [5] },                          // sum of the rows k = 240..479
                     "pin":  { "3": [3], "6": [6], "12": [12], "18": [18], "24": [24] } } } }           // rule innovations (pin_eps with T = 480)
```
`calibration_hash` = sha256 of the UTF-8 bytes of: the four research `params.json` re-serialised with `json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))` in the order blocs, trade, oil, finance; the same serialisation of `pstar.json`; the raw bytes of the five bloc CSVs in the placement-table order; and the string `K=240;KT=480;T=720;TP=480;`. The same hash goes into both files.

**Rust types** (`world/`): 
```rust
pub const K: usize = 240; pub const NB: usize = 12;
pub struct Forcing  { pub is: [f64; K], pub pc: [f64; K], pub rule: [f64; K], pub v: [f64; K] }         // the four forcing paths f_e[k]
pub struct UserPaths<'a> { pub pin: &'a [f64; K], pub uis: &'a [f64; K] }                               // sums over the bloc's active BlocPolicy / BlocDemand / GlobalDemand shocks, aligned to this month
pub struct UsHist   { pub ius: [f64; 4], pub y_us: [f64; 4], pub u: [f64; 3], pub a: [f64; 14] }       // index m = month t-1-m; a = Dollar-shock level, log points
pub struct BlocHist { pub y: [f64; 14], pub pi: [f64; 14], pub i: [f64; 14], pub z: [f64; 14], pub mrr: [f64; 14] }   // m = 0 is month t-1; zero-filled at creation
pub struct BlocState{ pub s: [f64; 33], pub hist: BlocHist, pub g: f64, pub u_rp: f64, pub u_okun: f64, pub ln_p: f64, pub ln_s: f64 }
pub struct Net      { pub f: [[f64; 3]; NB], pub wz: [[f64; 13]; NB] }                                    // f[i][m] = F_{i,t-1-m} incl. s_us y_US(t-1-m); wz[i][m] = sum_{o != i} w_io z_{o,t-1-m}
pub struct Bases    { pub ln_p: [f64; NB], pub ln_s: [f64; NB], pub ln_pt: f64, pub fx: f64, pub p_world: f64 /* stage 2 adds base_s: [f64; N_SECTORS] */ }
pub struct WorldOut { pub ius: f64, pub y_gap: [f64; NB], pub pi_gap: [f64; NB], pub i_gap: [f64; NB], pub z: [f64; NB] /* stage 1; later stages add fields named in their Files paragraph */ }
pub struct CurveInputs { pub gap_on: bool, pub base: f64, pub i_star: f64, pub i_inf: f64, pub flat: f64 }  // Copy; w.monetary.curve_inputs
#[inline(never)] pub fn record_curve_inputs(w: &mut World, gap_on: bool, base: f64, i_star: f64, i_inf: f64, flat: f64)
```
`Peff_{i,j}` for `j <= t-1` is `net.wz[i][t-1-j] - 100 a_j w_us[i]` (the Dollar addend with a common `a`: `100 a (sum_o w_io - 1) = -100 a w_us[i]`); beyond `t-1` it is `net.wz[i][0] world_rho_p^(j-t+1) - 100 a_hat_j w_us[i]` with `a_hat_j = a_t d^(j-t)` for `j > t`, `a_hat_t = a_t`.

**Orientation.** `s_bb[i][o]` and `w_bb[i][o]` are `CSV.loc[row = i, column = o]` of `bloc_spillover_coeffs_2024.csv` (row label `receiver_i`) and `bloc_trade_weights_2024.csv` (row label `bloc`): `i` is the receiving / own bloc, `o` the partner; `s_us[i]`, `w_us[i]` are the `US` column; diagonals are 0; the rows `(w_us[i], w_bb[i][*])` sum to 1 (to 1e-4). The CSVs also have a `US` row, which is dropped.

**Accessors and definitions.**
- `policy_rate_t = w.cb.policy_rate` (decimal) read in `world::step`, after `monetary::set_policy` of the same month; `i_inf` as in 2.1.
- `y_US(t-1)`: `u_{t-1} = w.unemployment_rate()` (decimal, the last recorded month, which is month `t-1` inside `world::step` because `metrics::record` of month `t` runs later) is pushed into `UsHist.u` at the start of `us_inputs`; `u*_t = w.natural_unemployment()`; `y_US = 100 * okun_ratio * (u*_t - mean(UsHist.u))` with the ring after the push shifted so that it holds `u_{t-1}, u_{t-2}, u_{t-3}`. At the first step the ring is filled with `u_{t-1}` three times, so `y_US` starts at `100 okun_ratio (u* - u_{t-1})`. Okun for reporting uses `okun.a1` and `okun.a2`.
- Dollar shock: `a_t = w.shocks.dollar_log()` after `activate_due` (exog runs before `world::step`); `d_t = if w.shocks.dollar_decay == 0.0 { 1.0 } else { w.shocks.dollar_decay }` (half-life 0 means permanent); future levels `a_t d_t^k`. A scheduled `Dollar` shock is not anticipated before it fires. `UsInputs` carries `dollar = a_t` and `dollar_decay = d_t`.
- Isolation mode: `bloc::build_forcing(bs, p, &UsInputs, net: Option<&Net>, &UserPaths, &mut Forcing)`; `net = None` sets `F = Peff = 0` and is used with `UsInputs::zero()` by `own_hold12_*`, the unit-IRF test, `bloc::demand_scale` and `bloc_demand_peak_equals_pct`; `bloc::isolated_run(p, sol, &UserPaths, eps, months) -> Vec<[f64; 5]>` wraps it.
- `bloc::demand_scale(p, sol, half_life, pct)`: isolated run over `max(36, ceil(4 half_life))` months, at most 240, of the unit path `0.5^(k/half_life)` in `fIS` with the tail term; the scale is `pct / max_t y_t`.
- Shock validation (`Shock::validate`, error strings in the existing style): `BlocPolicy`: bloc in the 12 names, `|bps| <= 2000`, `months` in {3, 6, 12, 18, 24}. `BlocDemand`: bloc valid, `|pct| <= 20`, `half_life_months` in [1, 120], `risk_off` defaults to **false** for every bloc (the CN preset sets it to true; there is no CN default). `GlobalDemand`: `|pct| <= 20`, `half_life_months` in [1, 120]. `Tariff`: partner null or valid, sector null or < 9, `pct` in [0, 500], `retaliate` defaults to false. `OilSupply`: `pct_of_world_supply` in [-30, 50], `ramp_months`, `hold_months`, `recover_months` >= 1. `OpecCut`: `|mbd| <= 10`, `months` >= 1. `RiskOff`: `vix_points` in [0, 100]. `UsCredibility`: `tp_bp` in [0, 200], `months` >= 1, `dollar_pct` in [0, 50). `OfficialTreasury`: bloc null or valid, `|bn| <= 5000`, `months` >= 1. With the block off the new shocks validate and are ignored.
- `sim.rs`: `PHASES: [&str; 21]` (`"world"` after `"fx"`), `step_timed(w, t: &mut [f64; 21])` with `fns: [fn(&mut World); 21]`; the `exog` entry becomes `|w| { exog::step(w); world::after_exog(w); }`, the `fx` entry `|w| { if !world::dollar_active(w) { monetary::exchange_rate(w); } }`, and the `firms.close` closure ends with `world::settle(w)`. `crates/econsim-cli/src/bin/profile.rs`: `let mut t = [0.0; 21];` and `(0..20)` (all phases but the last, `metrics`). `step` and `step_timed` keep the same order.
- `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s1.json` = `{"world_block_enabled": true, "world_ius_offset": <value measured in step 0>}`; all links stay at their default false. Profiles are flat key-to-value objects passed as `ECONSIM_CFG="$(cat FILE)"` (the existing mechanism, `Config::from_env`).
- Step 0 setup: the 3.0 setup items (2)-(3) come first (worktree, frozen base binaries from the `M3` build into `$S/bin/base-*`); then `mkdir -p $S/ius0` (`$S` = a scratch directory of the run).

**Tools** (interfaces; every tool exits non-zero on failure):

| Tool | Command | Reads | Writes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `build_world_params.py` | `[--out PATH] [--check]` | research `params.json` x4, 5 bloc CSVs, `pstar.json` | `world_params.json` |
| `solve_blocs.py` | `[--out PATH] [--check] [--bloc B]` | the same, via `world_ref.solve_horizon(q, g_z, Kk=480, T=720)` and `pin_eps(q, L, g_z, T=480)` | `bloc_solution.json` |
| `make_golden.py` | `[--check]` | the same | runs `world_ref.py goldens --write` (or `--check`) and `trade_hand.py --write` and, from stage 3, `oil_reference.py --golden` |
| `world_ref.py` | `selftest`, `gates`, `w109`, `kcheck`, `goldens`, `gen-inputs clean` or `gen-inputs stress`, `--inputs FILE`, `centring` | as above | stdout; goldens with `--write` |
| `measure_ius_offset.py` | `--files F...` or `--runs DIR --tag T`, optional `--seeds 12` | ABM run JSON (the last line of `run 20000 240 S --json`) | stdout |
| `econsim-cli world-dump` | `HOUSEHOLDS MONTHS SEED [SHOCK_JSON]` | config from `ECONSIM_CFG` (the stage profile); `SHOCK_JSON` is a path to a JSON array of `ScheduledShock` | the replay file of 2.1 on stdout, one JSON line per month |

**`--check` (both builders).** Exit 0 iff (a) the hash recomputed from the inputs equals `meta.calibration_hash` in the committed file and (b) regenerating the file in memory gives the same JSON structure (keys, array lengths) with every number within `1e-10` absolute plus `1e-10` relative of the committed one (the stacked LU solves are not bit-reproducible across BLAS builds, so bit identity is not required). `make_golden.py --check` applies the same rule to the goldens with tolerance `1e-9`.

---

## 2. Equations

Conventions. Month `t`; bloc `i, j, o`; sector `s` (Energy 0 ... Construction 8). Rates and inflation are annualised percentage points (pp); gaps and `z` are percent; `z` = 100 x log real exchange rate against the dollar, **plus = depreciation of the bloc currency**; `S` = foreign currency per USD (1.0 at creation; a stronger dollar is a higher `S` and a higher ABM `fx`). "Known at `t`" means computed before the bloc step of month `t`. `K = 240` is the forecast horizon of the offline solution (plus the tail term of section 2.2.3). Every coefficient is a path into `params.json` (section 1.7) unless marked.

### 2.1 US inputs (`net.rs::us_inputs`)

```
i_inf_t   = monetary::current_r_star(w) + w.monetary.pi_lr.unwrap_or(inflation_target)      // decimal
ius_t     = 100 (policy_rate_t - i_inf_t) - world_ius_offset                                // pp, realised, after set_policy (D28)
ex[k]     = 100 (expected_rate_path[k] - i_inf_t) - world_ius_offset d_k,  k = 0..K-1       // pp; d_k = 0.97^k (rule_path_decay^k when expected_rate_path uses it)
y_US(t-1) = 100 * okun_ratio * (u*_t - mean(u_{t-1}, u_{t-2}, u_{t-3}))                      // %, stored in a ring; u* = w.natural_unemployment()
PT        = role_mean(tradable_weight, sector_price) of the latest clearing (month t-1); every PT below means this lagged value
```
`ex[0] = ius_t` away from the zero floor and `ex[k] -> 0`. With b7-transmission M-F on (D33), `ius_t` and every `ex[k]` pass through `100 * bounded_rate_differential(x/100, fx_rate_knee, fx_rate_sat_scale)` before they enter `fS` and `fR` (identity at the default `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0); `G` and the dumped `UsInputs` keep the unbounded values.

**`UsInputs`** (returned by `us_inputs`, written by `econsim-cli world-dump`, read by the Python reference):
```rust
pub struct UsInputs { pub ius: f64, pub ex: [f64; K], pub y_us: f64, pub oil: f64, pub qty: f64, pub fex: f64, pub dollar: f64, pub dollar_decay: f64 }
// ius, ex: pp, offset applied.  y_us = y_US(t-1) in %.  oil: p_bar gap in % (0 before stage 3).  qty: OPEC+ output deviation in % (0 before stage 3).
// fex: VIX points of non-policy risk (0 before stage 4).  dollar: level a_t of the Dollar shocks in log points, common to all blocs; dollar_decay: the monthly decay d_t in effect (1.0 if unset; section 1.9).
```
The replay file is JSON lines: line 0 `{"header": {k, rho_f, rho_p, rho_oil, rho_qty, ius_offset, fx_rate_knee, fx_rate_sat_scale, pstar_sha, shocks}}` (shocks: the active BlocPolicy / BlocDemand / GlobalDemand, each with its `t0`), then one line per month `{t, ius, ex[240], y_us, oil, qty, fex, dollar, dollar_decay, eps, out}` where `eps` is null or the 12 x 4 innovations (`e_y, e_pi, e_i, eta`) of the month and `out` the month's `y, pi, i, z` of the 12 blocs. The format is the docstring of `scripts/world/world_ref.py`; worked examples are `inputs_in2019_clean.jsonl` and `inputs_stress.jsonl` (placement table, section 1.1). The reference applies the M-F bound to `ius` and `ex` in the rule and UIP forcing only, from the header knee and scale. `world-dump` runs with `world_shock_scale` 0 (no `eps`), or writes `eps`.

**`expected_rate_path(w, out)`** (H5) fills `out[k]` for `k < out.len()` with the monthly rate that `update_curve` averages into `curve.expected[j]` (`curve.expected[j]` is the mean of the first `TENORS[j]` entries, `TENORS` = [3, 24, 60, 120, 360] months). It reads the `CurveInputs` that `update_curve` stored in the same month. Three branches, as in `update_curve`:

1. **Rule branch (`gap_on`)**, as `floored_expected`: `out[k] = max(i_inf + (i_star - i_inf) dk + (base - i_star) rho^k + policy_gap(tick + k), 0)` with `rho = cfg.rate_smoothing` and `dk = 0.97^k` (`rule_path_decay^k` only when `rule_path_weight > 0` and `rule_path_decay` is finite and differs from 0.97).
2. **Override branch, `pin_path_on` off** (`flat > 0`, `gap_on` false), as `expected_average`: `out[k] = base` for `k < flat`, then `i_inf + (base - i_inf) 0.97^(k - flat)`.
3. **Override branch with `pin_path_on`** (b7-transmission M-P, D33), as `pinned_expected` (`monetary.rs:451-466`; the same recurrence is in `expected_rate_path`, `monetary.rs:606-615`; checked line by line at `ccdcc48`, the formula needs no change): `out[k] = i_inf + (base - i_inf) a_k` with `a_0 = p_0 = 1` and, after each `k`, `pn = if k + 1 < flat { p (1 - h) } else { 0 }; a = pn + (p - pn) + rho (a - p); p = pn` (`h = pin_exit_hazard`, `rho = pin_exit_decay`). **Since joint calibration 4 this is the default branch under any rate override** (`pin_path_on` true, `h` 0.0417, `rho` 0.9; before J4: false, 0, 0.97; `scripts/profiles/legacy_b7pinoff.json` restores them). Hand values for `flat = 24` with the J4 defaults: `a_0..a_4 = 1, 1, 0.995830, 0.988081, 0.977277` and `a_22..a_26 = 0.601555, 0.580576, 0.560062, 0.504056, 0.453650` (the pin is priced as the rule releases it, with a 4.2 % monthly exit hazard). With `h = 0` and `rho = 0.97` the branch equals branch 2 (`pinned_expected_tests`, `monetary.rs:1739`).

`base`, `i_star`, `i_inf`, `flat` and `gap_on` are `update_curve`'s locals: `base = rule - gap_now` with the shadow rate rounded to `RATE_STEP` (or `cb.policy_rate` under an override), `i_star = (1 - rule_path_weight) base + rule_path_weight i_star_smooth`, `flat = until - tick` under an override. **Test** `expected_path_means_equal_curve_expected`: with a **360-length** slice (the 30-year tenor needs 360 entries) the mean of the first `TENORS[j]` entries equals `curve.expected[j]` to 1e-10 for all five tenors in each of the three branches (rule branch with and without a policy gap and at the zero floor; override branch with `pin_path_on` set to false by the test; override branch with `pin_path_on` true, the J4 default, and the `a_k` hand values above). The blocs use `out[0..239]`.

### 2.2 The bloc model (`bloc.rs`, solved offline by `solve_blocs.py`)

#### 2.2.1 Equations (GPM6 form, monthly; deviations from steady state)

With monthly coefficients `b1..b5, l1, l2, l3, g1, g2, g4, phi, uip_w` (`blocs.<id>.monthly_coefficients`, converted by the root-matching rules of blocs REPORT 3.2), `rho_z = ((1-phi)/phi)^(1/3)`, `kappa = uip_w (1-rho_z)/(12 (2 phi - 1))`:

```
rr_t    = i_t - (1/3) sum_{j=1..3} pi_{t+j}
rr12_t  = (1/12) sum_{j=0..11} rr_{t+j}
mrr_t   = 0.10 rr_t + 0.35 rr12_t + (0.35/3) sum_{m=0..2} rr12_{t+12m} + (0.20/5) sum_{m=0..4} rr12_{t+12m}
IS      y_t  = b1 y_{t-1} + b2 y_{t+1} - b3 mrr_{t-3} + (b4/12) sum_{l=2..13} z_{t-l} + fIS_t
PC      pi_t = (l1/12) sum_{j=2..13} pi_{t+j} + ((1-l1)/12) sum_{j=2..13} pi_{t-j} + l2 y_{t-3} + (l3/4)(z_t - z_{t-12}) + fPC_t
RULE    i_t  = g1 i_{t-1} + (1-g1)[ (1+g2) (1/12) sum_{j=-1..10} pi_{t+j} + g4 y_t + g_z z_t ] + fR_t
UIP     z_t  = rho_z z_{t-1} - kappa V_t ;   V_t = rr_t + V_{t+1} + fS_t      (V = present value of expected real-rate gaps; the solver's equation key is "S"; S_j below is always foreign currency per USD)
Okun    u_t  = a1 u_{t-1} - LR (1-a1) y_t        (reporting only; blocs.<id>.okun)
```
`g_z` is new (D1; zero except IN, KR, BR, MX, RoW). This is exactly `bloc_model.build(p, k=3, uip='closed')` plus the `g_z` term; the prototype is `scripts/world/bloc_ir.py`.

#### 2.2.2 Forcing (what enters the right-hand sides)

```
fIS_t = b5 F_{i,t-3} - theta_i G_t - theta^F_i Fex_t + beta^oil_i oil_{t-3} + beta^q_i qty_t - (b4/12) sum_{l=2..13} Peff_{i,t-l} + eps^y_t + uIS_t
fPC_t = -(l3/4)(Peff_{i,t} - Peff_{i,t-12}) + eps^pi_t
fR_t  = (1-g1) k_us,i ius_t + eps^i_t + pin_t
fS_t  = -( ius_t + eps^rp_t ),     eps^rp_t = u_t + lambda_rp,i G_t
G_t   = rho_G G_{t-1} + (1-rho_G) ius_{t-3},   rho_G = 0.8^(1/3) = 0.9283     (blocs global.financial_channel.monthly)
u_t   = rho_rp u_{t-1} + eta_t,   rho_rp = 0.8^(1/3)                            (blocs FX.risk_premium_shock_AR_quarterly = 0.8 per quarter)
```
`theta_i = P*(theta_scale) x blocs.<id>.monthly_coefficients.theta x world_theta_mult`; `k_us,i`, `lambda_rp,i` from P\* x their mults; `beta^oil_i = blocs.<id>.oil.IS_oil_coefficient_monthly x oil_is_scale_i x world_oil_is_mult` (P\* scales: EA, JP, UK 2.0; CN 2.2; IN 0.4; KR, RoW 0.3; others 1.0; they make the FSGM trough bands W1-24..26); `beta^q` is nonzero only for OPEC+: `world_opec_va_share (1 - b1_m - b2_m)` per 1% of OPEC+ production deviation [assumed]; `oil_t` = the real oil price gap `p_bar` in % (USD, offset removed) with the market's stochastic offset convention of D6; `theta^F_i = theta_i world_theta_f_ratio / world_vix_per_100bp` per VIX point (stage 4; `Fex` in VIX points, so a 13-point non-policy event equals `world_theta_f_ratio` of one unit of `G`); `qty_t` = OPEC+ production deviation `o_t / 0.4626` in % of OPEC+ output (0.4626 = `oil.blocs.OPEC+.liquids_production_2024_mbd` / 103.1); `uIS_t` = active user `BlocDemand`/`GlobalDemand` paths; `pin_t` = active `BlocPolicy` innovations (tables below). `eps^y, eps^pi, eps^i` are i.i.d. normals times `shocks_monthly_sd.{sd_demand, sd_cost_push, sd_policy}` times `world_shock_scale`; `eta` has sd `sd_risk_premium` times `world_shock_scale x world_fx_rp_sd_mult`. In `fR` and `fS`, `ius_t` and `ex[k]` are the bounded values of D33 (identity at the default). **Rev 9 (D48):** with `world_link_dollar` on and `world_rp_sat_scale` > 0, `fS[k] = -( M(ex[k] + lambda_rp,i G_p[k]) + u_t rho_rp^k )` with the raw `ex[k]` (the bound acts on the total, below) and `fR` keeps the bounded `exb`; `G` and `theta gp` in `fIS` stay unbounded.

**Peff** (REER partner term, includes the dollar addends of section 2.6): `Peff_{i,j} = sum_{o != i, o in 12 blocs} w_io (z_{o,j} + 100 a_{o,j}) - 100 a_{i,j}` with `w_io` from `bloc_trade_weights_2024.csv` (the weights on the US and on `i` itself are not in the sum), `a_{o,j}` the log-point exogenous dollar addend of bloc `o` (zero in stage 1 except `delta_t`, the `Dollar` shock: `a = delta`), known up to month `t` (the shock fires in `exog`, before the bloc step) and projected as `a_t d^(j-t)` beyond.

#### 2.2.3 Solution and per-month algorithm

Offline, for each bloc, `solve_blocs.py` builds the stacked system (kernels solved to 480 with T = 720; the first 240 are `IR`, the rest are summed into `tail`) and writes

* `C` (5 x 33): period-0 values `(y, pi, i, z, mrr)` as a linear function of the 33-state `s = (y_{t-1..t-3}, i_{t-1}, z_{t-1..t-13}, pi_{t-1..t-13}, mrr_{t-1..t-3})`;
* `IR[e][k]` (e in IS, PC, rule, V; solver key "S" for V; k = 0..239; 5 outputs): the period-0 response to a unit right-hand-side impulse in equation `e` at period `k`, and `tail[e]` (5 numbers) = the sum of `IR[e][k]` over k = 240..479 (D29);
* `pin[L]` (L in 3, 6, 12, 18, 24): the rule innovations that hold the bloc's rate 1.0 above the rule for `L` months (direct solve of an `L x L` system, `scripts/world/pin_tables.py`);
* the coefficients above.

Each month, for each bloc (serial loop, fixed order):

1. Build four forcing paths `f_e[k]` (k = 0..239) from the channel rules in table 2.2.2b.
2. `x = C s + sum_e sum_k IR[e][k] * f_e[k] + sum_e tail[e] * f_e[K-1]` (5 numbers, about 5k multiply-adds).
3. Shift the state with `x` (lag 1 <- `x`, lag j <- lag j-1) and push `x` to the 14-month history.

Because the solution is linear and the forcing paths are those the agents expect, this equals the perfect-foresight network exactly when the projections are exact (W1-09; reference Python: 3e-8 for a single bloc, 1e-6 for the 12-bloc network with oracle cross-bloc terms; the gate is 1e-4).

**Forcing horizon (D29, review problem 5).** The UIP sum is undiscounted, so the kernels do not decay quickly with `k` (JP, IS equation: max |IR| 1.26 at k = 0, 0.17 at k = 119, 0.037 at k = 239). A persistent addend (the permanent `Dollar` shock, a held oil gap, a long-half-life `BlocDemand`) therefore needs the kernel beyond k = 120: the period-0 inflation response of JP to a permanent unit IS forcing is +5.4 / +2.6 / -1.8 / -6.06 / -5.38 for K = 60 / 84 / 120 / 240 / 480. K = 240 alone still misses by up to 1.39 (JP) against K = 480; the tail term brings the worst error to 0.022. **Test** `forcing_horizon_truncation` (W1-32): for every bloc and each of the four equations, the period-0 `(y, pi, i, z)` response to a forcing path of persistence 0.99 and to a permanent addend, with K = 240 plus tail, differs from the K = 480 solution (no tail) by at most 0.05.

**Table 2.2.2b: forcing-path construction** (`k` = months ahead; "known" values are realised up to the stated month)

| Channel | Known | Projection beyond |
|---|---|---|
| `ius`, `ex[k]` | `ius_t` | ABM expected path `ex[k]` (section 2.1) |
| `G` | `G_t` from `ius_{t-3}` | `Gp[k] = rho_G Gp[k-1] + (1-rho_G) x`, with `x = ius_{t+k-3}` realised for `k <= 2` and `ex[k-3]` otherwise |
| `u` (premium state) | `u_t` | `u_t rho_rp^k` |
| `F_{i,j}` (activity) | `j <= t-1` | `F_{i,t-1} world_rho_f^(j-t+1)` |
| `Peff_{i,j}` | `z_{o,j}` for `j <= t-1`; `a_j` for `j <= t` | `z_{o,t-1} world_rho_p^(j-t+1)`; `a_j = a_t d^(j-t)`, `d` = `shocks.dollar_decay` (1.0 if unset; Dollar shocks scheduled but not yet fired are not anticipated); rev 7: `a_{t+k} = delta_t d^k + dollar_proj[k-1]` with `dollar_proj` of section 2.6 (zero and `proj_on` false with the dollar link off, so `a_{t+k} = a_t d^k` as before) |
| `oil_j` | `j <= t` | `oil_t world_rho_oil^(j-t)` |
| `qty` | `qty_t` | `qty_t world_rho_qty^k` |
| `Fex` | `Fex_t = F_t - Fbar - Fpol_t` (2.7; zero without the dollar link) | `Fex_t rho_fin^k`, k = 0..K-1, entering `fIS[k]` as `- theta^F_i` times it (rev 7: this term does not exist in `bloc.rs` at `ccdcc48`, `fis[k]` at line 306) (`rho_fin` = `fin_factor_rho` 0.83, not the activity persistence `world_rho_f`) |
| i.i.d. shocks | `k = 0` only | zero |
| `pin`, `uIS` | the stored path | the stored path |
| every channel beyond `k = K-1` | | continued at `f_e[K-1]` through `tail[e]` |

`f_IS[k]` uses the delay structure of 2.2.1: its `F` term at `k` is `F_{i, t+k-3}`, the oil term `oil_{t+k-3}`, and the partner sum `sum_{l=2..13} Peff_{i, t+k-l}`; `f_PC[k]` uses `Peff_{i,t+k}` and `Peff_{i,t+k-12}`; `f_R[k] = (1-g1) k_us ex[k] + pin[k]` (+ `eps^i` at `k=0`); `f_S[k] = -(ex[k] + u_t rho_rp^k + lambda_rp Gp[k])`, rev 9 with the dollar link: `f_S[k] = -(M(ex[k] + lambda_rp Gp[k]) + u_t rho_rp^k)`, `M` the bound of D48 (the premium innovation enters `u_t` before the step).

#### 2.2.4 Pre-registered calibration P\* (table; `scripts/world/pstar.json`, the lead modeller's `pstar_blocs.json`)

| Bloc group | `theta_scale` | `lambda_rp` | `k_us` | `g_z` | `uip_w` | `oil_is_scale` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EA, UK, CA, AU | 0.8 | 1.5 | 0.3 | 0 | research | EA, UK 2.0; CA, AU 1.0 |
| JP | 0.8 | 1.5 | 0.1 | 0 | research | 2.0 |
| IN, KR, BR, MX | 0.8 | 2.0 | 0.4 | 0.25 | research (IN 0.7) | IN 0.4, KR 0.3, BR, MX 1.0 |
| RoW | 0.8 | 2.0 | 0.33 | 0.25 | research | 0.3 |
| OPEC+ | 0.8 | 2.0 | 0.325 | 0 | 0.6 (research) | 1.0 |
| CN | 1.0 | 0.3 | 0.2 | 0 | **0.85** | 2.2 |

(Research defaults are in `blocs.<id>.IS_curve`, `FX`, `policy_rule`.) These reproduce W1-10..W1-27 in the reference simulation; the selection rule in stage 1 allows one grid step per parameter group.

#### 2.2.5 Outputs, price levels, nominal rates, headline

```
ln P_{j,t}   = ln P_{j,t-1} + (pi*_j + pi_{j,t})/1200            (pi*_j = blocs.<id>.steady_state.inflation_target)
level policy = Rbar_j + pi*_j + i_{j,t}; reported policy rate = max(level, steady_state.policy_rate_floor)
lnS^bloc_{j,t} = z_{j,t}/100 + (ln P_{j,t} - ln P_{j,0}) - (ln PT - ln PT_0)               (D11, D26)
headline_{j,t} = pi_{j,t} + 12 omega^E_j phi_j (0.6 dc_{j,t} + 0.4 dc_{j,t-1}),   dc = monthly % change of p_bar - offset
                 (omega^E = blocs.<id>.oil.energy_cpi_weight; phi = oil blocs.<name>.crude_share_of_fuel_price_phi; reporting only)
GDP gap aggregates: Y^W = sum_i ppp_i y_i (ppp from blocs weights share_world_pct_ppp_2024; US weight 14.5% uses y_US)
```

#### 2.2.6 User shocks on blocs

* `BlocPolicy{bloc, bps, months}`: `pin_t = (bps/100) pin[bloc][months][t - t0]` for `t - t0 < months`, known to all agents at `t0` (forcing in the rule equation). Golden: EA, 12 months, 100bp: `y` trough -0.56 at month 15, `pi` -0.42, `z` -0.93 on impact (W1-01).
* `BlocDemand{bloc, pct, half_life}`: unit path `uIS_k = 0.5^(k/half_life)` in the IS equation; at activation run the isolated bloc (zero US inputs, `max(36, ceil(4 half_life))` months, at most 240, the tail term included; section 1.9) and scale so that `min/max y` equals `pct` (deterministic). With `risk_off` (default **false** for every bloc; the China-slowdown preset sets it true): at activation also add `world_china_risk_pts_per_pct x |pct|` VIX points to `f_t` (stage 4).
* `Dollar{pct, half_life}` (existing): `delta_t = shocks.dollar_log()` is the addend `a` common to all blocs (stage 1), seen through `Peff`; in the ABM it acts through `fx.rs` as before (dollar link off) or as an additive level in section 2.6 (on).

### 2.3 Network (`net.rs`)

```
F_{i,j} = sum_{o != i, o in 12} s_io y_{o,j} + s_{i,US} g^{US->i}_j ,    s_io = blocs/data/bloc_spillover_coeffs_2024.csv (import elasticity eps_M = 1)
g^{US->i}_j = y_US(j)                                     stage 1, and whenever world_us_import_channel = false or link_trade off
            = y_US(j) + 100 * ewma_{0.25}( ln(Q_{i,j}/Y_j) - bench_{i,j} ) clamped to +/-10      coupled mode (stage 2)
              Q_{i,j} = real US imports from bloc i = customs value / import price of origin i; Y_j = real GDP; months 0..11 use y_US only.
              bench (S2-R5, F3) = the mean of the first 12 observations of ln(Q/Y) of the bloc, then an EWMA with a 60-month half-life
              (`BENCH_HALF_LIFE`, weight 1 - 2^(-1/60)); the base is no longer one month (q0, y0 removed).
```
Cross-bloc terms use month `t-1` values (section 1.3). `w_io` and `s_io` are fixed 2024 matrices.

### 2.4 Oil market (`oil.rs`; port of `scripts/world/oil_reference.py`)

The prototype is the oracle: `OilState::step(&OilParams, &OilShocks, ExternalInputs) -> OilOut` must reproduce `step()` line by line, including the order of updates, `clip` of `tau` to `[tau_min, spare_cap]`, `o = min(o + theta_O (tau - o), spare_cap)`, the three-month `hist`, and the exact-path branch. Units: flows in % of world consumption `Q = 103.1 mb/d` (`_meta.Q_mbd`); `p` = end-of-month log price deviation (%) of real Brent from `anchors.Brent_2024_usd` ($80.53); `p_bar = w p_t + (1-w) p_{t-1}`, `w = time_aggregation.w` = 0.789.

```
shocks:  uI = rho_I uI + e_I ; q = rho_q q + theta_q e_I ; sD = rho_D sD + dD ; n = rho_n n + nu ; cut_pend += news_cut_share nu ; step_cut = (1 - news_phase) cut_pend ; cut_pend -= step_cut
OPEC+:   p~ = mean(p_bar_{t-1..t-3});  dtau = -kappa_L max(0, pi_L - p~) + kappa_H max(0, p~ - pi_H) - stock_kappa i_{t-1} + e_O - step_cut
         tau = clip(tau (1 - tau_decay 1[p~ >= pi_L]) + dtau, tau_min, spare_cap);  o = min(o + theta_O (tau - o), spare_cap)
US, rest: u1 = rho_U1 u1 + (1-rho_U1) eps_US_LR pbar_{t-1};  u2 = rho_U2 u2 + (1-rho_U2) u1;  r = rho_R r + (1-rho_R) eps_R_LR pbar_{t-1}
S_pre  = o + sh_us u2 + sh_rest r - sD
demand:  d_i = beta_i ylag_i + q - eta0_i pc_i - z_i ;  z_i = rho_z z_i + (1-rho_z) eta1_i pc_i ;  pc_i = p_bar + rfx_i
         D = sum_i omega_i d_i  (omega_i = oil blocs.<name>.consumption_share)
         eta0_i = eta0 phi_i/phibar, eta1_i = eta1 phi_i/phibar, phibar = sum_i omega_i phi_i = 0.3523   (D12)
         ylag_i = rho_ylag ylag_i + (1-rho_ylag) y_{i,t-1}   (US: y_US; blocs: y_i)
         rfx_i  = z_{i,t-1} (bloc real depreciation, % ; US 0)
clearing: S_t - D_t = dI_t ; dI = -psi (1-rho_E) p_t - [xi max(i,0) + xi_d min(i,0)] + psi lambda_n n_t + uI ; i_t = i_{t-1} + dI
closed form: k = eps_S + eta0 + (1-rho_z) eta1 ; a = S_pre - Sum omega beta ylag - q + rho_z Sum omega z_{t-1} + Rx ;
             Rx = Sum omega_i (eta0_i + (1-rho_z) eta1_i) rfx_i  (the prototype's fixed_demand = -Rx)
             p_t = [uI + psi lambda_n n - fb - a - k (1-w) p_{t-1}] / (k w + psi (1-rho_E))
```
Parameters (symbol = `oil/params.json` path): `w` = `time_aggregation.w`; `eta0, eta1, rho_z, rho_ylag` = `demand_aggregate.*`; `beta_i` = `blocs.<n>.cyclical_income_elasticity` (per bloc, consumption-weighted mean 0.85 = `demand_aggregate.beta_y`; the bloc price-elasticity columns are not used, D12); `eps_S` = `supply.eps_S`; `spare_cap, theta_O, pi_L, pi_H, kappa_L, kappa_H, tau_min (= tau_floor in the prototype), tau_decay` = `supply.opec.{spare_capacity, theta_O, price_floor, price_ceiling, kappa_L, kappa_H, tau_min, tau_decay}`; `stock_kappa` = 0 (prototype default, no params leaf; keep the term so the port is exact); `sh_us, eps_US_LR, rho_U1 = rho_U2` = `supply.us_tight_oil.{share_of_Q, eps_LR, rho_stage}`; `sh_rest, eps_R_LR, rho_R` = `supply.rest_of_world_conventional.{share_of_Q, eps_LR, rho}`; `psi, rho_E, xi, xi_d, lambda_n` = `inventories.{psi, rho_E, xi_builds, xi_draws, lambda_news}`; `sig_I, rho_I, theta_q, rho_q, nu_I, a, b, lam_I, mu_I, sd_I` = `shocks.oil_specific.{sigma, rho_I, theta_q, rho_q, student_nu, garch.alpha, garch.beta, jumps.intensity, jumps.mean_sd_units, jumps.sd_sd_units}`; `jump_lam_D, jump_mean_D, rho_D` = `shocks.supply_disruption.{intensity, mean_size, rho_D}`; `sig_O, nu_O` = `shocks.opec_policy_surprise.{sigma, student_nu}`; `news_cut_share, news_phase, rho_n` = `shocks.news.{cut_share, phase, rho_n}`. Profile selected by `world_oil_profile` from `oil.alternatives.<name>.changes`. With `rfx = 0` and one aggregate bloc this is the prototype exactly (test `oil_matches_python_prototype`, golden `oil_golden.json`).

**Shock distributions** (`shocks.oil_specific`, from the prototype `simulate`): `e_I = sig_I * mult * [sqrt(max(1 - var_j, 0.05)) sqrt(h) z_t + jump - lam_I mu_I]`, `z_t` Student-t (nu = 6, unit variance, winsorised at +/-8 as `exog::oil_month`), GARCH `h = (1-a-b) + a base_{t-1}^2 + b h_{t-1}` clamped to [0.04, 25], jumps Poisson(`lam_I`) of N(`mu_I`, `sd_I`) sd-units, `var_j = lam_I (mu_I^2 + sd_I^2)`. Supply disruptions: Poisson(`jump_lam_D`) of exponential(`jump_mean_D`), minus the mean (compensated). OPEC+ surprise: `sig_O` x unit-variance t(5). Activity is not drawn in the integrated run: `y_i` come from the blocs (D5). In `oil-check` (the isolated market) the prototype activity `y = rho_y y + sigma_y N(0,1)` (`shocks.activity`, keys `world_oilcheck_activity_*`) is drawn on `WORLD_OIL_S` and enters as the one aggregate gap with `beta_y`; W3-02, W3-05, W3-06, W3-09 and the test `oil_matches_python_prototype` run on it. All draws on `WORLD_OIL_I`/`WORLD_OIL_S`, taken every month.

**Exact path (legacy `OilPrice{pct, hl}`)** with `world_oil_exact_path`: while `|oil_log_shock| > 1e-4` run a second copy U of the market with the same innovations; each month choose copy U's `uI` so that `p_bar^U = p_bar^A + 100 oil_log_shock_t` (the prototype's `target_pbar`). The ABM price uses `p_bar^U`; blocs and trade see `p_bar^U` too; copy A continues undisturbed; U is dropped once `|oil_log_shock| < 1e-4` (about 12 years at a 12-month half-life, i.e. in practice not inside a run; the cost is one more market step per month). Copy A reads the bloc gaps of the arm it runs in, so across the two arms of `compare()` A differs by the feedback of the shock on activity: 2% of the shock at 3 months, 16-18% at 12 months and 46% at 24 months for OilPrice +50% (rev 6, S3-R2; the earlier 'about 2%' held only at 3 months). The exact-path property holds against *that arm's* structural price. `world_oil_feedback` false removes the feedback and gives the exact path (pair difference equal to the shock, test `exact_path_pair_difference_is_the_shock_without_feedback`); W3-10 is gated that way.

**Open item for stage 5 (S3-R2).** With the link on and feedback on, an `OilPrice` preset delivers 16-18% less oil price at 12 months than the user asked for. Stage 5 decides whether exact-path mode should freeze copy A's feedback (`world_oil_feedback` false inside the preset) or keep the structural response, and it re-runs W3-10 under the decision.

**Interface to the ABM (rev 5, ruling S3-R1).** The ABM log deviation of the oil price is the decaying splice
`A_t = p_used,t - offset_t + G_t`, `G_t = kappa rho^(t+1)` for reported months `t >= 0` (`t = 0` is the first reported month),
`kappa = L_b - (p_b - offset_t)`, where `L_b = 100 (oil_ar + oil_shock)` at the last burn-in month (the legacy level, read in
`settle` of that month) and `p_b` is the market's `p_bar` there (copy A, shadow). `rho` is the legacy persistence `oil_ar` (0.985)
unless `world_oil_splice_rho` is set (`-1` means follow `oil_ar`; `1` is the permanent anchor, `0` a month-0 jump). Then
`exog.oil_price = oil_base * oil_index(w) * exp(A_t/100)` (with `p_used` the copy-U price when a live OilPrice shock is on the
exact path), `exog.oil_ar := (p_A,t - offset_t + G_t)/100` (copy A), and the blocs receive `oil_t = A_t` (the price the economy
faces). `oil_shock` is display only on the exact path; without the exact path (`world_oil_exact_path` false) the shock adds
`100 oil_shock` to `A_t`. In a matched shocked and unshocked pair (same seed, same burn-in) `kappa` and `G_t` are identical, so
`A_shock - A_base = p_shock - p_base` to machine precision (test `splice_kappa_cancels_in_matched_pair`). The splice is a deviation
from the literal rev 4 interface (`oil_ar := (p_bar_A - offset_t)/100` with no splice): the literal rule either carries a
seed-specific base level for ever (`rho = 1`: a seed-level dispersion of about 50-60 log points, it fails W3-14 by construction)
or forces a precautionary innovation of about 22 % of Q in one month (`rho = 0` with an exact month-0 match). Metrics:
`brent_usd = 80.53 exp(p_used/100)` with `p_used` the market's price (offset not subtracted).

**OPEC+ quantity channel (rev 6, S3-R8 option b).** The blocs' quantity input is `Q_t = q_t - world_oil_qty_offset + G^q_t`, with `q_t = o_used/opec_q` (the market's OPEC+ deviation, % of OPEC+ liquids) and `G^q_t = kappa_q rho^(t+1)`, `kappa_q = 0 - (q_b - world_oil_qty_offset)`: the legacy quantity input is zero in burn-in, and `q_b` is the market's base-month value. The decay and `rho` are those of the price splice. The offset is the stochastic mean, measured on the shipped profile with the oil link on, 1000 households, months 0-239: -6.627 (SE 0.601) on seeds 1-200; the check on seeds 201-400 gives -7.631 (SE 0.610). It applies only when `stochastic_oil` is on (the D6 rule), so deterministic runs are unchanged. In a matched pair `kappa_q` cancels (test `splice_qty_kappa_cancels_in_matched_pair`, to 1e-10); the month-0 input is continuous with the base up to the market's one-month move and the decay of `kappa_q` (test `splice_qty_month_zero_is_continuous`).

### 2.5 Trade and prices (`trade.rs`)

**Notation.** `omega0_{j,s}` = bloc `j`'s share of US imports of sector `s` (column shares of `sector_shares.matrices.imports_base_C26_in_Tech.usd_bn_2024`); `x0_{s,j}` = US exports of sector `s` to `j` over 2024 GDP ($29,381bn) (`...exports_base_C26_in_Tech...`); `theta_j` = `invoicing.theta_usd_share_of_bloc_exports_to_US`; `z~_j` (log points x 100; plus = the dollar is stronger against `j`'s currency in real terms) is, with the dollar link **off**, `z~_j = c_t + (z_j - sum_k wH10_k z_k)` where the common component `c_t = 100[ln(fx_t/fx_0) + ln(PT_t/PT_0) - ln(p_world,t/p_world,0)]` is the ABM's *own* real dollar relative to its base (`fx_t = w.row.fx`, `p_world = common::world_price(w)`; all bases are those captured at the end of the last burn-in month, section 1.6, D36) and the bloc cross-section has zero mean by construction; with the dollar link **on**, `z~_j = z_j + 100 a_{j,t}` with `a_{j,t} = delta_t + q_t + g_{j,t}` (section 2.6; `zeta` excluded) and `zrel_j = z~_j - z_{j,b}`, where `z_{j,b}` is the bloc's own `z` at the base (`TradeBase.z_level`; `a` is zero at the base); **rev 9 (D47) with `world_dollar_neutral`: `zrel_j = z_j - z_fc,j + 100 a_{j,t}`**, the bloc's forecast error plus the addend, so the ABM's real dollar has no deterministic drift from the base-month misalignment of the blocs (`TradeState::post_bloc_ref`; `post_bloc` is the `None` case). The trade base therefore holds two arrays, `z_base` (cross-section, link off) and `z_level` (level, link on), both captured at the end of the last burn-in month (rev 7). The dynamic terms below (`Phi`, `uf`, the export FX term) read `zrel_j = z~_j - z~_{j,0}`, the change since the base month (`z_base`; `c_t` is zero there), so they start at the base-month levels (S2-R1); `z~_j` itself is reported and gated by W2-15. The earlier definition through the bloc price levels `ln P_j` made `z~` drift by about -1 to -1.5% a year (bloc inflation targets 2-4% against 0.9-1.8% US tradables inflation: about -15% by reported month 0 and -40% by month 240, review problem 12); it is not used. With every bloc gap at zero the trade block therefore sees exactly the ABM's real dollar, so the integrated trade shares follow master's drift (gate W2-15).

**Import prices by origin (dominant currency).**
```
Phi^d_{j,t}  = Phi^d_{j,t-1}  + 0.08 (0.47 zrel_{j,t} - Phi^d_{j,t-1})        (world_dcp_speed_usd, world_dcp_kappa_usd)
Phi^nd_{j,t} = Phi^nd_{j,t-1} + 0.90 (0.95 zrel_{j,t} - Phi^nd_{j,t-1})
r_{j,t}      = -[ theta_j Phi^d_{j,t} + (1-theta_j) Phi^nd_{j,t} ] / 100      (log price of origin j relative to PT; fall when z~ rises)
```
Import-weighted `theta` (non-energy origin shares of `imports_base_C26_in_Tech`, energy has `r = 0`) = **0.893** [derived, `scripts/world/trade_hand.py`]; the month index `h` counts the impact month, so the value at month `h` is the state after `h+1` updates. The implied pass-through of a permanent 1% dollar move is **0.220 / 0.379 / 0.469 / 0.502** at months 3 / 12 / 24 / 36 (research path 0.15 / 0.30 / 0.40 / 0.45, ranges [0.08, 0.35], [0.20, 0.50], [0.30, 0.55], [0.35, 0.55]; W2-04). For `Dollar +10%` (`z~` = 100 ln 1.10 = 9.531) the non-fuel import price index falls by 2.08 / 3.55 / 4.37 / 4.67% at months 3 / 12 / 24 / 36 (W2-03). Energy is priced by the oil factor of `common::world_sector_factor`; set `r_{j,Energy} = 0`.

**Origin shares and import price index.** Duty-inclusive log price `q_{j,s} = r_j + ln(1 + tau_{j,s})`; `x_{j,s} = q_{j,s} - sum_k omega0_{k,s} q_{k,s}`; lagged state `zw_{j,s,t} = zw_{j,s,t-1} + 0.05 (x_{j,s} - zw_{j,s,t-1})`;
```
omega_{j,s,t} = omega0_{j,s} exp(-[sigma_SR x_{j,s} + (sigma_LR - sigma_SR) zw_{j,s}]) / sum_k omega0_{k,s} exp(-[...]_k)         (sigma_SR 0.5, sigma_LR 2.0)
rbar_{s,t}    = sum_j omega_{j,s,t} r_{j,t}
imp_rel_s     = imp_level (PT_{t-1}/PT_0) exp(rbar_{s,t}) exp(-g n/12)  -> world.out.imp_rel[s]   (imp_level: the base-month legacy import level, D36, S2-R1; replaces import_pass_through; Energy keeps world_f in goods.rs; g = world_import_rel_drift, n = reported months since the base, rev 12 D62; g = 0 is the stage-5 form)
imp_rel_cpi   = role_mean(cpi_tradable_weight, import_share[s] imp_rel_s) / role_mean(cpi_tradable_weight, import_share[s])
```
