**Baseline drift of the import price (rev 12, D62).** The legacy price `import_pass_through` (the world price over the dollar, partly passed through) falls against PT at about 0.38 % a year in the ABM's baseline; D26's numeraire is flat against PT. The factor `exp(-g n / 12)` restores the legacy slope: with g = 0.0032 the slope of ln(import_price_index / PT) is -0.32 %/yr in the cell against -0.38 in W' (64 exploratory seeds), the household import share drifts +0.13 pp a year against +0.155, and imports / GDP is -0.04 pp (SE 0.05) below W' instead of -0.37. The factor is exactly 1.0 in the first reported month. It multiplies the CPI import component with the prices, so the CPI path also follows master's. The centred import-demand term of 2.3 sees the faster rise of Q/Y: its mean rises by 0.35-0.86 pp per bloc, which is why the D57 constants are re-measured (D64).
Elasticity profile of origin substitution at 12 / 60 months: 1.19 / 1.93 (Boehm-Levchenko-Pandalai-Nayar bilateral profile: 0.76 at 18 months, about 2 long run; Fajgelbaum et al. 2.53 across origins).

**Tariffs (D34).** All tariffs are decimals (0.25 = 25%). `tau_{j,s,t} = w.shocks.tariff + sum of the active Tariff components matching (partner j, sector s)`, capped at 5.0: the first term is the legacy set-level `ImportTariff` level (all origins and sectors, replaced not added: `ImportTariff 10` then `ImportTariff 0` leaves 0), the bilateral `Tariff` components add on top. Passed to b7 as two scalars, one per channel:
```
tau_eff^c_t = sum_{s in cov_c} m_{s,t-1} sum_j omega_{j,s,t} tau_{j,s,t} / sum_{s in cov_c} m_{s,t-1},   c in {hh, firm}
cov_hh = {s : cfg.cpi_tradable_weight[s] != 0} (household duty, retail wedge, CPI wedge; excludes Food), cov_firm = {s : cfg.tradable_weight[s] != 0} (firm inputs and equipment; includes Food)
m_s = flows.imports_by_sector[s] of month t-1 (base shares at t=0)
```
`world.out.tau_eff_hh` is what `goods::clear` charges households and what `tariff_rate_pct` reports; `world.out.tau_eff_firm` is the `tariff` argument of `firm_stage` and what `firms.rs` uses for `goods_input_mult` (hook H8), so b7's border duty, retail wedge, tariff-elastic shares, importer absorption and CPI wedge run unchanged (a uniform tariff gives `tau_eff_hh = tau_eff_firm = w.shocks.tariff`: identical to b7; rev 12, D63: a component with no partner and no sector is folded into the set-level term before the duties by origin and sector are formed, so `Tariff{null, null, p}` and `ImportTariff{p}` are bit-identical). Sector heterogeneity of the retail wedge is lost (first order; open item). Memo: `tau_by_origin_j = sum_s omega_{j,s} m_s tau_{j,s} / sum_s omega_{j,s} m_s`.

**Exports.** For sector `s`, destination bloc `j` (nominal, model units per month):
```
X_{s,j,t} = kappa_s x0_{s,j} * fiscal::trend_gdp_month(w) * exp( eps_y yhat_j + lnA^fx_j + lnA^rp_{s,j} ) * R_{s,j,t}
yhat_j   = mean(y_{j,t-1}, y_{j,t-2}, y_{j,t-3}) / 100 ,  eps_y = elasticities.us_exports.foreign_income_elasticity = 1.4
lnA^fx_j = -[ 0.15 zrel_j/100 + (0.30 - 0.15) uf_j ] ,   uf_j = uf_j + 0.04 (zrel_j/100 - uf_j)                 (DCP: importer depreciation vs USD)
x^rp_{s,j} = rbar^comp_j - [ ln(sector_price_s/PT_{t-1}) - base_s ] ;  base_s = that log ratio at the base month (D36; zero there) ; Energy: x^rp = ln(world_f / world_f0) - dev (world_f0 at the base) ;  rbar^comp_j = sum_{k != US, j} m_jk r_k / sum m_jk  (m_jk = bloc_goods_matrix.import_shares_2024[k][j]: the matrix is stored [exporter][importer] with importer columns summing to 1, so m_jk is importer j's share of exporter k; Energy: ln world_f instead)
lnA^rp   = 0.10 x^rp + (0.90 - 0.10) up_{s,j},   up_{s,j} = up + 0.04 (x^rp - up)                         (world_export_price_*)
kappa_s = the ABM's legacy exports of the base month / sum_j x0_{s,j} trend exp(eps_y yhat_j) at zero states (S2-R1, F1; one when no month has cleared); exports[s] = sum_j X_{s,j}  -> pool_sales(RoW, ...) as before; realised sector exports are allocated to j pro rata to X_{s,j}
```
Hand calculation for `Dollar +10%` (`scripts/world/trade_hand.py`, price and exchange-rate channels, no income effect, no sector-price response): exports **-3.2 / -4.5 / -5.4%** at months 12 / 24 / 36 (W2-02 bands -2.9 +/-1.5, -5.7 +/-2.5, -7.1 +/-2.5). Real imports (the ABM's own share-factor mechanism, before demand effects): master pair (0.2, 0.6) +1.6 / +2.3 / +2.7%; (0.4, 1.0) +2.9 / +4.0 / +4.6; (0.7, 1.3) +4.0 / +5.4 / +6.1 (W2-01, D13). These are the only hand values in this spec; the unit tests read the full-precision paths (h = 0..60) of `trade_hand.json` (placement table, section 1.1). They are exact for a *step* in `z~`; with the dollar link off `z~` follows the ABM's `fx`, which adjusts at `fx_adjust` 0.3 a month, so integrated paths lag the hand values at the shortest horizons (the +/-2-month windows at months 12-36 are not affected).

**Retaliation (stage 4-3; D9, D51).** Active when `world_retaliation` is true (it needs `world_link_trade`). The state is per bloc `j` and sector `s` (sectors in model order; all zero at creation and through the burn-in, section 1.6b): the history `hist_j[0..13]` of the average US duty on the bloc (index 0 is this month), `tau*_{j,s}`, `zr_{j,s}` and the factor `R_{j,s}` (1.0). It steps once per reported month in `TradeState::post_bloc`, after the duty components of the month are in `tariffs` (a `Tariff` that fires this month counts this month, `add_user` runs first) and before the export loop:
```
add_{j,s}  = min(TAU_CAP, sum of tau_c over the active components c with c.retaliate, (c.partner is None or j), (c.sector is None or s))      (decimal, 5.0 cap)
taubar_j   = sum_s B_{j,s} add_{j,s} / sum_s B_{j,s},   B_{j,s} = trade.imports_base[j][s]  (2024 import values in $bn, all nine sectors: fixed weights)
hist_j     = [taubar_j, hist_j[0], ..., hist_j[11]]                                   (13 entries, shifted every reported month)
tgt_{j,s}  = min(cap_j, kappa_j w_s hist_j[lag_j])                                    (the average duty of lag_j months ago)
tau*_{j,s} = tau*_{j,s} + mu (tgt_{j,s} - tau*_{j,s}),   mu = RET_MU_UP = 1.0 if tgt >= tau*_{j,s} else RET_MU_DOWN = 0.1
x_{j,s}    = ln(1 + tau*_{j,s});   zr_{j,s} = zr_{j,s} + world_ret_speed (x_{j,s} - zr_{j,s})
R_{j,s}    = exp(-[world_ret_eps_sr x_{j,s} + (world_ret_eps_lr - world_ret_eps_sr) zr_{j,s}])       X_{s,j} <- X_{s,j} R_{j,s}   (the export formula above, before the finiteness guard)
```
The factor multiplies the bilateral exports only when `world_retaliation` is true (the multiplication is skipped when it is false). With no flagged component `add`, `taubar`, `tgt`, `tau*`, `x` and `zr` are exactly 0.0 and `R` is exactly 1.0, so the switch on and the switch off give bit-identical output (identity I7). Tables (builder `build_retaliation`, trade `retaliation.recommended_rule`, copied by path; bloc order EA, CN, JP, UK, CA, MX, IN, KR, BR, AU, OPEC+, RoW; the research names the euro area `EU`): `kappa` 0.2, 0.95, 0, 0, 0.5, 0, 0.1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0.05; `lag` (months) 2, 0, 1, 1, 0, 1, 12, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1 (the research gives EA 2, CN 0, CA 0, MX 1, IN 12; the other blocs get 1 month [assumed], and only RoW has `kappa` > 0 among them); `cap` 0.5 for every bloc but CN (1.25); `w_s` Energy 1.0, Food 1.5, Durables 1.2, Nondurables 1.0, Tech 0.5 and 0 for Leisure, HealthEdu, OtherServices and Construction. Only `Tariff` components flagged `retaliate` count: the set-level `ImportTariff` does not retaliate, and an all-origin tariff with retaliation is `Tariff{null, null, pct, true}` (the same duty as `ImportTariff` for the b7 channels). Components are permanent and non-negative (D34), so `taubar` never falls: the ratchet-down branch and the lag as a delay-then-hold are reachable only through the state function, which the tests call directly, until a tariff end date exists (open item 32).
**The lag is a delay (D51).** The research writes `max_{k=0..lag_j}`; a window maximum that includes the current month passes a rise at once whatever the lag, so every lag would be invisible to a permanent duty, while the evidence for the lags (EU two months, India twelve) is a delayed response. The block reads the average duty `lag_j` months ago; for CN and CA (lag 0) the two readings are identical, so no gated row depends on the choice [assumed].
**The retaliation table, UK row (rev 13; owner choice, 2026-10-15).** The rule has no per-partner table in this section; its values are read from `data/world/research/trade/params.json` (`retaliation.recommended_rule`). The UK: `kappa` 0.2 (range 0.0 to 0.6), lag 2 months, cap 0.5, as the euro area. A modelling choice and a proxy, not a measured 2025 response (2018: inside the EU rebalancing, 25% on about $3.5 bn of US imports, Bown PIIE 2018; 2025: consulted on a list, agreed the deal, no duties). Before rev 13 the UK had `kappa` 0.0 and no lag entry.

**Hand values** (`hand_ret`; exports at the 2024 mix of `exports_base`; "goods" = Energy, Food, Durables, Nondurables, Tech). The exponent on `x` after `n` updates is `eps_sr + (eps_lr - eps_sr)(1 - (1 - speed)^n)`: 1.2000 (n = 1), 1.5420 (3), 1.9371 (6), 2.4351 (12), 2.8405 (24), 2.9549 (36), 3 in the limit; the update count of month `h` after a shock at `T0` is `h` (the shock month is update 1) for lag 0 and `h - lag_j` for the others. `Tariff{CN, null, 15, true}`: `taubar_CN` 0.15, `tau*_{CN,s}` 0.14250 / 0.21375 / 0.17100 / 0.14250 / 0.07125 and 0 for the services; `ret_tau_CN` (the mean of `tau*` over the goods sectors weighted by `x0_{s,CN}`, the metric) 0.15121; goods exports to CN -15.38 % (h = 1), **-28.58 % (h = 12)**, -32.41 % (h = 24); all sectors -21.37 % at the 2024 mix (-23.4 % at the model's 82 % goods share of its exports to CN); the W4-24 statistic `ln(goods exports shocked/baseline) / ln(1 + ret_tau)` is -1.186 at h = 1 and **-2.390 at h = 12**. `Tariff{CN, null, 25, true}`: `ret_tau` 0.25202, goods -41.27 %, statistic -2.368. CA 25 points: `ret_tau` 0.13898, goods -26.95 %; EA 25 points (lag 2): `ret_tau` 0.04895, goods -10.36 % at h = 12 (ten updates), nothing before the third month; IN 25 points (lag 12): `ret_tau` 0.02460 from the thirteenth month; RoW 25 points: `ret_tau` 0.01276. Probe cells (10,000 households, 16 probe seeds, shocked minus baseline inside `compare`, ABM feedback included): CN 15 goods -28.96 % (SE 0.03), statistic -2.428 (SE 0.003); CN 25 goods -41.75 %; CA 25 goods -27.34 %; EA 25 goods -10.64 % (statistic -2.355).

### 2.6 Dollar (`dollar.rs`, stage 4a; before stage 4 only the cross-section of 2.5)

**Rev 7.** Re-derived against `fx.rs` and `monetary.rs` at `ccdcc48` (PREFLIGHT sections 2-3). The premium step below is the code of `exchange_rate` (`fx.rs:74-120`); only its guards and its use differ. All quantities are log points (natural log) unless marked; `z` is in %.

**Two phases a month.** `dollar_active(w)` is `w.cfg.world_link_dollar && w.world.is_some() && reported(w)` (`#[inline(never)]`, hook H2: `monetary::exchange_rate` is skipped when it holds, in `step` and in the `fx` closure of `step_timed`). In `WorldBlock::step_month` the dollar module runs in two phases around the bloc step:

* **Phase A (premium and addend)**, after `net::us_inputs` and before `core`: computes `q_t`, `cred_t`, `g_{j,t}`, `zeta_{j,t}`, the common addend `a_t` and its projection, and fills the dollar fields of `UsInputs` (below).
* **Phase B (index)**, after `core` and before `trade.post_bloc`: computes the bilateral rates and the broad index, writes `w.row.fx`, and hands the bilateral addends `a_{j,t}` to the trade module.

```
delta_t   = w.shocks.dollar_log()                      the Dollar shock level (decay d_t = UsInputs.dollar_decay, 1.0 if permanent)
q_t       = qpsi_t + cred_t                            qpsi: the premium step below; cred: UsCredibility, D31
cred_t    = sum over active UsCredibility shocks of ln(1 - dollar_pct/100) p(age),  age = t - t0 (0 in the shock month),
            p(age) = 1 for age < months, 0.5^((age - months + 1)/3) for age >= months
g_{j,t}   = world_fx_risk_mult (gamma_j/100)(Frisk_t - Frisk_b)      gamma_j = finance dollar.risk_off_loading_gamma_pct_per_vix_pt.by_bloc.<j> (JP -0.006, OPEC+ 0)
Frisk_t   = world_fx_risk_rho Frisk_{t-1} + eps^F_t ,  eps^F_t = e_t if e_t > 0 else world_fx_risk_asym e_t ,  e_t = F_t - fin_factor_rho F_{t-1}
zeta_{j,t}= world_fx_idio_rho zeta_{j,t-1} + sd_j eps_{j,t}          eps_{j,t} ~ N(0,1) from agent_rng(w.seed, w.tick, WORLD_FX, j); sd_j = world_fx_idio_sd_{ae|eme|cn|opec}; zeta_{j,-1} = 0 at the base
gbar_t    = sum_j wH10_j g_{j,t}                       the broad risk addend (sum_j wH10_j gamma_j = 0.18838 %/pt, equal to the research gamma_broad 0.1884)
a_t       = delta_t + q_t + gbar_t                     the addend the blocs see (UsInputs.dollar); a_{j,t} = delta_t + q_t + g_{j,t} is the bilateral one (trade, ln S_j)
lnS_{j,t} = (z_{j,t} - z_{j,b})/100 + (lnP_{j,t} - lnP_{j,b}) - (ln PT_{t-1} - ln PT_b) + a_{j,t} + zeta_{j,t}              (rev 7; rev 9 with world_dollar_neutral: z_{j,b} -> z_fc,j(m), below)
ln D_t    = sum_j wH10_j lnS_{j,t} ,   w.row.fx = fx_0 exp(ln D_t)         wH10 = trade.tab.dollar_w (finance dollar.h10_broad_weights_pct_2026, normalised)
```
The base values `z_{j,b}` (= `blocs[j].hist.z[0]`), `lnP_{j,b}` (= `blocs[j].ln_p`), `ln PT_b` (= `ln trade::pt_level(w)`), `fx_0` (= `w.row.fx`) and `Frisk_b` are captured at the end of the last burn-in month by `WorldBlock::capture_bases` (called from `WorldBlock::settle` where `tr.capture` is called today, and as the first-step fallback in `step_month` for a world without burn-in); they are **not** the stage-1 `Bases`, which are captured at the first reported step (section 1.6b). `ln PT_{t-1}` in phase B is `ln trade::pt_level(w)` evaluated in `step_month` before the month clears, i.e. the cleared price of month `t-1`; at reported month 0 it equals `ln PT_b`. `Frisk` is updated in `world::after_exog` every month while `world_link_dollar` is true (burn-in included), after the month's injection (2.7); with the default ABM keys `f_t` is identically zero and so are `Frisk`, `g` and `Fex`. Because `sum_j wH10_j = 1`, `ln D_t = D^bloc_t + delta_t + q_t + gbar_t + zbar_t` where `D^bloc_t = sum_j wH10_j [(z - z_b)/100 + (lnP - lnP_b)] - (ln PT_{t-1} - ln PT_b)` and `zbar_t = sum_j wH10_j zeta_{j,t}`.

**The conditional forecast (rev 9, D47).** With `world_dollar_neutral` (default) every `z_{j,b}` above is replaced by `z_fc,j(m)`, `m = n - n_0` the reported month (`n` the bloc clock, `n_0` the clock of the first reported month), so
```
lnS_{j,t} = (z_{j,t} - z_fc,j(m))/100 + (lnP_{j,t} - lnP_{j,b}) - (ln PT_{t-1} - ln PT_b) + a_{j,t} + zeta_{j,t}
```
and likewise `zrel_j` in 2.5. `z_fc` is made once, at the base capture (`WorldBlock::forecast(w, us_b, skip)`, called in the base-month branch of `settle` with the inputs of the month that just ran, `skip` = 1, and in `capture_bases` for a world without burn-in with the first month's inputs, `skip` = 0). A light copy of the block (parameters, the twelve bloc states, the US history; no trade, oil, dollar or finance state) is stepped `FC_MONTHS` = 480 months through `core`, coupled (the network on), with no innovation, no user shock, no dollar addend and no import-demand gap, under the inputs the blocs expect at the base, month `m` (`s = skip + m`):

| Input | Forecast law (the projection the blocs themselves use) |
|---|---|
| `ex_m[k]` | `ex_b[s + k]` (zero beyond `K`): the expected policy path of the base month moved forward |
| `ius_m` | `ex_b[s]` (`ius_b` for `s` = 0) |
| `y_US` | `y_US,b rho_f^s` (`world_rho_f`, the persistence the blocs project activity with) |
| `oil`, `qty` | `oil_b rho_oil^s`, `qty_b rho_qty^s` |
| `Fex` | `Fex_b rho_fin^s` (`fin_factor_rho`), `Fex_b = F_b - Fbar - Fpol_b` read from the dollar state |
| dollar addend | zero (`a_b`, `qpsi_b`, `gbar_b` are zero at the base by 1.6b); `proj_on` false |
| innovations, shocks, `gap` | none |

The state the copy starts from includes `G` and `u_rp` as they stand, and the `UsHist` rings, so the first forecast months use the realised history. `z_fc,j(m)` for `m >= 480` is the last value (the expected US path is zero beyond `K` and every state decays; |z_fc| < 0.05 at the horizon). Properties, each a test: **(a)** fed these same inputs the block reproduces the forecast exactly (`forecast_reproduces_the_block`); **(b)** by linearity `z - z_fc` is the response of a zero-state block to the difference between the actual and the expected inputs, the *news* alone (`forecast_error_is_the_response_to_the_news_alone`, with the bounds off); **(c)** it does not depend on any shock, so the shocked and the unshocked arm subtract the same path and every paired difference of `ln D` and of `zrel` is unchanged (`forecast_is_shock_independent_and_shared`); **(d)** the first-month forecast error is an innovation, not a level (`first_month_forecast_error_is_innovation_sized`). Measured on 64 exploratory seeds (20,000 households): the weighted `z - z_fc` of month 0 has mean -0.05, sd 0.34, max |.| 1.08 points; the same statistic against the path of zero inputs has mean +1.49, sd 2.41, max 7.88. The bloc real exchange rate is therefore read as the model's own surprise, the dollar-link analogue of the "change since the base" of D37. The cost is one 480-month coupled bloc run per world (about 0.1-0.2 s), shared by the clones.

**The premium step, exactly as `exchange_rate` does it (`fx.rs:74-120`; unchanged since b7-transmission 6faf872).** `world::dollar` phase A makes the same calls and the same state updates, because `exchange_rate` is skipped when `dollar_active`:
```rust
let burn_in = w.tick_offset == 0 && w.tick < w.cfg.burn_in_months;        // false whenever dollar_active holds
let psi = fx::anchored_premium(w.fx_psi_state, w.fx_psi_exog_prev, w.exog.fx_psi(), w.cfg.fx_psi_rho, burn_in);
let mp_on = w.cfg.fx_mp_premium != 0.0;                                   // b7-transmission M-X; default 1.25 since joint 3
let z = if mp_on { fx::monetary_surprise_premium(w.cfg.fx_mp_premium, w.cfg.fx_mp_cap, w.shocks.policy_gap(w.tick)) } else { 0.0 };
// psi_step(ln_fx_prev, q_prev, psi_prev, ln_target, psi, a, w) -> (ln_fx, q); only q is used: q = (1-w) psi_now + w psi_prev; the other arguments only shape ln_fx
let (_, qpsi) = if mp_on { fx::psi_step(0.0, 0.0, w.fx_psi_state + w.monetary.fx_mp_prev, 0.0, psi + z, w.cfg.fx_adjust, w.cfg.fx_psi_weight) }
                else     { fx::psi_step(0.0, 0.0, w.fx_psi_state, 0.0, psi, w.cfg.fx_adjust, w.cfg.fx_psi_weight) };
if qpsi.is_finite() { w.fx_psi_state = psi; w.fx_psi_component = qpsi; if mp_on { w.monetary.fx_mp_prev = z; } }   // exchange_rate does this only when the new fx is finite
w.fx_psi_exog_prev = w.exog.fx_psi();                                      // kept whether or not the step was applied, as in exchange_rate
```
Differences from the rev-6 block: the state updates are guarded by finiteness as in `fx.rs:111-118` (the early `return` of `exchange_rate` on a non-finite `p_us` or `fx` does not exist here); `fx_psi_exog_prev` is updated last and always. Hand values for the test `dollar_premium_matches_exchange_rate` (`fx_mp_premium` 1.25, `fx_mp_cap` 0.03, `PolicyShift` 100 bp, 12 months, no psi): `z_0 = 0.012057`, `z_1 = 0.011115`, `z_2 = 0.010237` (`z_t = 1.25 x 0.03 tanh(gap_t/0.03)`, `gap_t = 0.01 (11/12)^t`); `qpsi_0 = 0.72 z_0 = 0.008681`, `qpsi_1 = 0.72 z_1 + 0.28 z_0 = 0.011378`. The test steps two tiny worlds (burn-in 0, 24 months, `fx_psi_sigma` 0.0215), one by `monetary::exchange_rate`, one with `dollar_active` and the dollar phase A only, and compares `fx_psi_component`, `fx_psi_state`, `fx_mp_prev` each month to 1e-15, for `fx_mp_premium` 0 and 1.25 (1.25 is the default now; 0 must be set explicitly).

**Interface to the blocs (`UsInputs` and `step_bloc`).** The merged `UsInputs` (`net.rs:13-22`) carries one scalar addend `dollar` and one decay `dollar_decay`, and `step_bloc` (`bloc.rs:255-262`) projects it as `a_t d^k`. Stage 4a adds three fields (the single full struct literal in `world/tests.rs` `us_from_row` takes `..UsInputs::zero()`):
```rust
pub dollar: f64,            // a_t = delta_t + q_t + gbar_t (was delta_t); the ring UsHist.a stores it
pub dollar_decay: f64,      // d_t of the delta part only (unchanged meaning)
pub dollar_delta: f64,      // NEW: delta_t
pub proj_on: bool,          // NEW: true only when dollar_active
pub dollar_proj: [f64; K],  // NEW: dollar_proj[k-1] = projected q + g + cred addend k months ahead, k = 1..K
```
`dollar_proj[k-1] = qpsi_t fx_psi_rho^k + gbar_t world_fx_risk_rho^k + sum_s ln(1 - dollar_pct_s/100) p_s(age_s + k)` (the last sum is exact, not an AR(1): the blocs know the credibility path as they know a `BlocPolicy` pin). In `step_bloc`, for `jrel > 0`: `a = if us.proj_on { us.dollar_delta * us.dollar_decay.powi(jrel) + us.dollar_proj[jrel - 1] } else { us.dollar * us.dollar_decay.powi(jrel) }`; the `jrel <= 0` branches are unchanged (`hist.a` holds realised `a`). With the dollar link off, `proj_on` is false and the arithmetic is the merged one. [assumed] The blocs see the broad addend `a_t` (common to all blocs, `gbar` not `g_j`): this keeps the merged common-addend code (`wsum[i] - 1`); the bilateral spread of `gamma_j` acts on `lnS_j` and on the trade module only.

The projection matters: for `UsCredibility{3.3, 3 months}` the exact path gives a bloc feedback of +0.029 on the impact-month weighted `z` (so `ln D` falls 3.327, within 0.05 of -3.36); projecting the same addend at 0.99 a month gives +0.32 (`ln D` -3.04) and the hand value fails.

**Non-policy risk in the blocs' demand.** `UsInputs.fex = F_t - Fbar - Fpol_t` (2.7), zero when the dollar link is off. `bloc.rs` has no `fex` term at `ccdcc48` (`fis[k]` at line 306): stage 4a adds `- theta_f[i] fexp[k]` to `fis[k]`, with `fexp[k] = fex rho_fin^k` (`rho_fin = fin_factor_rho`) and `theta_f[i] = par.theta[i] world_theta_f_ratio / world_vix_per_100bp` per VIX point (`par.theta` already holds the P\* scale and `world_theta_mult`), added only when `us.fex != 0.0`.

**M-F in the bloc channel (D33).** The merged code applies `bound_pp` to `ex[k]` in `core` (`mod.rs:236-239`) and not to `ius` separately (the rule and UIP forcing use `exb`). At the default `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0 it is the identity. W4-28 sets `fx_rate_knee` 0.02 and `fx_rate_sat_scale` 0.015 **only** (profile `b8-world-s4a-mf.json`); the diagnostic profiles `b7-pins-diag.json` and `b7-transmission-diag.json` also overwrite the J4 keys `disc_rate_*` with their pre-J4 values and must not be used. **Rev 9 (D48): the bound of the bloc dollar equation.** With the dollar link on, the premium term `lambda_rp,i gp[k]` is no longer outside the bound: `fS[k] = -( M(ex[k] + lambda_rp,i gp[k]) + u_t rho_rp^k )` with `M` the M-F function at `world_rp_sat_knee` 0.02, `world_rp_sat_scale` 0.015 (2.0 pp, 1.5 pp; ceiling 3.5 pp), whatever the `fx_rate_*` keys are; `fR` keeps `exb`. Hand values `M(x)`: `x` for `x <= 2`; 2.482269 at 2.5; 2.874174 at 3.0; 3.142391 at 3.5; 3.446041 at 5.0; 3.497443 at 7.3; 3.5 as `x -> inf` (`hand_values`). The totals: a +100 bp shock held 12 months has `G` peak 0.590 and `T` = 1 + 1.5 G = 1.89 (AE) to 2.18 (EME), held 24 months `G` 0.832 and `T` = 2.25 to 2.66, so the bound removes at most 0.0009 / 0.040 pp (0.04 % / 1.5 %) at the peak when the baseline total is zero; the baseline total moves with the ABM's policy gap (sd about 1.8 pp), so on average the bound takes 9-24 % off the first-year dollar response to a +100 bp surprise (exploratory seeds 1-16, paired broad `ln D` at months 1 / 3 / 6 / 12: 4.16 / 4.85 / 4.55 / 2.90 against 4.83 / 6.19 / 5.96 / 3.19 without the bound). A pin has `ius` 4.0-4.4 (pin7) or 7.0-7.4 pp (pin10) and `G` 2.4-3.7 / 4.2-6.2 after 12 / 24 months, so `T` is 8-14 pp and the bound holds it at 3.5 pp for the whole pin: the bloc dollar equation then prices the same excess return as the ABM's own M-F rule. In the stage-1 replays the bound is inactive by construction (the link is off) and, if switched on, would remove at most 0.0001 pp in the clean 2019 replay (`T` peaks at 2.082 for lambda 2.0) and up to 2.76 pp in the stress replay (`an_sat_replay`); W1-10..W1-20 and W1-23 are unchanged.

**`UsCredibility{tp_bp, months, dollar_pct}` (D31).** The dollar term is `cred_t` above; it is part of `q_t`, so on impact the broad index falls by `dollar_pct` less the bloc feedback. Hand values for `{tp_bp 22, months 3, dollar_pct 3.3}`: `100 cred_0 = 100 ln(0.967) = -3.3557` (exact, 1e-9); on `WorldBlock::step_inputs` with zero US inputs and the exact `dollar_proj`, the weighted bloc `z` moves +0.0285 in the impact month, so `100 ln D_0 = -3.327`, within 0.05 of the nominal -3.36 (test `credibility_dollar_hand_value`: `fx_psi_sigma`, `fx_mp_premium`, `world_shock_scale` and every `world_fx_idio_sd_*` set to 0, f process off). The term-premium part belongs to sub-round 4-2 (it needs the `update_curve` hook H5): `tp_bp p_t TP_SLOPE[j]/TP_SLOPE[3]` on tenor `j`, the 10y exact (rev 8, D46); `g` is not touched. The stage-2 pass-through and the exports use `z~_j = z_j + 100 a_{j,t}` (2.5), including `a` and excluding `zeta`. The master `fx_ppp_weight`, `fx_rate_sensitivity`, `r_world` and the partial adjustment are not used when `world_link_dollar` is on; `fx_psi_*`, `fx_adjust` (as an argument only), `fx_mp_*` and the b7 M-F keys are.

**A `Dollar` shock in dollar mode.** `delta_t` enters in full in the shock month (master moves 30 % of the way a month through `fx_adjust`). The blocs' UIP then offsets part of it: for `Dollar +10 %` permanent the weighted bloc `z` feedback is -0.91 (impact), -1.52 (m1), -2.59 (m5), -3.17 (m12) log points (isolated-bloc probe, zero other inputs), so `ln D` is 8.62 / 8.01 / 6.94 / 6.36 at impact / m1 / m5 / m12 and not 9.53. Rows that state a `Dollar +10 %` hand value in dollar mode are judged on this realised step (the S2-R6 form).

**What the dollar link does to the ABM's baseline (measured, PREFLIGHT 3).** The weighted bloc `z` is +14.9 % (sd 11.2) at the base month and unwinds to about 0 by month 60 (the ABM's own policy rate falls about 1 pp over the first 100 reported months: the start-up drift of the reported run, b9-warmup), so the block's `ln D` falls with the cross-seed mean path -3.6 % (reported month index 12, 0-based), -7.6 (24), -12.4 (36), -13.0 (60), -3.5 (120), -5.0 (239) against -0.9, -2.1, -2.7, -4.5, -3.7, -15.2 for the ABM's own `ln fx` (24 seeds, 5000 households, shadow run). Time means over months 12-239 agree (-7.9 % against -7.2 %), over months 12-59 they do not (-10.3 against -2.5). This is the model's own response to the ABM's rate path, not a defect, and it cancels in every matched pair; it is reported as W4-31 and checked for its effect on the ABM by W4-32. **Rev 9:** W4-32 failed on exactly this transient (the link replaced the ABM's baseline real dollar by one that unwound 4.8 / 6.5 / 8.7 points below `c_t`, core +0.344 over 128 seeds), so the real part is now read against the conditional forecast (D47) and the transient is gone from the ABM-facing dollar. What is left on 64 exploratory seeds: the ABM-facing real dollar `sum_j wH10_j (z_j - z_fc,j) + 100 a` against the ABM's own `c_t` is -0.1 / -0.7 / -1.1 / -0.9 / -3.5 points in months 0-2 / 3-11 / 12-59 / 60-119 / 120-239 (rev 7: about -5 to -9 from month 12), core PCE +0.108 (SE 0.026), policy +0.146 (0.041), unemployment -0.056 (0.014) against the matched cell; the remainder is the real dollar's own mean reversion in the ABM's rule (its `c_t` drifts +2 to +3 points; the forecast-error state has no drift) and the loss of the ABM's start-up transient. The nominal `ln D` still carries the inflation differential of the blocs' price levels against US tradables (+3.8 / +21.9 points above the ABM's own dollar in months 12-59 / 60-239, W4-31): the ABM does not read it in dollar mode with the trade link on, the official block reads its monthly change (D43), and the combined run after list B must check it.

### 2.7 Financial factor, official Treasury demand and term premium (`finance.rs`, stage 4; the factor and the injections in sub-round 4-1, the official block in 4-2)

**Rev 7 units rule (D39).** Every quantity of the official block is a **real-world-equivalent** quantity: a model amount divided by `k_FO`, and GDP is `GDP^rw`. This makes the hand values below independent of the ABM's size of RoW holdings (at the base month RoW holds 37.6 % of the face debt and 42.8 % of GDP in the model against 29.4 % and 28.4 % in the data; 24 seeds, 5000 households, `ccdcc48`; a GDP-based conversion would inflate every term-premium effect by 1.5 on average and by 1.2-2.3 between seeds). Model amounts are used only to execute trades and to hold the tranche.

```
F_t          = world_vix_per_unit f_t ,  f_t = w.exog.fin_factor (the ABM's own state, a state through burn-in);   Fbar = world_vix_per_unit fin_factor_mean(cfg)
inject       : in world::after_exog, reported months, world_link_dollar, after factors_step; each on its activation month only, so the response decays as f does (rho = fin_factor_rho)
               PolicyShift{bps}      : f += (world_vix_per_100bp/world_vix_per_unit)(bps/100)   (1.3 units = 13 VIX points per 100bp);  Fpol += world_vix_per_100bp (bps/100)
               RiskOff{vix_points}   : f += vix_points/world_vix_per_unit
               BlocDemand{risk_off}  : f += world_china_risk_pts_per_pct |pct| / world_vix_per_unit
Fpol_t       = fin_factor_rho Fpol_{t-1} + (this month's PolicyShift injection, VIX points)       (the policy-induced part of F; Fpol_b = 0)
Fex_t        = F_t - Fbar - Fpol_t       (UsInputs.fex; zero when world_link_dollar is off)
```
`PolicyShift` is not routed to `ShockState::world_shocks`; its activation is detected as `w.shocks.policy_shift = Some((b, t0, _))` with `t0 == w.tick` (`bps = b x 10000`; the slot holds one shock, so a second `PolicyShift` overwrites the first, as in the ABM). `RiskOff` and `BlocDemand` are read from `world_shocks` entries fired in this month through a cursor of their own (`fin_consumed`), separate from the bloc cursor of `step_month`. The earlier form `Fpol_t = rho_fin Fpol_{t-1} + 13 x 100 x (gap_t - rho_gap gap_{t-1})` is the same quantity written through the gap innovation; the injection form needs no reconstruction of the AR(1) gap. The injection happens after `factors_step` has used `f_t` for `tp_level` and `fx_psi` this month, so those see it from the next month; the ABM's equity risk premium (`equity.rs:89`, `erp_fin_loading x f_t`, unsmoothed) sees it in the same month at `monetary::markets`.

```
official tranche of RoW Treasuries (sub-round 4-2), captured at the end of the last burn-in month (the base month b) by `FinState::capture`, called from the `base_month` branch of `WorldBlock::settle` (where the trade and dollar captures are made for a world with a burn-in) and from `capture_bases` (the first-step fallback of a world without burn-in); once; a shock never captures it:
  H_j,b        = nu x official_share_j x tic_share_j x RoW_MV_b ,   tic_share_j = TIC_j / sum_k TIC_k   (finance treasury_demand.foreign_holdings_ust_bn_2026_07, official_share_of_bloc_ust_holdings)
  nu           = foreign_official_ust_bn (3773.1) / sum_j official_share_j TIC_j = 3773.1 / 3786.28 = 0.99652      (the bloc shares are an allocation; the sum must give the sourced total)
  FO_b         = sum_j H_j,b = 0.40800 RoW_MV_b  (3773.1/9247.9);   k_FO = FO_b / 3773.1  (model units per real-world $bn; seed-specific);   RoW_MV_b = w.row.bonds_value of the base month
  tau_t        = fiscal::trend_gdp_month(w) as read by finance::step_flows in world::step of month t, that is the value left by plan_spending of month t-1 (plan_spending runs after world::step in the tick); tau_b = the value at the capture (left by plan_spending of the base month) = the value the first reported step reads, so tau_{-1} = tau_b
  H_j,t        = H_j,b tau_t/tau_b + dH_j,t ,   dH_j,t = dH_j,t-1 tau_t/tau_{t-1} + executed_j,t       (model units; the memo path grows with the realised trend, no trade; dH = 0.0 bit for bit until a flow has executed)
  FO^rw_t      = (sum_j H_j,t) / k_FO  ($bn) ;   GDP^rw_t = world_gdp_rw_bn tau_t/tau_b  ($bn, annualised; world_gdp_rw_bn = 32560)
  stock_t      = 100 (sum_j dH_j,t / k_FO) / GDP^rw_t    (= 100 (FO^rw_t/GDP^rw_t - FO^rw_b/GDP^rw_b): the memo path and GDP^rw share the factor tau_t/tau_b, so the base ratio 3773.1/32560 cancels; % of annual GDP)
  ln D_t       = ln(w.row.fx / fx_0), fx_0 = the broad rate of the base month (the trade base fx0). With the dollar link off (S4-R15) it is the ABM's own dollar; ln D_b = 0, so the first reported month carries the ABM's own month-0 step (sd 1.7 %)
  F_t          = world_vix_per_unit f_t, the ABM's own f_t (the injections of 2.7 above act only with the dollar link); Fbar = world_vix_per_unit fin_factor_mean(cfg) (4.588 points under the profile, 0 with the default keys, where f is identically zero)
N_j,t [$bn rw]= tr_j/12 + eta 100 (ln D_t - ln D_{t-1}) H_j,t-1/k_FO + v_vix mean_6(F - Fbar) H_j,t-1/FO_t-1 + OfficialTreasury shock share      (D32), then N_j,t >= -H_j,t-1/k_FO (a bloc cannot sell more than it holds, D44)
             tr_CN = world_fo_trend_cn (-80 $bn a year), others 0 ; eta = world_fo_dollar_resp (-0.0023 per 1 % dollar rise, 6-month window: the monthly rule sums to eta x6) ; v_vix = 1.05 ; mean_6 = the mean of the last 6 values of F - Fbar INCLUDING this month's (the ring is updated first; it is filled every month from the ABM's f_t, burn-in included)
             OfficialTreasury{bloc, bn, months}: bn/months a month for `months` months from the shock month, to bloc `bloc`, or pro rata to H_j,t-1 when `bloc` is null
execute      : N = k_FO sum_j N_j (model units);  N > 0: monetary::trade_with(RoW, [Bank_0 .. Bank_nb-1, Fund], N, true) (Fund last, D45);  N < 0: monetary::trade_with_banks(RoW, |N|, false) (capped by what RoW holds);  executed share s = executed/|N|;  dH_j += s N_j k_FO;  extra += -(executed purchases) (the cumulative net official SALES at market value, model units; read by `rebalance_row`, hook H5, D45)
T_t          = world_fo_trans_rho T_{t-1} + 100 N_t^exec,rw / GDP^rw_t               (% of annual GDP; N^exec,rw = executed amount / k_FO; T_b = 0)
tp_FO_j,t    = -TP_SLOPE[j] [ (theta_L/1e4) stock_t + (theta_T/1e4) T_t ]   (decimal, exactly 0.0 until a flow has executed; folded into `tp` in the base loop of update_curve, hook H5, from the state of month t-1; theta_L 13, theta_T 20)
tp_CRED_j,t  = sum over active UsCredibility shocks of (tp_bp/1e4) p(age) TP_SLOPE[j]/TP_SLOPE[3]   (D46; p of 2.6; age 0 in the shock month; the 10y exact, the 3m zero)
```
**What the code reads, and when.** `world::after_exog` (before the curve of the month) registers the `OfficialTreasury` programmes and the term-premium part of `UsCredibility` fired since the last call, so a credibility shock moves the curve of its own month. `world::step` runs `step_month` and then `finance::step_flows` (the block is out of `w` in both; `step_flows` takes the finance state as an argument): first the F ring, then in reported months the flows. The dollar of month t (the ABM's `exchange_rate`, which runs before `world::step`; the link's phase B when the dollar link is on) is the one the rule sees. `update_curve` of month t+1 reads `tp_FO` and `tp_CRED` through `world_tp_fo`; `rebalance_row` of month t reads `extra` through `world_row_extra` after the month's flows (`markets` runs after `world::step`).

**The trend is the realised one.** `trend_gdp_month` is not a closed-form path: `tau <- tau (1 + g/12) + 0.03 (GDP - tau)` with `g` = `nominal_trend_growth` (3.7 %), updated in `plan_spending`. On the 64 E4a runs (J4 defaults, stage-4 profile) it grows 3.75 % a year; the cross-seed sd of `tau_t/tau_b` is 0.8 % at month 12, 3.7 % at 60 and 9.9 % at 239. The hand series below use the realised paths of those runs; a test that checks a series to 1e-9 builds its expectation from the world's own `trend_gdp_month` path.

**Hand values** (`world_fo_*` at the defaults; `hand_values`, `an_baseline_drift_cap` of `world/stage4_2_prep`). A $100bn purchase is `100/32560 = 0.30713 %` of `GDP^rw`: the stock term is `13 x 0.30713 = 3.99 bp`, the transitory term `20 x 0.30713 = 6.14 bp`, so the 10y falls 10.14 bp in the first month after the flow (record index T0+1, h = 2; the curve of the shock month itself is priced before the flow), 7.25 bp 13 months after, and tends to 3.99 bp (4.30 in the window h = 49..60). The mean of the paired 10y over h = 1..12 is -7.95 bp (the h = 1 value is 0). The response is the same in every seed to 0.001 bp whatever the RoW share of GDP (37 to 65 % at the base, 8 seeds): the units rule works. With no world flow `tp_FO = 0` bit for bit; the CN trend flow `tr_CN` (-80 $bn in 2026 dollars a year against a tranche that grows with trend GDP) is **not** neutral in the baseline. The CN tranche (585 $bn rw at the base, growing 3.75 % a year) is sold at 80 a year and **runs out at month 102 (93-111 over 64 seeds)**; the per-bloc cap (D44) then stops the flow, so the baseline 10y term premium that the official block adds is, with the realised trend and the ABM's own dollar term (seed sd 5.5 to 7 bp from the dollar term alone, plus 3 bp at 12 months and 8 bp at 60 months from the VIX term):

| record index (h - 1) | 1 | 11 | 35 | 59 | 107 | 119 | 179 | 239 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 10y tp_FO, bp (capped, rev 8) | 0.8 | 6.5 | 14.1 | 19.5 | 27.2 | 23.4 | 20.5 | 18.8 |
| the uncapped extrapolation of rev 7, bp | 0.8 | 6.5 | 14.1 | 19.5 | 29.3 | 29.6 | 38.2 | 44.4 |

(the CN trend alone, uncapped: 0.7 / 6.3 / 14.8 / 20.6 / 31.9 / 48.7 at records 1 / 11 / 35 / 59 / 119 / 239, the rev-7 series, confirmed on the realised trend). The rise after month 100 stops and the transitory part decays; the stock part (about +19 bp, 1.46 % of GDP) stays. This is the sourced trend (the foreign official holdings fell 76 $bn a year between February 2020 and July 2026) and it cancels in every matched pair, but it moves the absolute baseline rates; W4-32 judges it, and `world_fo_trend_cn` {-80, 0} is a pre-authorised lever of sub-round 4-2 (S4-h). Hand value of the dollar rule: a one-month step of `100 ln 1.10 = 9.531` gives `-0.0023 x 9.531 x 3773.1 = -$82.71bn` (test `official_flow_monthly_rule_hand_values`, -83 +/- 3). With the ABM's own dollar (link off) a permanent `Dollar +10 %` is `ln D_h = 9.531 (1 - 0.7^h)` before feedback (`fx_adjust` 0.3), so the flow is -24.81 in the impact month and the cumulative holdings change through month 6 (h = 6, record T0+5) is -72.98; the stored `dollar10` cell gives the realised -24.81 and -72.20 (SE 0.55, 12 seeds; the paired `ln D` is 2.86 / 4.85 / 6.24 / 7.23 / 7.87 / 8.28) - the pre-registered W4-12 value. In dollar mode the realised step is smaller (2.6: -74.8 and -60.3); that row is deferred (D43). `OfficialTreasury{bn, months}` buys or sells `bn/months` real-world $bn a month for `months` months from the shock month; the preset `fo400` (CN, -400 over 6 months) gives a stock term of 15.97 bp and a transitory term of 21.1 bp: the 10y rises 31.4 bp at h = 6, **37.05 bp at h = 7** (the first month after the sixth flow; rev 7 said 37.4 at "month 6", the constant-GDP value and the wrong month), and 25.19 bp as the mean over h = 1..12. With `world_fo_trend_cn` -80 the CN tranche (585) cannot carry both the 400 and the trend: it is exhausted 2.3 years after the shock, the shocked arm stops selling while the baseline arm goes on, and the paired response decays (8.2 bp in the window h = 49..60); with the trend off the window is 17.3 bp (hand; 20.0 measured on 8 seeds). The tranche is a sub-account: QE, auctions and `rebalance_row` change RoW holdings but not `H_j`; the private duration (`monetary::private_duration`) counts every holder except the central bank, so an official trade with the banks or the Fund leaves it unchanged (no double counting, D19), and the 10y response to QE differs by -0.13 bp from the response to the same QE with the explicit term absent (mechanism) and by +0.33 bp (SE 0.91, 32 seeds) in the 10y itself. Foreign term premia (memo): `TP_j = tp_base[3] + lambda_j (TP_us10,t - TP_us10,0)`, `lambda_j = us_policy_spillovers.foreign_term_premium_passthrough_lambda.by_bloc.<j>`; `y10_j = i_inf,j + [(1-0.97^120)/(0.03*120)] (i_j - i_inf,j) + TP_j`; the paired change is `0.2706 d i_j + lambda_j d TP_us10` (W4-10 is computed from the stored `world.policy` and the research table, not in Rust).

### 2.8 Positions, income and identities (`positions.rs`, stage 4-3)

**Rev 10.** Re-derived against `cc33f15` (PREFLIGHT 2-3, 5); a compiled sketch (`probes/proto_4_3_sketch.patch`) holds the identity to 4e-14 of monthly GDP over 240 months and 8 seeds and over every event cell. Active when `world_positions` is true; it changes no ABM quantity (D54).

**Stocks.** Nine stocks per bloc, in model units. Seven are memo: the research values (real-world $bn, `finance.cross_border.*`, copied by `build_positions`, bloc order `H10_KEYS`) times `k_GDP,b = 12 tau_b / world_gdp_rw_bn` once, at the base month, with `tau_b = fiscal::trend_gdp_month(w)` at the capture (the `tau_b` of the official block, D44; rev 10: not the actual GDP of the base month, whose ratio to the trend has sd 1.5 % across seeds [measured, 32 seeds], D52). Memo assets: `A_eq` = `us_holdings_of_foreign_securities_bn_2025_12.equity.by_bloc`, `A_debt` = `...{lt_debt + st_debt}`, `A_fdi` = `us_direct_investment_abroad_bn_2025_hist_cost` x `market_to_historical_cost_ratio_fdi.assets` 1.94, `A_bank` = `us_bank_cross_border_claims_bn_2026q1`; memo liabilities: `L_ag` = `foreign_holdings_of_us_securities_bn_2025_06.{lt_agency + lt_corporate + st_debt}`, `L_eq` = `...equities`, `L_fdi` = `fdi_in_united_states_bn_2025_hist_cost` x `...liabilities` 2.47. Two are ledger-backed and are read at the end of every month, never captured: `L_ust_j = share^tic_j x w.row.bonds_value` (`treasury_demand.foreign_holdings_ust_bn_2026_07.by_bloc`, normalised to sum 1) and `L_dep_j = share^dep_j x w.row.deposits` (`us_bank_cross_border_liabilities_bn_2026q1.by_bloc`, normalised).

**Month update** (`WorldBlock::settle`, reported months, after `goods::clear`, the markets and the close of the books; the base month captures instead and updates nothing):
```
ds_j   = change of ln S_j in the month (foreign currency per dollar; plus = dollar up):  ln(w.row.fx_t / w.row.fx_{t-1}) for every bloc when world_link_dollar is false (the block has no bilateral nominal rates then; rev 10),
         the change of the dollar module's ln S_j (DollarState::ln_s_now, relative to its base) when it is true
r_us   = ln(w.fund.share_price_t / w.fund.share_price_{t-1})
eta_j  = 0.02 x N(0,1) from agent_rng(seed, tick, WORLD_FX, 16 + j)     (index 16 + j: the dollar module's zeta uses 0..11; sd [assumed])
X_t    = X_{t-1} (1 + v + y/12),    y = 3.8 % for assets, 2.55 % for liabilities (implied_annual_yield_on_iip_positions_pct)
v:  A_eq: b_j r_us + eta_j - 0.9 ds_j ;  A_debt: -0.23 ds_j ;  A_fdi: -1.0 ds_j ;  A_bank: -0.3 ds_j [assumed] ;  L_ag: -0.03 ds_j ;  L_eq: r_us ;  L_fdi: r_us
        (b_j = global_financial_cycle.equity_loading_relative_to_us.by_bloc; the foreign-currency shares are 0.9, 1 - 0.77, 1.0, 0.3, 0.03)
PI_memo = sum_j [ y_A/12 sum(A_{t-1}) - y_L/12 sum(L_{t-1}) ] ;   Val_memo = sum_j [ sum(v A_{t-1}) - sum(v L_{t-1}) ]            (the pure function PositionsState::memo_step)
CA_ledger = (w.row.exports_this_month - w.row.imports_this_month) - (coupons + deposit interest paid to RoW this month)        (customs values)
Val_hold  = sum over the bonds RoW held at the end of t-1 of face_{t-1} (p_t - p_{t-1}),  p = monetary::issue_price at the month's final marks; a bond that has matured is priced 1.0
Val_prim  = sum over RoW's purchases at this month's auction of (face p_t - cash paid)     (the auction pays par; p_t is not 1 when a tenor's yield is below zero and the coupon is floored at 0)
NIIP_t    = sum A - sum L_memo - w.row.bonds_value - w.row.deposits
identity:   NIIP_t - NIIP_{t-1} = CA_ledger + PI_memo + Val_memo - Val_hold - Val_prim
```
`Val_hold` and `Val_prim` come from the ledger's own prices (`issue_price`), not from the stocks they test; the coupon, the deposit interest and the auction purchases are the amounts the ledger paid, accumulated by hooks H19-H21 (they cannot be rebuilt from end-of-month states: the holdings move between the auction, the service of the debt, QE and the rebalances). Official trades (finance link) and QE are ledger trades at `issue_price`, so they leave the identity exact (tests with both). The residual `NIIP_t - NIIP_{t-1} - (CA_ledger + PI_memo + Val_memo - Val_hold - Val_prim)` is `sfc_world_error x GDP`; the bound is 1e-9 of monthly GDP. **Bilateral:** `ca_j = X_j - M_j - (coupons share^tic_j + deposit interest share^dep_j) + PI_j`, with `X_j`, `M_j` the settled bilateral exports and customs imports of the trade state (they sum to the ledger totals: `exp_bloc`, `imp_bloc`); `sum_j ca_j = CA_ledger + PI_memo = ca_total`. **Base and scale:** the memo stocks, `NIIP_{t-1}`, `ln fx_{t-1}`, `share_price_{t-1}` and RoW's bonds with their prices are captured at the end of the last burn-in month (`settle`'s `base_month` branch, and `capture_bases` for a world without burn-in; there the first update has no previous marks, its residual is set to 0 and the identity holds from the second month). **Metrics:** `niip_pct_gdp` = 100 NIIP / (12 gdp), `ca_pct_gdp` = 100 ca_total / gdp, `ca_bilateral_bn[j]` = ca_j x 12 x scale / 1e9, `sfc_world_error` = |residual| / gdp, with gdp = `common::gdp_month_model(w)`; all are 0 while the switch is off. The memo yields are constants: the primary-income balance does not move with the model's interest rates (open item 33).
Sums over blocs of exports and imports equal the ledger totals; `world::check_identities` (hook H22, debug builds, after `ledger::check_consistency`) panics with the tick when the residual exceeds 1e-9 of monthly GDP or the bilateral accounts do not sum. **Coverage (rev 7, D40).** The nine stocks cover assets 37.57 tn of the IIP's 46.97 tn and liabilities 57.03 tn of its 69.39 tn: memo NIIP = -19.46 tn = -59.8 % of 32.56 tn (-59.77), memo assets 115.38 % and memo liabilities (without the two ledger stocks) 129.33 % of GDP, not the aggregate -68.9 % (reserves, derivatives and non-bank other investment are not modelled); the memo primary income is +1.086 % of GDP, and with the two ledger stocks at research scale (TIC 9.248 tn, BIS liabilities 5.671 tn) and the liability yield 3.8 % x 37.57 - 2.55 % x 57.03 = -0.027 tn = -0.082 % of GDP. With the two ledger stocks at the ABM's scale (RoW's Treasuries and deposits 42.8 % and 8.6 % of GDP at the base against 28.4 % and 17.4 % in the data) the model's own `niip_pct_gdp` is **-74.7 % at the first reported month (sd 11.4 across 8 probe seeds), -80.7 % at month 60 and -121.7 % (sd 35) at month 240** with a current account of -5.0 % of GDP (sd 1.25): the ABM's RoW keeps accumulating US assets; it is reported (W4-21) and the baseline drift is an ABM property, not the block's (open item 33). The uniform-dollar valuation (W4-22) is `-ln(1.10) x (FC assets 29.826 tn - FC liabilities 0.234 tn)/32.56 tn = -8.662 %` (a 10 % step: -9.089 %); `PositionsState::memo_step` with `ds = ln 1.10` for every bloc, `r_us = 0` and `eta = 0` returns it exactly at any scale. **Fund sleeve (4e):** value `E_t = units_t P^fe_t`, `ln P^fe_t = ln P^fe_{t-1} + sum_j phi_j (r^e_j - Dln S_j)` (`phi` = SHC equity shares), target `E* = world_sleeve_target x fund.equity_value`; each month trade `world_sleeve_speed (E* - E)` capped at 2% of Fund NAV by `ledger::pay(Fund -> RoW)` (buy) or `(RoW -> Fund)` (sell); dividends `world_sleeve_div_yield/12 E` paid `RoW -> Fund`; NAV includes `E` (hook H10). **Sleeve continuity (rev 7, 1.6b).** At the base month the sleeve is set to its target by re-labelling part of the Fund's existing equity holdings (`E_b = world_sleeve_target x fund.equity_value`; no `ledger::pay`, no cash move, the Fund's NAV unchanged to 1e-12); only later trades move cash.

---

## 3. Stages

### 3.0 Common to all stages

**Setup (every stage).** (1) `df -h /`: stop if under 4 GB free. (2) Worktree `git -C $R worktree add $DEV/<branch> -b <branch> <base>`; `$R` = the main checkout, which stays on master and clean; `$DEV` = the folder that holds the worktrees. (3) Freeze the base binaries before any change: copy `econsim-cli`, `econsim-server`, `hcheck`, `profile` from the base build to `$S/bin/base-*` (`$S` = a scratch directory of the session). (4) Record the base fingerprint: md5 of `run 5000 60 3 --json | tail -n 1` at `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1` and `8` (equal), and `scripts/json_strip_compare.py` reference files for seeds 1-32 (20000 x 240). The post-joint-3 value replaces `3215ea024d277e00ac88f556b9b92f6b` (the value on 0242ab9). At `ccdcc48` (joint calibration 4 defaults, block off) it is `9f90c88a2c8b653857029d235cd6b0a6` (measured on a `git archive` build, rev 7).

**Measurement harness `scripts/world_check.py`** (write in stage 1, extend later; model on `scripts/transmission_irf.py` and `scripts/tariff_check.py`). Subcommands: `run` (start `--servers` copies of `econsim-server`, `PORT` set, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, one `POST /api/compare` per `(scenario, seed)` with a single seed, store `DIR/<cell>/<scenario>_s<seed>.json.gz` with the listed fields and `world` keys; kill only the PIDs it started), `stats` (mean path over seeds; statistic on the mean path; leave-one-seed-out jackknife SE; paired differences against a base cell), `gates --stage k` (prints one PASS/FAIL line per target id of section 4 with value, band, nearest-bound distance in SE, flagging rows within 1 SE; **F rows and G rows are printed in separate tables and the summary counts quote G only**, D30), `moments` (240-month default runs: dollar/oil/rate moments), `identity` (I1-I3 below), `cost` (total ms/month of the full step, world on against world off, with the `profile` bin at 5000 households, RAYON 1 and 8, 3 repeats, best of; the per-phase `world`/`exog` rows alone miss `settle` and the hooks in `goods` and `curve`). Scenario codes (overlay `config` applies to both arms): `pol100` PolicyShift 100bp 12m; `pol100l` PolicyShift 100bp 24m; `dollar10` Dollar +10 hl 0; `dollarm10` Dollar -10; `tar10`/`tar25` ImportTariff; `tarCN25` Tariff{CN,None,25,false}; `tarCN15r` Tariff{CN,None,15,true}; `oil50` OilPrice 50 hl 12; `opec2` OpecCut 2.0; `hormuz` OilSupply{12,1,3,6}; `riskoff14` RiskOff 14; `uscred` UsCredibility{tp_bp 22, months 3, dollar_pct 3.3}; `pin7`/`pin10` PolicyRate pin 7% / 10% for 24 months (`transmission_irf.py`); `fo400` OfficialTreasury{CN,-400,6}; `qe1` QE 10y 1%/m x12; `ecb100` BlocPolicy{EA,100,12}; `china3` BlocDemand{CN,-3,12,risk_off}. Defaults: 10,000 households, seeds 1-16 (1-32 where stated), 60 months. Every statistic is a shocked-minus-baseline mean over seeds.

**Identity checks (all stages).** I1: world off, `run 5000 60 3 --json | tail -n 1` md5 equal to the recorded base md5 at RAYON 1 and 8. I2: 32 seeds x 20000 x 240 on the base CLI and the branch CLI, 8 at a time, `json_strip_compare.py base new` exit 0 with **"stripped 0 new keys"** (the `world` key must be absent). I3: `irf 10000 36 12` identical after removing timings (`sed -E 's/  \([0-9.]+[a-zµ]*s\) ===/ ===/'`); `hcheck 4 20000 240` zero failures and identical to base. I4 (stages 1-4): shadow identity, with `world_block_enabled=true` and every link off, all non-`world` keys of the 32-seed output are bit-identical to I2's base (`json_strip_compare` with `world` stripped: exit 0, "stripped 1 new keys"). From stage 2 on I4 applies to the stage's *earlier* links only (e.g. stage 3 with `world_link_oil` off must equal stage 2 with its links as they were). **I5** (4-2): `world_link_finance` on with every driver at zero equals the link off in every non-world key. **I6** (4-1b): `world_dollar_neutral` false and `world_rp_sat_scale` 0 with the dollar link on equals the `cc33f15` link-on output in every key but `z_fc`. **I7** (4-3): `world_retaliation` and `world_positions` on change no non-world key in a run without a flagged `Tariff` (differing records 0; the four position world keys excepted). **I8** (merged tree, S4-R54): with the dollar link on, the 4-3 switches change no non-world key of any record (`b8-world-s4e.json` against `b8-world-s4d.json`: differing records 0; `niip_pct_gdp`, `ca_pct_gdp`, `ca_bilateral_bn` and `sfc_world_error` are the only world keys that change). **I9** (stage 5, D58 and D61): `scripts/profiles/legacy_b8off.json` reproduces the default fingerprint of I1 (`9f90c88a2c8b653857029d235cd6b0a6`) at RAYON 1 and 8 and under the alternative codegen; after the joint switch the same statement holds for `legacy_defaults`. **I10** (5-T2, D62 and D63): with `world_import_rel_drift` 0 and no uniform `Tariff` the stage-5 profiles (`s5a`, `s5b`, `s5c`, `s5a-nc`, `s5c-nd`, `s5a-lean`) reproduce their stage-5 fingerprints (`51001607...`, `78287b98...`, `d67ad754...`, `3bbb033c...`, `852b9a3d...`, `e20bc1a4...`) at RAYON 1 and 8, and `ImportTariff` runs of the 5-T2 build are byte-identical to the stage-5 build's (the fold is inert without a uniform `Tariff`).

**Selection and stop conventions (b7 style).** The pre-registered values (section 1.5, 2.2.4, 2.5) are measured once. One group of parameters may change per round, only for the gate named, **at most 2 rounds per lever group** (a lever group is the list of levers against one gate family in the stage's *Selection* paragraph) and at most 4 rounds per stage (8 in stage 4, which has five lever groups); never touch an ABM parameter that is not named. If several grid points pass, take the one with the largest minimum distance to a band edge (in band widths); ties go to the pre-registered value. A stop rule ships the link **off by default** with its best parameters in `scripts/profiles/b8-world-sN.json`. Tuning an F row is allowed (that is what F means); a G row is never tuned: a failing G row is reported and decides by the stop rules. The only exceptions are the levers of D13 (W2-01) and D5 (W3-15): a G row on which such a lever fires is reported F for that run (D30).

**Stage-4 protocol (rev 7; pre-registered before any stage-4 result; D41).** (1) *Horizon.* Month `h` after a shock scheduled at reported month `T0` is record index `T0 + h - 1` of the 0-based record vector (the `irf` and `transmission_irf.py` convention, S3-R2); `h = 1` is the shock month ("impact"). An AR(1) response with impact `x` is `x rho^(h-1)` at month `h`: the W4-02 6-month and 12-month rows use the exponents 5 and 11. (2) *Cells and pairing.* A link G row compares the cell with the link on (L) with the matched cell with the link off (E: block on, every earlier link as shipped, the same ABM keys and profile) on the same seeds (S2-R2); the profile's own ABM effect (E against W, the block-off world with the same ABM keys) is reported separately. The ABM keys of the stage-4 profile are the f-process keys and `erp_fin_loading`. (3) *Seeds.* 240-month default-run rows (G1-G4, the W2-15 shares, W4-29, W4-31, W4-32, the moments W4-01/05/06): 64 seeds (1-64), 20,000 households. Event rows: 16 seeds, 10,000 households, 60 months (W4-26: 32 seeds; W4-28: 36 months; W4-18: 64 paired). A G row within 1 SE of a band edge is re-run once on 48 seeds (existing rule). (4) *Confirmatory rule.* A G row that fails with its statistic within 1 SE of the bound (a G4 statistic with Z in [-2.5, -2.0)) is re-run once on fresh seeds 1001-1064 (event rows 1001-1048); the decision is the pooled estimate; no other seed set is ever added and no band or tolerance changes. G4 and the policy counts of G2 are evaluated once per sub-round, on its final switch combination; earlier prefixes are looked at only to bisect a failure. (5) *Power.* A point rule is gated only where a correct model passes with probability >= 0.8 (PREFLIGHT 6). W4-26 row P7 cannot be decided by a point rule at any feasible seed count (master itself: 0.473 with SE 0.34 at 16 seeds; pass probability 0.55 at 12 seeds) and is judged paired against E; the G2 policy counts of master sit at the limit (2 of 32 seeds below 1.0) and are judged against the matched count; every other stage-4 G row has a margin of 2.2 SE or more at its stated seed count. (6) *Harness pins.* Every script request body carries `config.warmup_months` explicitly (0); every server a script starts has `ECONSIM_WARMUP_MONTHS=0` and `ECONSIM_CACHE_MB=0`; a script that reads a compare response asserts that it has no `warmup_months` key (the field is omitted at 0, `sim.rs` `Comparison`). The 24-month server default (`crates/econsim-server/src/main.rs:46`) applies to the web and to stage 5: W5-04 presets are measured at warm-up 0 and 24 and both are reported. (7) *Ports and CPU.* `world_check.py` starts its servers on a block of free local ports named in the dispatch; at most the process budget named in the dispatch, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, `python3 -B -I`; only PIDs the script started are stopped.

**Sub-round 4-2 protocol (rev 8; pre-registered before any 4-2 run).** (1) *Switches and cells.* `world_link_dollar` false, `world_link_trade` true, `world_link_finance` true in `L4b` (`b8-world-s4b.json`); the matched cell `E4b` is `b8-world-s4a.json` as shipped (dollar off, finance off) - the E4a of 4-1; the horizon, pairing and confirmatory rules of the paragraph above apply. (2) *Seeds, fresh for finance on every one.* Default runs 2001-2064; confirmatory 2101-2164; events 1001-1016 (W4-14 and W4-10: 1001-1032), event confirmatory 1017-1048. Nothing with `world_link_finance` on has been run on any seed except the pre-flight probes on 5001-5032 and 6001-6032, which decide no row. Used by 4-1 and its review without the finance link: 1-64 and 1001-1064 (the E4a and L4a cells), events 1-32, pins 1-16 and 1001-1016, the ablations 1-24, and the E cell and the dollar prototypes on 2001-2064; the dollar redesign (list B, S4-R15) will measure on the same default seeds 2001-2064 and its pins on 2101-2116, so 4-2 and list B share the matched cell and nothing else. (3) *E is re-run* on the 4-2 binary for every seed used (about 8 s a run); the stored `big_E2` (2001-2064) must agree bit for bit on the non-world keys (I4) and is the cross-check. (4) *Power.* W4-14 is judged on 32 seeds (the statistic is the difference of two 10y responses whose arms diverge: paired sd 5.2 bp at h = 12, SE 1.29 at 16 seeds and 0.91 at 32; pass probability of a correct model 0.88 and 0.97), pooled with 1033-1048 under rule (4). The W4-11 long-run window (paired sd 7.2 bp) passes a correct model with probability 0.91 at 16 seeds and is pooled to 48 when within 1 SE. W4-32 G1 `y_sd` is the row at risk: the matched cell sits 0.028 above its lower edge on 2001-2064 (1.128, SE 0.039; 1.170 on 1-64 and 1.267 on 1001-1064), the paired SE is 0.016 at 64 seeds, so a miss on the first set is decided on the pooled 128. (5) *Ports and CPU.* `world_check.py` takes its own block of free local ports; at most 2 simulation processes; no process the script did not start is touched.

**Sub-round 4-1b protocol (rev 9, D49; pre-registered before any 4-1b run).** (1) *Switches and cells.* The base is `cc33f15` (head of `b8-world-s4b`, list A') plus the 4-3 spec if it has landed; worktree `b8-world-s4d`. `L4d` = `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s4d.json` (the dollar link on, `world_link_trade` true, `world_link_finance` false: the cumulative prefix with the finance link is the combined run after list B, D43; `world_dollar_neutral` true, `world_rp_sat_knee` 0.02, `world_rp_sat_scale` 0.015); `E4d` = `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s4a.json` as shipped (block on, trade on, dollar off; the E4a and E4b of earlier rounds, bit-identical on the 4-1b binary); `W` = the block off with the same overlay (pins: `b8-world-s4d-mf.json` for L and the block off for W). `legacy_s4a_dollar.json` is used for I6 only. The horizon, pairing, harness pins and process rules of rules (1)-(7) above apply. (2) *Seeds, fresh for the redesigned link.* Default runs (G1-G4, W2-15, W4-29, W4-31, W4-32, the moments W4-01/05/06): **3201-3264** (20,000 households, 240 months), confirmatory **3301-3364**; events (W4-02, W4-03, W4-04 dollar half, W4-07, W4-08, W4-09, W4-15, W4-16, W4-26): **1201-1216**, W4-26 **1201-1232**, event confirmatory **1217-1248** (W4-26: 1233-1248); pins (W4-28): **2201-2216** (S4-R25), confirmatory pins **2217-2232**. These sets are added to S4-R25 (4-3 holds 3001-3164 and 1101-1164); none was used by any earlier round, the reviewers or this pre-flight (the pre-flight's design probes used the exploratory seeds 1-64 only and decide no row). (3) *Re-run.* `E4d` and `W` are re-run on the 4-1b binary for every seed used; stored 4-1 and 4-2 cells are not evidence for list B. (4) *Confirmatory rule (D41 rule 4), with one addition:* a G count row (G2 policy counts, G2 spike guard) that fails by one unit counts as within 1 SE; the decision on a re-run row is the pooled estimate with the limits `joint_gates.py --stage4` computes for the pooled n. (5) *Pre-registered readings.* W4-29 clause 2 and the G4 effect floor are the two readings of D49 [assumed; S4-R26]. The G4 floor, for the per-seed statistics `u_ac1` and `tot_lt25` (the pooled ones keep Z only): a statistic with Z < -2.0 is a failure only if its paired shift `|d|` is at least 0.2 times the cross-seed sd of that statistic in the matched cell (`u_ac1` on seeds 1-64: sd 0.044, floor 0.0088, shifts over five variants of the link -0.004 to +0.003; `tot_lt25`: sd 15.3, floor 3.07); the unfloored Z is always printed (`world_check.py g4-floor`). (6) *Ports and CPU.* `world_check.py` takes its own block of free local ports; at most 2 simulation processes; no process the script did not start is touched.

**Sub-round 4-3 protocol (rev 10; pre-registered before any 4-3 run on a registered seed).** (1) *Switches and cells.* `world_link_dollar` false, `world_link_finance` false (S4-R24), `world_link_trade` true; `E4c` is `b8-world-s4a.json` as shipped (the E4a of 4-1), `L4c` is `b8-world-s4c.json` (`E4c` plus `world_retaliation` and `world_positions` true). The horizon, pairing and confirmatory rules of the stage-4 protocol apply with two readings for this sub-round: an event row compares the shocked arm with its own baseline arm inside `compare` (as in 4-2), and the retaliation effect is read against the matched switch-off cell, the same shock in `E4c` (`E4c_tarCN15r`: the flag is ignored there and the goods exports to China must move by less than 0.5 %); the default-run rows are identities (D54). (2) *Seeds (S4-R25), fresh for retaliation and positions.* Default runs 3001-3064 in `E4c` and `L4c` (the confirmatory set 3101-3164 is reserved for pooling the reported moments to 128 seeds; an identity needs no confirmation); events 1101-1116, extended to 1101-1148 for a row within 1 SE of its edge (pooled, rule 3), confirmatory 1149-1164 for a lever-tuned F row (the levers are chosen on 1101-1148, the row is judged once on 1149-1164). The pre-flight probes ran on 7001-7008 (default runs) and 7101-7116 (events) and decide no row; two smoke runs of the sketch touched 3001 and 3002 (an identity residual and a NIIP path, no row, no band, no lever; they stay registered, S4-R36, and the registered runs repeat them). Development proofs of the implementer (I1, I2, I4, I7) use seeds 2001-2008 and 7001-7008: no row depends on them. 2101-2164 and 1033-1048 are spent; list B's sets (3201-3264, 3301-3364, 1201-1248, 2201-2232) are disjoint from these. (3) *E is re-run* on the 4-3 binary for every seed used; the stored `big_E2` is the non-world cross-check. (4) *Power.* Every 4-3 row is exact (E), deterministic (F by construction) or has a margin of at least 9 SE at 16 seeds (PREFLIGHT 6); no row needs more than the first seed set. The W5-04 rows of the tariff-war preset are at their edges (section 5 note) and are a stage-5 matter. (5) *Ports and CPU.* The 4-3 harness takes its own block of free local ports (S4-R38); at most 2 simulation processes, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, `python3 -B -I`; the default server port and the ports of other running services are never used; the cost row uses the interleaved protocol of S4-R20 (at least 12 rounds, the minimum per cell).
**Stage-5 protocol (rev 11, D55; pre-registered before any stage-5 run on a registered seed).** (1) *Cells and the ladder.* `L0`: the J4 default (no keys). `W'`: `{"world_block_enabled": false}` over the ABM keys of the profiles (`import_price_elasticity` 0.4, `import_elasticity_lr` 1.0, `fin_factor_rho` 0.83, `fin_factor_sigma` 0.30, `fin_jump_intensity` 0.06, `fin_jump_mean` 1.3, `fin_jump_sd` 0.5, `erp_fin_loading` 0.0028). `T`, `D`, `F`: the profiles `b8-world-s5a.json`, `-s5b.json`, `-s5c.json` (D58). A rung is judged against the cell below it as its matched cell (S2-R2: T against W', D against T, F against D) on the same seeds; the ABM-key effect (W' against `L0`) is reported; W5-01 and W5-02 compare T, D and F with `L0` (as written in the spec). If the dollar rung is `off`, the finance rung runs on `b8-world-s5c-nd.json` (T plus finance) against T. A **reported arm** `Tl` (`b8-world-s5a-lean.json`: T without the financial-factor keys) against `Wl` (`b8-world-wl.json`) runs on 6101-6132 for G1-G4, W1-31 and the shifts against `L0`; it decides nothing (D58, PREFLIGHT Q13). A rung that ends `off` stops the ladder above it when the rung above needs it (dollar and retaliation need trade; the finance rung needs nothing of dollar). (2) *Seeds (fresh for every link, the pre-flight note (not published) section 2).* Default runs (G1-G4, W2-15, W1-31, W4-29, W4-31, W4-32, the moments, W5-07): **6101-6164**, 20,000 households, 240 months; confirmatory **6165-6228** (the two cells of the failing rung only). Events: **1401-1416**; extension **1417-1448** for a row within 1 SE of an edge and for the rows that name 32 or 48 seeds (W4-26 and W4-14: 1401-1432; W2-06 and the `tarwar` preset: 1401-1448); confirmatory **1449-1464**. Pins: **2301-2316**, confirmatory **2317-2332**. Oil (D59): on-arm **10001-10800** at 1,000 households, legacy benchmark **20001-24000** at 50 households. Nothing else is ever added. Exploratory seeds 1-64 decide no row. (3) *Horizon, pairing, harness pins, confirmatory rule, power:* rules (1)-(7) of the stage-4 protocol and the count addition of 4-1b apply; every request carries `warmup_months` 0 and decides; the W5-04 presets are also read at the server default of 24 (reported, with the rule that a row failing only at 24 is flagged). (4) *Re-run:* every matched cell is re-run on the stage-5 binary for every seed used. (5) *Reading of the b7 set:* `tariff_check.py` gates T1-T9 in every cell with the trade link at 48 seeds; T8 (the dollar band) is read on the cell's reported `dollar_index`. (6) *G2 spike guard (S4-R32):* unchanged, decided on the pooled 128 seeds; the exact one-sided binomial probability of (link, matched) spike counts is printed beside it. (7) *Scorecard and IRF gates:* `stylized_facts.py` and `irf_check.py --stage final` read seeds fixed in their scripts (1-8; 1-N); they are exploratory by construction and are compared cell against `L0` with the same seeds. (8) *Ports and CPU:* a block of free local ports, at most 2 simulation processes, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1` (8 only in the short fingerprint runs and, alone, in the cost row), `python3 -B -I`; no process the script did not start is touched; the cost row (W5-05) runs alone in a window guaranteed by the orchestrator (PREFLIGHT section 9).

**Sub-round 5-T2 protocol (rev 12, D64; pre-registered before any 5-T2 run on a registered seed).** (1) *Cells.* `L0`, `W'` and the ladder cells of the stage-5 protocol with the profiles `b8-world-t2a.json` (T2), `-t2b.json` (D2), `-t2c.json` (F2); `T2` is judged against `W'`, `D2` against `T2`, `F2` against `D2`; the old cells `T`, `D`, `F` are not re-run (their rows were measured in stage 5 and decide nothing now). (2) *Seeds (D64; fresh for every link, the 5-T2 pre-flight note (not published) section 8).* Default runs **4401-4464**, 20,000 households, 240 months, for `L0`, `W'`, `T2` (and `D2`, `F2` if the rung runs); **4465-4528** run unconditionally for `T2` and `W'` (the G2 counts and the W2-13 / W4-32 trade prefix are decided on the pooled 128, S4-R32 and S5-c); every other G row of a rung is decided on its first set and pooled with the confirmatory set only when it lies within 1 SE of its bound (D41 rule 4). Events: **4601-4616**, extension **4617-4648** (W2-06 and the `tarwar` preset to 48; W4-26 and W4-14 to 32), confirmatory **4649-4664**. Pins: **4701-4716**, confirmatory **4717-4732**. Nothing else is ever added; exploratory seeds 1-64 decide no row. (3) *Step 0 (before any code is trusted).* The 3.0 setup on `212af62`; the fingerprints of I1 and the stage-5 profiles; **re-measure on seeds 1-12** with the 5-T2 build: g from `world_s5.py relprice` (cells `T` = `s5a` and `W'`; expected +0.277 %/yr, SE 0.058, i.e. g 0.0028; a value outside 0.0032 +- 2 SE of the 12-seed estimate, 0.0012, stops and asks) and the constants from `gap-offset` in the cell `t2a` with the key `world_import_gap_offset` removed (expected: the values of D64 within 2 SE each). (4) *Rows.* Rung T, D and F rows as in the stage-5 text with the names of the 5-T2 cells; new row W2-18 (R); W5-06 is an E row (bit identity); W2-15 keeps its band. (5) *No lever.* g, the constants and every band are fixed; a failed row is reported, not repaired. (6) *Horizon, pairing, harness pins, confirmatory rule, power, ports and CPU:* rules (1)-(7) of the stage-4 protocol and (6)-(8) of the stage-5 protocol; ports 9180-9189, at most 2 simulation processes, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, `python3 -B -I`.
