### Stage 1: blocs, offline solution, shadow coupling (`b8-world-s1`, base `M3`)

**Scope.** Step 0 (the `world_ius_offset` measurement) and everything of sections 2.1-2.3 and 2.2.6 except forcing channels that need later stages (they are zero): the 12 blocs, the network, the US-to-bloc inputs, user shocks `BlocPolicy`/`BlocDemand`/`GlobalDemand`, metrics `world` (first group), `expected_rate_path` and `CurveInputs`. Blocs are *driven by* the ABM; nothing flows back (shadow mode).

**Step 0 (before any code; setup as in section 1.9).** Measure `world_ius_offset` (D28): `for s in 1..12: RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1 $S/bin/base-econsim-cli run 20000 240 $s --json | tail -n 1 > $S/ius0/s$s.json` (3 at a time), then `python3 -B scripts/world/measure_ius_offset.py --files $S/ius0/s*.json --seeds 12`, which prints the mean over seeds and months 0-239 of `policy_rate - (r_star_est + pi_lr)` in pp. **Expected -0.40** (the joint2-S6 runs, 20,000 households: -0.400 for seeds 1-12, SE 0.091; -0.459 for seeds 1-32; sd across seeds 0.53) [verify@stage1]. Put the printed mean, rounded to 0.01, in `world_ius_offset`. If it differs from -0.40 by more than 0.2, stop and report. Record the value and the command in CALIBRATION.

**Files and functions.** `world/{mod.rs, params.rs, bloc.rs, net.rs, wmetrics.rs}`; `Config` keys `world_block_enabled`, `world_uae_in_opec`, `world_shock_scale`, `world_theta_mult`, `world_lam_rp_mult`, `world_kus_mult`, `world_oil_is_mult`, `world_rho_f/p/oil/qty`, `world_okun_ratio`, `world_ius_offset`, `world_opec_va_share`; hooks H1, H2, H4 (World field only), H5 (`expected_rate_path`, `CurveInputs`, visibility), H11, H12, H13, H14 (stage-1 variants), each followed by I1 and a WIP commit (section 1.4); `PHASES`/`profile.rs`. Functions: `WorldParams::load()`, `world::init_if_enabled`, `net::us_inputs(w) -> UsInputs`, `bloc::build_forcing(&BlocState, &UsInputs, &Net, &mut [[f64; K]; 4])`, `bloc::step(&mut BlocState, &Forcing, shocks) -> [f64; 5]` (includes the `tail[e] f_e[K-1]` terms), `bloc::pin_path`, `bloc::demand_scale`, `world::after_exog`/`world::step`/`world::settle` (stage-1 bodies: blocs and metrics only), and `econsim-cli world-dump` (writes the replay file of section 2.1). **Reference tooling** (the 18 tools of the placement table, section 1.1; copied into `scripts/world/`, run with `python3 -B -I`; it regenerates every golden from the committed defaults, with no hidden overrides). `world_ref.py`: K = 240 plus tail; P\* read from `pstar.json`; defaults rho_F 0.93, rho_P 0.95, rho_oil 0.98, rho_qty 0.97; M-F bound from the replay header; sub-commands `selftest` (monthly coefficients against the research, the band pairs of `targets.json`, the layout, the golden meta), `gates` (R rows print REPORT), `w109`, `kcheck`, `goldens --write` or `--check` (stage1, irf_unit, pf_ar1_EA, kref, inputs_clean, inputs_stress), `gen-inputs clean` or `stress`, `--inputs FILE` (replays a `world-dump` file and prints the max abs difference per variable), `centring`. `solve_blocs.py` calls `world_ref.solve_horizon(q, g_z, Kk=480, T=720)` for each bloc with the P\* table, stores `IR[:, :240]`, `tail = IR[240:].sum(0)` and the `pin` tables (`pin_eps(q, L, g_z, T=480)`), and writes `bloc_solution.json` (schema in 1.9); `build_world_params.py` writes `world_params.json`; `make_golden.py` runs `world_ref.py goldens --write`, `trade_hand.py --write` and (stage 3) the oil golden. Goldens and their file names: the placement table of section 1.1.

**Order of work.** (0) Step 0. (1) Python: in the repo layout, `world_ref.py selftest`, `gates` (every F and G row of the reference passes), `w109` (W1-09), `kcheck` (W1-32) and `goldens --check` (every golden reproduces; placement table verified). (2) Rust: loader and bloc step; unit tests 1-12. (3) Network, shocks, shadow wiring, metrics, `world-dump`; the remaining tests (the last, `cost_smoke`, is ignored). (4) Identity I1, I2, I4; cost. (5) Gates.

**Tests (+37, plus one ignored).** `params_embedded_json_parses`; `bloc_solution_shapes_and_finite`; `coefficients_match_params_monthly`; `autonomous_bloc_decays` (600 months, norm < 1e-6); `sequential_matches_perfect_foresight_ar1` (W1-09: EA with `world_theta_mult` 0, isolated, ius = 0.95^t realised and expected, against `pf_ar1_EA.json`, 1e-4); `forcing_horizon_truncation` (W1-32: `bloc_solution.json` IR and tail against `kref.json`; persistence 0.99 against K = 480 and K = 960, permanent addend against K = 960; <= 0.05); `own_hold12_matches_golden` (all 12 blocs of `stage1.json`: the 12 pin innovations `eps` to 1e-9; y, pi, i, z at months 0, 1, 3, 6, 9, 12, 15, 18, 24, 36, 48 to 1e-6; isolated mode); `own_hold12_bands` (W1-01..04); `monthly_irfs_match_golden` (W1-06: isolated unit-shock IRFs of EA, JP, UK, MX against `irf_unit.json`, 1e-6); `pin_tables_hold_rate_one` (each bloc, each L: |i-1| < 1e-8 for L months); `pin_scales_linearly`; `forcing_g_and_premium_paths`; `forcing_partner_term_and_dollar_addend` (an addend of 10 raises demand by `b4 w_iUS 10`-scaled amount; sign test); `nominal_rate_depreciates_with_higher_foreign_inflation`; `price_level_and_floor_reporting`; `us_inputs_okun_gap_and_deviation`; `y_us_ring_initialisation` (first three months: ring filled with `u_{t-1}`, then shifted; section 1.9); `reported_flag_and_base_capture` (D36: `reported(w)` false in burn-in, true at tick 0 for `burn_in_months` 0 and after the tick reset; `Bases` captured exactly once, at the first reported step); `ius_offset_shifts_inputs_and_expected_path` (`ius` and `ex[k]` shifted as in section 2.1; `ex[0] = ius_t` away from the floor; offset 0 reproduces the unshifted inputs); `expected_path_means_equal_curve_expected` (360-length slice, three branches; branches 1-2 only under the D33 fallback); `ius_bounded_by_rate_sat` (M-F on: `ius` and `ex` bounded in `fR`/`fS`, not in `G`; off: identity); `shock_draws_keyed_and_thread_independent` (RAYON 1 vs 4 in-process pools); `zero_scale_shocks_still_consume_draws`; `world_off_has_no_world_key_in_json`; `shadow_mode_leaves_abm_bit_identical` (tiny world, 24 months, all `Metrics` JSON minus `world`); `metrics_mean_includes_world`; `world_stream_ids_registered` (the four ids are in `ALL_STREAMS`, pairwise distinct in the low 20 bits, and the existing collision test passes); `bloc_demand_peak_equals_pct`; `shock_validation_rejects_bad_bloc_and_months` (and the ranges of section 1.9); `world_clone_does_not_alias`; `uae_flag_conserves_world_gdp` (both composition sets embedded; the flag is a runtime switch); `network_in_path_matches_golden` (replay `inputs_in2019_clean.jsonl` through the Rust bloc code with injected `UsInputs`; per-bloc paths against the stored `out` to 1e-6; the group statistics computed from `out` equal `stage1.json` `gates` for W1-10..W1-20 to 1e-6 and sit inside the bands); `replay_stress_parity` (W1-23, E: the same for `inputs_stress.jsonl`, which exercises every forcing channel with the M-F bound on); `oil_gap_channel_fsgm_troughs` (W1-24..26 with an injected oil gap); `china_demand_spillover_ratio` (W1-27); `gvar_lower_bound_report` (W1-28); `baseline_centring_smoke` (240 months, W1-31 on one seed, loose); `cost_smoke` (`#[ignore]`, not counted). Report the new total (the base count is read from `cargo test` at `M3`; the README shows 256 on 0242ab9) [verify@M3].

**Measurement.** (a) Reference simulation (Python, no ABM) with P\*: `world_ref.py gates` (W1-01..W1-20, W1-24..W1-28), `w109` (W1-09), `kcheck` (W1-32). (b) ABM shadow runs, `pol100` and `pol100l`, seeds 1-16, 10k households, 60 months: W1-21, W1-22 (foreign trough from `world.y_gap` of the shocked minus baseline arm, US trough from `gdp_real_bn_ar`); W1-23 parity: `econsim-cli world-dump [households] [months] [seed] [shock-json]` writes the replay file of section 2.1 (header + one line per month); `python3 -B world_ref.py --inputs FILE` replays it; max abs difference <= 1e-5. (c) I1, I2, I4, W1-29. (d) Default 240-month runs (32 seeds, 20000 hh) for W1-31 (mean of `world.y_gap` and `world.z` over months 12-239, with the measured `world_ius_offset`; **scored on the holdout seeds 13-32**), cross-checked by `world_ref.py centring --runs DIR --tag TAG --offset X --seeds 13-32` on the same runs (the reference approximates the expected path by `ius_t 0.97^k`, so it is a cross-check, not the gate). (e) `cost`: W1-30.

**Gates.** G: W1-21, W1-22, W1-27, W1-31. F (must pass; not evidence of validity): W1-01..W1-04, W1-10..W1-20, W1-24..W1-26. E: W1-06..W1-09, W1-23, W1-29, W1-30, W1-32. Reported: W1-05, W1-28. **Expected:** the "expected" column of section 4 (reference: AE5 trough -0.44, EME5 -0.53, CN -0.60, AE5 policy -0.94 at m24, floaters +0.64, z +5.5% / +4.2% peaks; W1-31 |z| <= 1.4 and |y| <= 0.14 with the offset, z -2.6..-5.3 without).

**Selection (F rows W1-10..W1-20 only if a row fails in (a)).** Grids: `theta_scale` {0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0} (AE; floaters-RoW-OPEC+ jointly), `lambda_rp` AE {1.0, 1.5, 2.0}, floaters {1.5, 2.0, 3.0}, `g_z` {0.15, 0.25, 0.35}, CN `uip_w` {0.70, 0.85, 1.0}, `world_rho_f/p` in [0.90, 0.97]. Structural changes (`g_z`, `uip_w`) need `solve_blocs.py` and a regenerated JSON.

**Stop rules.** S1-a: I1, I2 or I4 fails after 2 repair attempts: ship nothing. S1-b: W1-06/W1-09/W1-23/W1-32 (implementation parity) fail after 2 attempts: ship nothing. S1-c: an F row W1-10..W1-20 cannot be met inside the grid: ship the stage (it has no ABM effect), record the row in CALIBRATION as an open gap and continue; stage 4 re-evaluates with the risk channel. S1-d: W1-30 above 5%: profile first (`profile 5000 48`, total ms/month); only if the total is still above 5%, reduce K for the **S equation only**, recompute `tail[S]` as the sum from the new K to 479 (not the old 240..479 sum), and re-run `forcing_horizon_truncation`, W1-09 and W1-10 before stopping; K is never reduced for IS, PC or rule (D29). S1-e: W1-31 fails with the measured offset: ship the stage (no ABM effect), re-measure the offset once (step 0, 12 more seeds), and do not start stage 2 until the cause is understood (`z~` feeds stages 2-4).

**Docs.** `docs/DESIGN.md` new section 19 "World block" (state, tick order, hooks, shocks); `docs/CALIBRATION.md` append `## b8-world S1: blocs` (P\*, tables W1-*, golden); `docs/API.md`: `world` metrics keys and the three shocks; README: new feature line, test count.

**Commits.** (1) `world: bloc models (monthly GPM, offline RE solution, forcing paths with tail), network, shadow coupling, shocks and metrics; default output byte-identical` (2) `scripts: world solver, parameter builder, reference simulation, golden vectors, world_check.py and profile b8-world-s1` (3) `docs: world block S1 (design, calibration, API, README)`. Each hook (H1, H2, H4, H5, H11-H14) first gets its own WIP commit with I1 recorded; the WIP commits are squashed into commit (1) before the review.

### Stage 2: trade and prices (`b8-world-s2`, base: reviewed head of `b8-world-s1`)

**Scope.** Section 2.5 without retaliation: bilateral trade by sector, origin import prices with dominant-currency pass-through, import-share allocation across origins, partner-sector tariffs feeding b7 (`tau_eff_hh`, `tau_eff_firm`), exports on foreign income, the US-import-demand channel into the blocs, `Flows.imports_by_sector`, the tariff read sites (H8) and `st.import_rel` (H6). `common::world_price` is unchanged (D10).

**Files and functions.** `world/trade.rs`: `TradeState::new`, `TradeState::update(&mut self, w, &BlocOut) -> TradeOut` (computes `z~`, `Phi`, `r_j`, `omega`, `imp_rel[9]`, `imp_rel_cpi`, `tau_eff_hh`, `tau_eff_firm`, exports `[sector][bloc]`), `TradeState::settle(w)` (bilateral memo flows); hooks H6-H9 (each followed by I1 and a WIP commit); `Tariff` shock (`shocks.rs`; `ImportTariff` keeps its set-level semantics, D34); `Config` keys `world_link_trade`, `world_origin_*`, `world_dcp_*`, `world_export_*`, `world_us_import_channel`. Python: extend `build_world_params.py` (matrices) and `world_check.py` scenarios.

**Order.** (1) Params and `TradeState` with unit tests on hand values. (2) `imports_by_sector` and the hooks (`trade_active` false -> literal legacy expressions). (3) Link on: exports, import prices, tariffs. (4) b7 gates and ladder.

**Tests (+28).** `dcp_pass_through_hand_values` (h = 1, 3, 12, 24, 36 equal `trade_hand.pass_through(h)` to 1e-9: theta-bar 0.893, 0.220 / 0.379 / 0.469 at 3 / 12 / 24; origin substitution switched off in this test); `origin_shares_sum_to_one_and_equal_base_at_t0`; `origin_substitution_profile` (1.19 at 12 months, 1.93 at 60); `energy_origin_neutral`; `tariff_eff_uniform_equals_b7_scalar`; `tariff_eff_partner_weighted`; `tariff_eff_channels_use_own_cov` (household set excludes Food, firm set includes it); `import_tariff_level_is_set_not_added` (ImportTariff 10 then 0 leaves 0; bilateral components add); `tariff_read_sites_use_tau_for` (`firms.rs` and `metrics.rs` read the same scalar as `goods.rs` under a `Tariff{CN}` shock); `imports_by_sector_sum_equals_customs_imports` (both routings, tiny world: the sum equals `w.row.imports_this_month`); `exports_base_shares_equal_matrix` (W2-12); `exports_income_elasticity_analytic` (W2-09); `exports_price_and_fx_hand_values` (Dollar +10: -3.2 / -4.5 / -5.4 within 0.05 of `trade_hand.export_path`); `import_volume_hand_values` (master pair and the two fallback pairs within 0.05 of `trade_hand.import_volume_path`); `m_jk_orientation_and_column_sums` (`import_shares_2024` is [exporter][importer], importer columns sum to 1, `m_jk = M[k][j]`); `exports_use_trend_gdp_not_last_month_gdp` (a US GDP spike does not move exports); `import_price_numeraire_has_no_drift` (240 months, all z = 0 and c_t = 0: relative import price constant to 1e-9); `ztilde_common_component_is_abm_real_dollar` (all blocs at zero: `z~_j = c_t`); `bases_captured_at_last_burn_in_month` (bases at the end of burn-in; `z~_j - (z_j - zbar) = c_t` each reported month); `cpi_import_component_weights`; `trade_off_goods_identical` (link off: `clear` output equals base on a tiny world); `trade_link_ledger_consistent_24_months` (`check_consistency` each month); `us_import_gap_channel_clamped_and_lagged`; `bilateral_sums_equal_ledger` (W2-11); `terms_ratio_continuous_at_reported_month_0` (S2-R1, F6: a burn-in world with goods inflation 2%: `fx_base` and `trade_import_z` not overwritten; the import index at reported month 0 within 0.3% of the base-month level); `import_rel_hook_feeds_firms_and_metrics` (`st.import_rel` set under H6 reaches `firms.rs` and `metrics.rs`); `b7_uniform_tariff_gates_unchanged_with_link` (b7's own unit tests rerun with the link on); `dollar_shock_trade_path_monotone`. Report total. Fix round (S2-R1, S2-R5, F6): the link-on step tests (`trade_link_ledger_consistent_24_months`, `bilateral_sums_equal_ledger`, `import_rel_hook_feeds_firms_and_metrics`, `b7_uniform_tariff_gates_unchanged_with_link`) run on a world with a 12-month burn-in; new `link_first_month_is_continuous` (burn-in 24, goods inflation 4%, link on against off: |dX| and |dM| below 0.2% of GDP in months 0-2), `import_demand_benchmark_is_warm_up_mean_then_slow_ewma`, `deflator_is_post_bloc_pt_ratio` (F4).

**Measurement.** Cells, 10k households, 60 months, seeds 1-16: R (P\*), D1 (`world_dcp_kappa_usd` 1, `world_dcp_speed_usd` 1: full pass-through), D2 (`world_us_import_channel` false), D3 (import elasticities `(0.4, 1.0)` in the profile), D4 (`(0.7, 1.3)`). Scenarios `dollar10`, `dollarm10`, `tar10`, `tar25` (via `tariff_check.py` with the same overlay), `tarCN25`, `pol100`. The dollar scenarios start at reported month 0 (the month-1 workaround of the first measurement is removed: the bases are captured at the end of burn-in, D36). The 64-seed default runs of W2-13, W2-15 and W2-17 are paired against the matched link-off cell E (block on, link off, the same profile ABM parameters; S2-R2), the lever's own effect reported separately. 32 seeds 20000 x 240 default for W2-13 and W2-15 (trade link on, paired against world off: mean X/GDP and M/GDP over months 12-239; mean and trend of `z~_j - c_t` for W2-15, and of `c_t` itself, reported as W2-16). Single-month rows (W2-01, W2-02) use the mean of months h-2..h+2 (4-seed master readings at month 24 range from -1.6 to +0.6). Report-only: W2-05, W2-08, W2-10.

**Gates.** G: W2-01, W2-06, W2-07, W2-13, W2-15. F: W2-02, W2-03, W2-04, W2-09. E: W2-11, W2-12, W2-14, W2-17. W2-01 and W2-02 are judged on the realised real-dollar step (S2-R6). Reported: W2-05, W2-08, W2-10, W2-16 (`c_t`: master's own real dollar). **Expected:** hand values in section 4 (`scripts/world/trade_hand.py`: theta-bar 0.893, pass-through 0.220 / 0.379 / 0.469, import price -4.37% at 24 months, exports -3.2 / -4.5 / -5.4%).

**Selection.** (i) W2-01 (real imports, D13): move `(import_price_elasticity, import_elasticity_lr)` along `(0.2, 0.6) -> (0.4, 1.0) -> (0.7, 1.3)` **in the world profile** and stop at the first that passes W2-01 and keeps G1-G4 and the b7 gates. (ii) F rows W2-02/W2-03: `world_export_price_lr` {0.8, 0.9, 1.0}, `world_export_fx_lr` {0.2, 0.3, 0.4}, `world_dcp_kappa_usd` {0.40, 0.47}. (iii) W2-06 never tuned (b7 owns it): a failure means a bug in the `tau_eff_hh`/`tau_eff_firm` wiring. (iv) W2-15 is not tuned: a failure means a base or hook error (section 1.6, H6).

**Stop rules.** S2-a: I1/I2/I4 fail (2 attempts): ship nothing. S2-b: W2-06, W2-13 or W2-15 fail: ship `world_link_trade` off (profile `b8-world-s2.json` carries it on). S2-c: W2-01 or an F row W2-02 cannot be met inside the grid: ship off and record. S2-d: ledger inconsistency in debug: fix; if not fixable, remove the link (module stays).

**Docs.** DESIGN 19.x trade; CALIBRATION `## b8-world S2`; SCENARIOS limits ("tariffs: partner-sector duties enter b7 as one weighted scalar").

**Commits.** (1) `world/goods: bilateral trade by sector, dominant-currency import prices, partner-sector tariffs into b7, exports on foreign income; link off by default, defaults byte-identical` (2) `scripts: world_check trade scenarios and profile b8-world-s2` (3) `docs: world block S2`.

### Stage 3: oil market (`b8-world-s3`, base: reviewed head of s2)

**Scope.** Section 2.4: the market, OPEC+/shale/inventories, shock processes, exact-path shadow copy, interface to `exog.oil_price`, bloc oil gaps (already channels in stage 1), shocks `OilSupply`, `OpecCut`, `GlobalDemand` wiring to oil demand, `econsim-cli oil-check`.

**Files and functions.** `world/oil.rs`: `OilParams::from(&WorldParams, profile)`, `OilState::step(&OilParams, &OilShocks, &OilInputs) -> OilOut`, `OilShocks::draw(seed, tick)` (the prototype activity draw only in `oil-check`, D5), `oil::apply(w)` (called from `world::after_exog`: builds `OilInputs` from bloc `y`, `z` of month `t-1` and the ABM gap, steps copy A and (if live) U, writes `exog.oil_price`, `exog.oil_ar`, `world.diag`), `oil_active(w)`; hook H3; `Config` keys `world_link_oil`, `world_oil_*`, `world_opec_regime`; Python `scripts/world/oil_reference.py` (copy of the prototype) and golden `world/data/golden/oil_golden.json` (IRFs of the four shocks, 61 months; the 2008 collapse; Hormuz; OPEC cut; SPR); `econsim-cli oil-check` prints K1, K2a-d, K3, K4a-c, K5, K6, K7, K8 tables (K4: 1000 samples of 468 months, `rayon` over samples, each sample keyed by index).

**Tests (+25).** `oil_matches_python_prototype` (4 unit IRFs, 61 months, 1e-9; isolated market with the prototype activity process); `market_clearing_identity` (K1); `zero_shock_stays_zero`; `exact_path_hits_target`; `exact_path_drops_copy_u_after_decay` (drop at |shock| < 1e-4); `opec_rule_clip_decay_and_spare_cap`; `us_shale_two_stage_profile` (cumulative 0.06 / 0.14 / 0.22 / 0.28 / 0.37 / 0.43 at 6 / 12 / 18 / 24 / 36 / 60 months per 1% for eps_US_LR 0.45, +/-0.005); `demand_elasticity_aggregates` (0.100 within the month, 0.152 at 12, 0.300 long run: W3-12); `oil_bloc_elasticities_aggregate` (D12); `oil_bloc_beta_uses_cyclical_income_elasticity` (consumption-weighted mean 0.85); `bloc_balances_2024_sum_to_world` (0.1 mb/d); `uae_flag_oil_runtime_switch` (both composition sets conserve world totals); `oil_shocks_keyed_and_thread_independent`; `activity_drawn_only_in_oil_check` (the integrated run never draws the activity process; `oil-check` draws it on `WORLD_OIL_S`); `mean_offset_only_when_stochastic`; `oil_interface_price_formula`; `legacy_oil_draws_still_taken` (a world-on/oil-link-on run has identical risk-premium and TFP draws to world off: compare `rp_ar` and `tfp` paths); `news_shock_matches_golden` (K3); `oil_supply_shock_path` (Hormuz: peak +40% +/-5, back to baseline by month 13 +/-1); `opec_cut_mapping` (+5.8% at 6 months, +/-1); `oil_gap_to_blocs_uses_usd_price`; `qty_channel_opec_gdp`; `k6_replay_rmse` (golden STEO path, RMSE <= 12); `oil_active_off_leaves_exog_untouched`; `oil_levers_wired` (`world_oil_rho_q`, `world_oil_sigI_mult` change only the named parameter). Report total.

**Measurement.** (a) `oil-check` (isolated market; the prototype activity process on `WORLD_OIL_S`): W3-01..W3-06, W3-09, W3-12, W3-13, W3-16 and W3-07/W3-08 against the market alone. (b) ABM: `oil50` with `stochastic_oil=false` (K9), 12 seeds, 10k households, 36 months, oil link only: W3-10 (pass if |difference| <= 0.05 pp + 2 SE of the paired difference at each horizon; report the SE, and where 2 SE > |effect| the horizon is reported, not gated, because the test has no power there); `oil50` default stochastic, 16 seeds: W3-11 (O1-O3 from `irf_check.py`); 32 seeds x 20000 x 240 default: W3-14, W3-15 (`scripts/facts_shocks.py`; **expected `oil_lvl` 0.269, 0.001 below the floor 0.27: the first run is expected to miss and a D5 lever then fires, after which W3-15 is reported F for that run**), W3-17, W3-19; `opec2`, `hormuz` for W3-08 against the ABM; W3-18 (ABM Energy output vs market US demand). (c) I1, I2, I4, `cost`.

**Gates.** G: W3-07..W3-11, W3-14, W3-15. F: W3-01..W3-06, W3-12. E: W3-13. Reported: W3-16..W3-19. **Expected:** section 4.

**Selection.** Only `world_oil_profile` in `default`, `low_demand`, `high_demand`, `shale_discipline`, `shale_boom` (the F rows W3-01..W3-04 decide; W3-07 is a G row and is not a selection criterion; the `default` set passes 7/7, 6/7, 4/7 in the research prototype) and, for W3-15 `oil_lvl`, the pre-authorised levers of D5 in this order: `world_oil_rho_q` 0.995, `world_oil_sigI_mult` up to 1.1, both. Never edit a K-band.

**Stop rules.** S3-a: I-checks fail: ship nothing. S3-b: W3-01..W3-04 fail with every profile: ship `world_link_oil` off (a failing W3-07 is reported and decides nothing by itself). S3-c: withdrawn in rev 6 (S3-R2): the W3-11 failure was a horizon convention error, and the W3-10 h12 failure is the copy-A feedback, which is structural; W3-10 is gated with `world_oil_feedback` false. S3-d (re-decided in rev 6, S3-R3 on fresh seeds 1001-1400): W3-14 fails at the point estimate, so the link ships off (there is no lever: the offset 8.355 comes from the research). S3-e: W3-15 `oil_lvl` is below 0.27 after both levers (or any other W3-15 row fails): ship the link off and record the row.

**Docs.** DESIGN 19.x oil (processes, mapping table 1.8); SCENARIOS (oil rows annotated "exact path"); CALIBRATION `## b8-world S3`; `docs/API.md` shocks.

**Commits.** (1) `world: endogenous oil market (OPEC+, shale, inventories, Kilian/Kanzig shock processes), exact-path presets, oil shocks; link off by default` (2) `scripts: oil reference, golden vectors, oil-check, world_check oil scenarios, profile b8-world-s3` (3) `docs: world block S3`.

### Stage 4: dollar, financial links, official demand, retaliation, positions (`b8-world-s4`, base: reviewed head of s3)

**Pre-flight (rev 7; the stage 4 pre-flight note (not published)).** Step 0 of rev 6 is done: b7-transmission (6faf872) is an ancestor of `ccdcc48`; `fx.rs` and `monetary.rs` were re-read; sections 2.1 (branch 3), 2.6, 2.7 and 2.8 are rewritten against the merged code and the J4 defaults, the continuity rule is applied (1.6b) and the protocol is pre-registered (3.0). The stage is four sub-rounds (D42). Each is its own commit group, switch, gate subset and independent review; a later one starts from the reviewed head of the earlier one. A sub-round that fails a stop rule ships its link off and does not block the next.

| 4-1b | dollar redesign (list B) | `world_link_dollar` (same switch), `world_dollar_neutral`, `world_rp_sat_scale` | the forecast reference of the blocs' real exchange rate (2.6, 2.5, D47) and the bound on the total US-rate excess return (2.2.2, 2.6, D48); no new module | W4-28, W4-29, W4-32 re-decided; W4-01..W4-09, W4-15, W4-16, W4-26 re-scored; W4-35, W4-36 new | +14 |

W4-27 (cost) is measured in every sub-round on the cumulative switch set. 4-2 and 4-3 do not depend on the code of 4-1: the official block reads `ln D = ln(w.row.fx/fx_0)` and `w.exog.fin_factor`, and the positions read the ledger and the ABM's broad dollar (the dollar module's bilateral rates when that link is on, rev 10, D52); they depend on its reviewed head only for the shared files. Rev 8: 4-2 is built and measured with `world_link_dollar` off (S4-R15, D43).

**Step 0 of 4-1 (before any code).** (a) The 3.0 setup, freezing the base binaries from the starting head (`ccdcc48` with the block off, equal to J4 master); record the identity fingerprint `run 5000 60 3 --json | tail -n 1 | md5` at `RAYON_NUM_THREADS` 1 and 8: `9f90c88a2c8b653857029d235cd6b0a6` at `ccdcc48` (joint calibration 4 defaults, block off). (b) *Offsets (decision of the pre-flight).* `world_ius_offset` -0.43 and `world_yus_offset` -0.90 were measured on M3, before joint calibration 4. Measured on the J4 defaults (seeds 1-12, 20,000 households, 240 months, block off): ius -0.490 (SE 0.095; the stage-3 review: -0.490), yus -1.013 (SE 0.146; review -1.008), shifts of -0.06 and -0.11 pp, 0.6 and 0.75 SE. With the constants in use, W1-31 on the holdout seeds 13-32 passes on the J4 defaults (profile `b8-world-s1.json`: max |mean y| 0.202 against 0.5, max |mean z| 0.896 against 3.0). Step 0 repeats both measurements and the W1-31 run on the starting head. **The constants stay** unless a pre-registered trigger fires: (i) W1-31 fails on seeds 13-32, or (ii) a re-measured value differs from its constant by more than 2 SE (0.19 pp for ius, 0.29 pp for yus). If a trigger fires, the constants are changed in the `Config` defaults and in every `b8-world-s*.json` in one commit, reported to the orchestrator, and W1-31, W2-15, W2-13 and every stage-4 cell are re-run. The rows that depend on the constants are W1-31, W2-13, W2-15, the baseline path of the dollar (W4-31, W4-32) and, through the bloc gaps, W3-14/W3-15; paired rows (W1-21/22/27, every event cell) are unaffected by a constant. (c) *Base and J4.* Stage 4 builds on `ccdcc48`, the reviewed head of s3 (S3-R13), and measures on the J4 defaults, with matched pairs (L against E) on the same defaults; `legacy_j4off.json` is never merged under a stage-4 profile. Stage 1-3 numbers in CALIBRATION reproduce only with `legacy_j4off.json` underneath and are never compared across that boundary. (d) *Stage-3 record and test items carried by S3-R13, done before any stage-4 code (one commit, `records: stage-3 review items`):* D1 replace the circular OPEC+ quantity tests `splice_qty_kappa_cancels_in_matched_pair` and `splice_qty_month_zero_is_continuous` by the reviewer's independent-base test (`proposed_test_qty_independent_base.diff`: `q_b` read from the last burn-in month's `diag.qty_mkt`, `oil_ar` 0.9); D3 the retired key `world_oil_sigI_mult` gets `#[serde(alias = "world_oil_sigI_mult")]` on `world_oil_sig_i_mult` (server path; the CLI overlay keeps rejecting it) and a line in `docs/API.md`; D4 a server test that `validate_config` returns `ApiError::BadRequest` for an unknown `world_oil_profile`, `world_opec_regime` and shock-mask character; D6 one line at the head of the CALIBRATION S3 section, "superseded by Fix round and DECISIONS S3-R2..S3-R12"; D7 W5-03 and the "M3" text rebased on J4 (done in rev 7 for W5-03; the setup text of Stage 1 and 3.0 keeps M3 as history). (e) Run the shadow baseline probe of PREFLIGHT 3 (the cross-seed mean path of `ln D` against the ABM's `ln fx`, 24 seeds) with the committed code and compare with the pre-registered W4-31 reference.

#### Sub-round 4-1 (4a: dollar and risk factor)

**Files and functions.** `world/dollar.rs` (new): `DollarState { base: Option<DollarBase>, frisk: f64, f_prev: f64, fpol: f64, zeta: [f64; NB], .. }`, `DollarBase { ln_s: [f64; NB], ln_pt: f64, fx0: f64, frisk0: f64 }`, `DollarState::phase_a(&mut self, w: &mut World, us: &mut UsInputs, ..)`, `DollarState::phase_b(&mut self, w: &mut World, blocs: &[BlocState; (2) `capture_bases` and the shadow `DollarState` (Frisk update, phase A and B computed but not applied to `w.row.fx`): I4. (3) Hook H2 and the application of `fx`: I1 with the link off. (4) Injections and shocks. (5) Tests, profile, harness scenarios. (6) Identity (I1, I2, I4), cost, gates.

**Tests (+25).** `dollar_premium_matches_exchange_rate` (2.6: M-X off and on); `dollar_active_flag_and_exchange_rate_skip` (false in burn-in, true at reported ticks, false with the link or the block off; `exchange_rate` does not run when it holds); `dollar_base_captured_at_last_burn_in_month` (burn-in 12: `lnS_b`, `ln PT_b`, `fx_0` equal the values after that month's settlement; `ln D_0` equals the sum of the month-0 bloc changes; no capture in a later month); `burn_in_zero_world_dollar_base_at_first_step`; `dollar_first_month_is_continuous` (burn-in 24 world, 8 seeds: mean `|ln(fx_0/fx_b)|` below 2.5 %, none above 7 %; the matched link-off world's month-0 step printed); `dollar_pair_identical_before_shock` (1.6b; also through `compare` with `warmup_months` 12); `dollar_psi_zero_through_burn_in` (`fx_psi_state`, `fx_psi_component`, `fx_mp_prev` zero at the base; `qpsi_0 = 0.72 (psi_0 + z_0)`); the cross-seed mean of `Frisk_t - Frisk_b` over reported months 0-24 is within 1.5 VIX points of its mean over months 100-124; a zero-anchored start would be about 9 points lower in the first window); `frisk_asymmetry_and_decay` (0.588 on falls; decay `world_fx_risk_rho`); `fin_factor_moments_with_world_profile` (AR slope 0.83, P(rise >= 10 points) = 0.039 +/- 0.003 over 300,000 months, mean `F` 4.6 points); `policy_surprise_adds_13_points` (`PolicyShift` 100 bp: `f` +1.3 in the shock month only; `Fpol` +13 points, decays at 0.83; a second `PolicyShift` overwrites the slot and injects its own bps); `fpol_excluded_from_bloc_demand` (`UsInputs.fex = F - Fbar - Fpol`); `fex_term_in_is_equation` (`fis[k]` gains `-theta_f fex 0.83^k`; zero when `fex` is 0: output bit-identical to the merged code); `riskoff_event_path` (`RiskOff 14`: `F` +14 exactly, `Frisk` +14 or `14 + 0.412 |e_base|` when the baseline innovation is negative, 12-month ratio `world_fx_risk_rho^11`; W4-02 impact and 12-month rows inside the bands, 6-month row printed); `riskoff_injects_exactly_vix_points`; `dollar_bilateral_sum_to_broad` (weights sum 1, `fx = fx_0 exp(ln D)`, `ln D = sum wH10 lnS`); `dollar_addend_seen_by_blocs` (with `proj_on` and `dollar_proj` 0 the bloc output equals the link-off arithmetic to 1e-15; a unit `q` projected at 0.99^k reaches the Peff path; `UsHist.a` holds the realised total); `credibility_dollar_hand_value` (2.6: -3.3557 exact, -3.327 +/- 0.01), `credibility_profile_hold_then_halflife` (`p` is 1 for `months`, then 0.5^((age - months + 1)/3)) and `credibility_shock_sign` (dollar term only: dollar down; the 10y half is a 4-2 test); `yen_gamma_small_no_haven` (JP gamma -0.006: USD/JPY over 3 months after `RiskOff 14` inside [-3, +1] %, W4-03); `zeta_noise_ar1_sd` (per-group sd 10.6 % AE / 14.2 % EME unconditional over 5000 months; `WORLD_FX` draws taken when the sd is 0); `trade_ztilde_dollar_mode_zero_at_base` (all blocs at zero: `z~ = 100 a`, `zrel = 0` at the base; dominant-currency states start at 0); `fx_base_unchanged_by_dollar_link` (replaces rev 6 `fx_base_captured_when_dollar_active`, which belongs to the removed overwrite design: with the link on `monetary.fx_base` and `trade_import_z` are not touched and the terms ratio continues from burn-in); `world_off_and_links_off_identical` (all stage-4 switches off equals stage 3: world keys of the first 24 months and every non-world key bit-identical on a tiny world; the `UsInputs` replay of `inputs_stress.jsonl` is unchanged). Report the new total.

**Measurement.** Cells (cell names of 3.0): `W` (block off, stage-4 ABM keys), `E4a` (profile `b8-world-s4a.json` with `world_link_dollar` false), `L4a` (the profile as shipped for measurement). (a) 240-month default runs, 20000 households, seeds 1-64, `econsim-cli run 20000 240 S --json` at `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, at most the CPU budget of the dispatch: cells `E4a` and `L4a` (and `W`, 16 seeds, for the profile's own effect): W4-01, W4-05, W4-06, W4-29 (`continuity-dollar`, reading `world.fx_broad`), W4-31 (`dollar-path`, reading `world.fx_broad`), W4-32 (`joint_gates.py --stage4 RUNDIR L4a RUNDIR E4a --seeds 1-64` and `centring2` for the shares). (b) Event cells, 10,000 households, 60 months, seeds 1-16 (W4-26: 1-32), `world_check.py run` with the profile `b8-world-s4a.json` (paired inside `compare`): `riskoff14` (W4-02, W4-03), `uscred` (W4-04 dollar half), `pol100` and `pol100l` (W4-07, W4-08, W4-09, W4-26), `china3` (W4-15, W4-16), `ecb100` (W4-17, reported), `dollar10`, `dollarm10` (W4-19 reported; realised step of 2.6), `pin7` and `pin10` with `b8-world-s4a-mf.json` in dollar mode and, as the world-off reference cell, the same overlay with `world_block_enabled` false (W4-28; `transmission_irf.py` is the template, 36 months). (c) `identity` (I1, I2, I4) and `cost` (W4-27).

**Gates (4-1).** G: W4-03, W4-26, W4-28, W4-32. F: W4-01, W4-02 (impact, 12-month), W4-04 (dollar half), W4-05, W4-06, W4-07, W4-08, W4-09, W4-15, W4-16. E: W4-27, W4-29. Reported: W4-02 (6-month), W4-17, W4-19, W4-31. **Expected (pre-registered in section 4 and in the pre-flight note (not published) before any run).** W4-05 with the rev-7 profile: sd 2.17 %, correlation 0.36, P(VIX rise >= 10) 0.039, slope 1.66 % per +10 points (hand Monte Carlo; with the rev-6 intensity 0.035 the probability is 0.024 and the row fails); W4-02 impact 2.64 less a bloc feedback of about 0.1.

**Selection (4-1).** Levers by gate family, each family one lever group of the round budget (3.0; at most 2 rounds per group, 4 per sub-round): W4-01/W4-02/W4-05: `world_fx_risk_mult` {0.8, 1.0, 1.25}, `world_fx_risk_rho` {0.90, 0.93, 0.95}, `world_fx_rp_sd_mult` {0.25, 0.5, 1.0}, `fx_psi_sigma` {0.0215, 0.015, 0.010}; W4-06: `world_fx_idio_sd_ae/_eme/_cn/_opec` +/-0.5; W4-07/W4-08: `world_kus_mult`, `world_lam_rp_mult` {0.75, 1.0, 1.25} (re-checks W1-10..W1-20 in the reference); W4-09: `erp_fin_loading` {0.0028, 0.0014, 0}; W4-15: `world_china_risk_pts_per_pct` {3, 6, 9}, `world_theta_f_ratio` {0, 0.5, 1}; W4-28, W4-32, W4-29, W4-03: not tuned. The f-process keys of the profile (rev 7: intensity 0.06) are set before the first run and are not levers.

**Stop rules (4-1).** S4-a: any identity (I1, I2, I4, ledger, the pair identity test) fails after 2 repairs: ship nothing of the sub-round. S4-b: `erp_fin_loading` breaks W4-32 or the scorecard: ship it at 0 (W4-09 then fails and the equity channel is absent). S4-c: W4-01 or the impact or 12-month row of W4-02 cannot be met inside the lever grids: ship `world_link_dollar` off (bilateral rates then come from blocs plus the ABM dollar, stage 2 semantics); the 6-month row cannot trigger this rule (D35). S4-e: W4-27 above 5 %: profile and optimise before stopping. S4-f: W4-28 fails: ship `world_link_dollar` off in the b7-pins profile and record it. S4-g: W4-29 fails after 2 repairs (the cause is the base capture or the phase order), or W4-32 fails on 64 seeds and again in the confirmatory run: ship `world_link_dollar` off. S4-i: W4-31 alarm (the cross-seed mean of `ln D` over months 12-59 differs from the matched cell's `ln fx` by more than 15 points on 64 seeds): report to the orchestrator before any further step; it is not a stop by itself.

#### Sub-round 4-1b (list B: the dollar-link redesign, rev 9)

**Why.** The 4-1 link shipped off on two design gaps (S4-f, S4-g): the unbounded `lambda_rp gp` term of the bloc dollar equation (W4-28: 30.5 / 54.3 against [5, 22] / [8, 27]) and the slow unwinding of the blocs' base-month real exchange rate, which the link handed to the ABM as its baseline real dollar (W4-32: core +0.344, policy +0.537 over 128 seeds). List B changes only those two things; the bands, the rows and the horizons are unchanged. It is the last attempt: a failure of W4-28 or W4-32 leaves the dollar link off for good (S4-p, S4-q) and stage 5 records it.

**Files and functions.** `world/dollar.rs`: `Forecast { n0, z: Vec<[f64; NB]> }`, `FC_MONTHS` = 480, `DollarBase.fc: Option<Arc<Forecast>>`, `DollarState::set_forecast`, `DollarState::z_ref(n)` (the forecast, or the base value with the key off, held after the horizon), `DollarState.z_ref_now`, `phase_b(.., n)` reads `z_ref`; `world/mod.rs`: `WorldBlock::forecast(w, us_b, skip)` (2.6), its two call sites (the `base_month` branch of `settle`, computed before the trade borrow; `capture_bases(w, Some(&us))` for a world without burn-in), the `post_bloc_ref` call in `step_month`, the diagnostic `z_fc`; `world/trade.rs`: `post_bloc_ref(w, blocs, dollar, zref)`, `post_bloc` the `None` wrapper; `world/bloc.rs`: `bound_rp`, the `fS` branch; `world/params.rs`: `Par.rp_knee`, `Par.rp_sat` (zero unless the dollar link is on); `world/wmetrics.rs`: `z_fc: [f64; NB]` (`#[serde(default)]`); `config.rs`: `world_dollar_neutral`, `world_rp_sat_knee`, `world_rp_sat_scale` and their range check in `validate_world_links` (server 400, CLI exit 2); profiles `b8-world-s4d.json`, `b8-world-s4d-mf.json`, `legacy_s4a_dollar.json`; `scripts/world_check.py`: `continuity-dollar` with the W4-29 reading of D49, `pins` (W4-28 with `W` reference), `g4-floor` (the effect floor); `scripts/joint_gates.py --stage4`: the floor printed beside Z. `fx.rs`, `monetary.rs`, `finance.rs` are not edited.

**Tests (+14, `world/tests_listb.rs`) and the tests that change.** `bound_rp_hand_values`, `rp_bound_acts_on_the_total_return`, `rp_keys_are_validated`, `forecast_exists_only_with_the_link_and_neutral_on`, `forecast_reproduces_the_block`, `forecast_error_is_the_response_to_the_news_alone`, `forecast_is_shock_independent_and_shared`, `forecast_holds_its_last_value_and_decays`, `z_fc_metric_and_phase_b_read_the_forecast`, `first_month_forecast_error_is_innovation_sized`, `trade_zrel_reads_the_forecast`, `burn_in_zero_world_forecast_starts_at_the_first_month`, `step_month_passes_the_forecast_to_the_trade_module`, `legacy_keys_reproduce_the_4_1_link`. `rv_phase_a_b_against_an_independent_recomputation` reads `fc.z[t]` in place of `base.z` (its expectation for `ln S_j` is otherwise unchanged); every other 4-1 and 4-2 test passes unchanged. Report the new totals (core 503 + 14 = 517, 2 ignored). Hand-mutant self-check: the 11 mutations of the brief (section 5) must each fail at least one test.

**Identity.** I1 and I2 (block off), I4 (block on, links off; the new world key `z_fc` is the one expected extra), I5 unchanged, and **I6 (new): `world_dollar_neutral` false and `world_rp_sat_scale` 0 with the dollar link on equals the cc33f15 link-on output in every key but `z_fc`** (stored cells `L4a_s*`: 8 long seeds; `run 5000 60 3` link on, base md5 `a413d497e7a7cebfd37f4e529e99ed5b`, new md5 differs only by `z_fc`). Link on is deterministic at RAYON 1 and 8.

**Measurement.** Cells `E4d`, `L4d`, `W` (3.0 rule 1 of the 4-1b paragraph). (a) Default runs 3201-3264 (E4d, L4d): W4-29 (`continuity-dollar`, and the trade clause `continuity`), W4-31 (`dollar-path`), W4-32 (`joint_gates.py --stage4`, `centring2` for the shares), W4-01, W4-05, W4-06; `y_sd`, spikes and G4 as in 4-1. (b) Events 1201-1216 (W4-26: 1201-1232), 10,000 households, 60 months (`uscred` 24, `dollar10` 24): `riskoff14`, `uscred`, `pol100`, `pol100l`, `china3`, `ecb100`, `dollar10`, `dollarm10`, as in 4-1. (c) Pins 2201-2216, `pin7` and `pin10`, 36 months, L (`b8-world-s4d-mf.json`) against W (block off, same overlay). (d) The S4-b pair: `erp_fin_loading` 0 against 0.0028, L4d against E4d, seeds 3201-3216 (closes the rule left open in 4-1). (e) `identity`, `cost`, `hcheck`.

**Gates (4-1b).** G: W4-03, W4-26, W4-28, W4-32. F: W4-01, W4-02 (impact, 12-month), W4-04 (dollar half), W4-05, W4-06, W4-07, W4-08, W4-09, W4-15, W4-16. E: W4-27, W4-29, W4-35, W4-36. Reported: W4-02 (6-month), W4-17, W4-19, W4-31. **Expected (pre-registered here from exploratory seeds 1-64, 1-16 for events and pins; they are expectations, not bands).** W4-28: pin7 DOL12 13.8 (SE 2.6), pin10 19.5 (SE 2.6), |dTR| 0.39 / 0.03, |dUPK| 0.16 / 0.15 (block-off reference 11.5 / 14.0). W4-29: mean 1.49, seed maximum 4.10 (seed 33, matched cell 3.85), trade clause 0.021 / 0.050 pp. W4-31: +3.8 / +21.9 points. W4-32 (L4d against E4d): core +0.11 (SE 0.03), policy +0.15 (0.04), unemployment -0.06 (0.014), months at the zero bound -12 (2.2); G1 policy 2.48, u 4.72, core 2.05, y_sd 1.18; G2 policy < 1.0 in 2 seeds (E 2), none below 0.5, spike seeds 1-2 against the limit E + 1 (the guard is at risk: 1, 1, 2 over three variants of the design); G3 pass; G4 minimum Z -1.3 (u_ac1 d -0.002, Z -0.6); W2-15 dX/GDP +0.115, dM/GDP -0.162 pp (4-1 as shipped 0.366 / 0.350, FAIL). Moments: W4-01 1.67, W4-05 sd 2.03, corr 0.395, P 0.040, beta 2.21 (4-1: 2.48); W4-06 unchanged (all gated blocs inside). Events: W4-02 impact 2.58, 12-month 0.56; W4-03 -0.05; W4-04 -3.22; W4-09 -6.31; W4-15 -0.09 (fragile, edge -0.07); W4-16 1.14 (impact-month statistic of S4-R12); W4-26 P1 -0.90, P3 0.62, P5 0.246, P7 paired pass. **At risk, F rows:** W4-07 2.1 and W4-08 1.8 (floor 2.0, SE 0.3-0.5; 4-1 as shipped 3.62 / 2.93): both the forecast reference and the bound lower the dollar's month-24 response on the ABM's less hot baseline, and none of the pre-authorised levers moves them upward on the exploratory seeds (`world_lam_rp_mult` 0.75 / 1.25: 1.8 / 1.8 and 0.9 / 1.3; `world_kus_mult` 1.25: 2.0 / 1.8). They are F rows without a stop rule.

**Selection (4-1b).** The levers of 4-1 for F rows are unchanged. **Not levers:** `world_rp_sat_knee`, `world_rp_sat_scale`, `FC_MONTHS`, the forecast laws, `world_dollar_neutral`; W4-28, W4-32, W4-29, W4-03 are not tuned. At most 2 rounds per lever group, 4 for the sub-round.

**Stop rules (4-1b).** S4-n: any identity (I1, I2, I4, I5, I6, the pair identity, the ledger, `hcheck`) fails after 2 repairs: ship nothing of the sub-round (the link stays as in 4-1, off). S4-o: W4-28 fails on pins 2201-2216 and, when a statistic is within 1 SE of its bound, again on the pooled 32 seeds (confirmatory 2217-2232): **the dollar link stays off for good**, stage 5 records it. S4-p: W4-32 fails on 64 seeds (3201-3264) and again in the confirmatory run (pooled 128, 3301-3364): **the dollar link stays off for good**. S4-q: W4-29 fails after 2 repairs (the cause is the forecast or the base capture, not a lever): the link stays off. S4-e (W4-27 above 5 %) and S4-c (W4-01 or W4-02 impact / 12-month cannot be met inside the grids) as in 4-1; S4-b is evaluated with the pair of measurement (d). There is no list C: after S4-o or S4-p the cumulative prefixes of 4-2 and 4-3 stay on the ABM's own dollar.

#### Sub-round 4-2 (4b: official demand and term premium)

**Rev 8 scope (S4-R15).** 4-2 starts from the list-A head of `b8-world-s4a` (worktree `b8-world-s4b`) with `world_link_dollar` **off** (the 4-1 link ships off, S4-f and S4-g): the ABM's own dollar drives the reserve-manager rule, the policy-surprise, `RiskOff` and `BlocDemand{risk_off}` injections into `f_t` do not run, and the VIX term of the flow is measured on the ABM's own `f_t` only. The profile keeps `world_link_trade` true. The dollar-mode rows are deferred to the combined run after the dollar redesign (list B): the dollar-mode value of W4-12 (-60.3), the dollar-mode W4-13 and W4-10, the cumulative W4-32 prefix (dollar and finance on together) and the official purchases that follow a `RiskOff` (section 5, "Global risk-off"). Section 4 gives each row's link-off cell.

**Step 0 of 4-2 (before any code).** (a) The 3.0 setup on the list-A head (the head named in the dispatch): freeze the base binaries; the fingerprint `run 5000 60 3 --json | tail -n 1 | md5` is `9f90c88a2c8b653857029d235cd6b0a6` at `RAYON_NUM_THREADS` 1 and 8. (b) *Matched cell E4b = E4a.* The profile `b8-world-s4a.json` as shipped (dollar off) is the matched cell; with the finance switch off the head must reproduce the stored 4-1 reviewer cell `big_E2` (seeds 2001-2064): the md5 of the last JSON line of `run 20000 240 2001 --json` under `ECONSIM_CFG` of `E` is `abebde3f5d14f7ef0b9636784d2207c6` (checked on `b19a29c` and on the list-A head in the pre-flight); repeat for seeds 2001-2004 on the 4-2 binary after the no-op commit. (c) Seeds: nothing of 4-2 has been run on any seed; the sets are in 3.0 (rev 8).

**Files and functions.** `world/finance.rs` (official part; the 4-1 factor code stays): `FinState`, `FinBase`, `OfficialProg`, `TpCred`, `FinState::new/capture/tp_fo/tp_cred`, `finance::register_shocks(w, fs)` (called in `world::after_exog` while `world_link_finance` and the month is reported, before the curve), `finance::step_flows(w, fs, fx0, rep)` (called in `world::step` after `step_month`, every month: the F ring always, the flows in reported months), `finance::tp_finance(w, j)` and `finance::row_extra_deposits(w)` (read by the two `#[inline(never)]` wrappers `world_tp_fo` and `world_row_extra` of `world/mod.rs`, which return `None` unless `finance_active(w)`); `world/mod.rs` (`WorldBlock.fin: Option<FinState>` created in `WorldBlock::new` when `world_link_finance`; `FinState::capture` in the `base_month` branch of `settle` **and** in `capture_bases`; the `settle` diagnostics); hook H5 in `monetary.rs`: in `update_curve`, after the `let tp = ...;` statement (`monetary.rs:563-567`) and before `w.curve.expected[j] = exp;`, `let tp = if let Some(fo) = crate::world::world_tp_fo(w, j) { tp + fo } else { tp };`; in `rebalance_row` (`:936`) the book adjustment of D45; `pub(crate)` on `TP_SLOPE` (`:19`), `trade_with` (`:861`), `trade_with_banks` (`:892`), `rebalance_row` (`:936`); `shocks.rs` (`OfficialTreasury { bloc: Option<String>, bn: f64, months: u32 }`, `validate`, routing to `world_shocks`); `world/wmetrics.rs` (ten new `#[serde(default)]` keys: `fo_bn`, `fo_flow_bn`, `tp_fo_bp`, `tp_cred_bp`, `fo_extra`, `f_ring_mean`, `row_mv_gdp_pct`, `row_dep_gdp_pct`, `fo_share_row_pct`, `row_face_share_pct`; `f_vix` is not touched); `Config` keys `world_link_finance`, `world_fo_theta_stock`, `world_fo_theta_trans`, `world_fo_trans_rho`, `world_fo_dollar_resp`, `world_fo_vix_resp`, `world_fo_trend_cn` (defaults in 1.5); `scripts/world/build_world_params.py` and `world/params.rs` (`finance.tic_ust_bn[12]`, `finance.official_share[12]`, `finance.foreign_official_ust_bn`, bloc order of `H10_KEYS`; the calibration hash does not change); `scripts/world_check.py` (ports 9140-9149 in `in_block`; scenarios `fo100`, `fo400`, `qe05`, `qe1`; subcommands `fo-events`, `fo-moments`, `w410`), profiles `b8-world-s4b.json` (= `b8-world-s4a.json` plus `world_link_finance` true) and `b8-world-s4b-trend0.json` (plus `world_fo_trend_cn` 0, the S4-h lever). `sim.rs` is not touched: `world::step` is one phase.

**Tests (+22, `world/tests_finance.rs`).** Worlds: `sim::new_world` with `burn_in_months` 12, 300-400 households and the profile's f keys (`fin_factor_rho` 0.83, `fin_factor_sigma` 0.30, jumps 0.06 / 1.3 / 0.5, `erp_fin_loading` 0.0028; the 4-1 review found that a config without them makes `f_t` identically zero and lets mutants of the centring survive); `quiet(cfg)` sets `world_fo_trend_cn`, `world_fo_dollar_resp` and `world_fo_vix_resp` to 0 so that only the test's shock moves the tranche. Every expectation is computed in the test from the spec formulas and from states the code does not compute in the same step.

1. `official_flow_monthly_rule_hand_values` (trend and VIX off; `step_flows` called directly with `w.row.fx` raised by `ln 1.10`: -82.71 +/- 3; a gradual path: the six-month sum within 2 % of `eta x 6 x 100 ln D_6 x FO^rw`).
2. `fo_rule_flows_at_realised_step` (link off, `Dollar +10 %`, hand series from `ln D_h = 9.531 (1 - 0.7^h)`: impact -24.81 +/- 2, cumulative through h = 6 -73 +/- 5; the measured cell value -72.2 is W4-12).
3. `official_execute_is_ledger_consistent` (24 months with the trend, a +100 and a -400 programme; `ledger::check_consistency` every month; `extra` equals minus the sum of executed purchases at market value to 1e-9 relative; RoW's face change equals the executed trades plus what the private book did).
4. `tp_official_100bn_hand_values` (quiet world, `OfficialTreasury{CN, +100, 1}`: -10.14 +/- 0.5 bp in the first month after the flow, -7.25 +/- 0.5 thirteen months after, between -4.0 and -4.9 at 40 months; **two worlds whose RoW holdings differ by more than 10 % of GDP at the base give the same bp to 1e-6**).
5. `official_tp_uses_real_world_units` (move bonds from the Fund to RoW before the base so that RoW's share is 1.5 times larger: the same bp to 1e-9; `k_FO` is the only thing that changes).
6. `fo_base_captured_and_normalised` (official share of RoW holdings 40.80 % +/- 0.01; `FO^rw_b` 3773.1 +/- 1e-6; `H_j` in $bn rw: 194.4 / 585.1 / 660.0 / 149.2 / 42.5 / 75.7 / 191.8 / 111.4 / 159.0 / 20.1 / 279.8 / 1304.0 within 0.06; `nu` 0.99652; the base is the end of the last burn-in month: `tau_b` equals `trend_gdp_month` after that month's settlement, it is not re-captured after three reported months, and a shock does not capture it).
7. `fo_first_month_is_continuous` (at reported month 0: `tp_FO` equals 0.0 bit for bit; `stock` and `T` are 0.0; `FO^rw` equals `3773.1 tau/tau_b`; the month-0 flow equals the rule's value computed in the test from the realised `ln D_0` and the ring - the rule has no month-0 exception, `ln D_0` is the ABM's own step).
8. `fo_stock_ratio_constant_in_baseline` (quiet world, 120 months: `tp_FO` equals 0.0 every month and `FO^rw` equals `3773.1 tau_t/tau_b` to 1e-9; trend only (-80): the 10y `tp_FO` equals the test's own recursion on the realised `trend_gdp_month` path to 1e-9, capped continuation included).
9. `vix_ring_filled_in_shadow` (after the burn-in `ring[5]` equals `F - Fbar` of the last burn-in month computed from the world's own `fin_factor`, and the six entries are the last six months; the ring includes the current month at a reported step).
10. `fo_vix_term_hand_values` (a forced `fin_factor` of +1.4 units, i.e. 14 points over `Fbar`, for six months: the flow is `1.05 x 14/6` in the first month, `1.05 x 14 x k/6` in month k, and falls out of the window after the sixth; pro rata to `H_j`).
11. `qe_and_official_terms_add` (D20: `QE` 0.5 % a month for 12 months with the finance link on and off in tiny worlds: the explicit term's own response `abs(d tp_FO)` <= 0.5 bp; `private_duration` is unchanged by an official trade (1e-12 relative); QE does not change any `dH_j`).
12. `rebalance_row_hook_keeps_extra_deposits` (after an executed sale: inside the band no bond is traded (faces equal); with the adjusted deposits below zero the private book sells `target' - dep_adj` to the banks, `target' = (bonds + x)/12 + 0.5 gdp`, while the raw deposits are positive; the rev-7 form, a shifted target only, trades nothing there and fails the test).
13. `tranche_clipped_to_row_holdings` (`OfficialTreasury{CN, -2000, 1}` with the CN tranche at 585: 585 executes, `H_CN` ends at 0 to 1e-9, never negative; with RoW's face cut below the request the executed share is the share RoW can deliver).
14. `credibility_tp_part` (finance link only: the 10y +22.0 bp to 1e-9 at ages 0-2, `22 x 0.5^((age-2)/3)` at ages 3 and 4 (17.46, 13.86), the 2y +4.4, the 3m 0; identical with the dollar link also on).
15. `official_treasury_shock_path` (`{CN, +100, 1}`: 100.0 +/- 1e-6 in the shock month, 0 after; `{null, -400, 6}`: -66.67 a month pro rata to `H_j,t-1`; `validate` rejects bloc "XX", `abs(bn)` > 2000, months 0 and 61; ignored with the link off).
16. `official_purchase_executes_in_full_when_banks_hold_no_bonds` (all bank holdings moved to the Fund: a +100 purchase executes 100.0 +/- 1e-6; the bank-only counterparty list of rev 7 executes 5-45 % in such worlds and fails).
17. `finance_pair_identical_before_shock` (spec 1.6b: two worlds, the shock at month `T0 >= 1` in one; every finance output and state and every ABM key bit-identical for months before `T0`; at `T0` the difference equals the analytic term (flow 100.0 or `tp` 22.0 bp) to 1e-9; the same through `sim::compare` with `warmup_months` 12).
18. `finance_on_zero_drivers_equals_off` (I5 at tiny scale: 24 months, every non-world key identical; `tp_FO` and `extra` are 0.0).
19. `cn_tranche_exhaustion_cap` (default trend, 3 seeds, 140 months in tiny worlds: `H_CN` >= -1e-9 every month; its flows stop after exhaustion; the exhaustion month lies between 60 and 140).
20. `rule_reads_the_dollar_link_index_when_on` (both links on, 12 months: the `ln D` the rule uses is `ln(w.row.fx / fx_0)` of the link's index, not of `exchange_rate`).
21. `finance_hooks_inert_when_off` (block off, or link off: `world_tp_fo` and `world_row_extra` return `None`; a 24-month world has the same curve and the same `Metrics` JSON as the same world built before the 4-2 code, new world keys aside).
22. `finance_metrics_keys` (the ten new `world` keys are present, finite, and zero or the base values with the link off).
Report the new core total (473 core with 2 ignored, 18 server, 2 cli at the list-A head, README).

**Measurement.** Cells (cell names of 3.0): `E4b` (the profile `b8-world-s4a.json`, dollar off, finance off) and `L4b` (`b8-world-s4b.json`); `L4b0` (S4-h) only if its stop rule fires. (a) 240-month default runs, 20000 households, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, at most 2 processes: seeds 2001-2064 for both cells (E4b is re-run on the 4-2 binary; the stored `big_E2` is the cross-check), then 2101-2164 for both when rule (4) of 3.0 calls for it: W4-32 (4-2 prefix: `joint_gates.py --stage4 RUNDIR L4b RUNDIR E4b --seeds 2001-2064`, `world_check.py centring2` for X/GDP and M/GDP), W4-13 and the baseline path of `tp_fo_bp` (`fo-moments`), W4-20 reported moments. (b) Event cells, 10,000 households, `warmup_months` 0, `world_check.py run` with the profile and `--base-port 9140`: seeds 1001-1016 (rule (4): fresh 1017-1048): `fo100` (W4-11, 60 months), `fo400` (the preset row; 60 months; also with `world_fo_trend_cn` 0 as the mechanism cross-check), `dollar10` (W4-12; 24 months), `uscred` (W4-04 10y half; 24 months), `pol100` seeds 1001-1032 (W4-10 reported; 36 months); `qe05` on **32 seeds** 1001-1032 in both cells (W4-14, 36 months; pooled with 1033-1048 under rule (4)). (c) `identity` (I1, I2, I4, I5), `cost` (W4-27), `hcheck 4 20000 240` with the finance-on profile.

**Gates (4-2).** G: W4-14, W4-32 (4-2 prefix). F: W4-04 (10y half), W4-11, W4-12. E: W4-20, W4-27, W4-33, W4-34. Reported: W4-10, W4-13. W4-32 is reported F for a run on which the S4-h lever fires (the D13/D5 form of D30). **Expected (pre-registered in the pre-flight note (not published) before any 4-2 run):** W4-04 10y +22.00 bp at h = 1..3; W4-11 -7.95 bp (mean h = 1..12) and -4.30 bp (h = 49..60); W4-12 -72.98 (impact -24.81; cell probe -72.2); W4-14 Q1 about -0.37 pp, `R(L) - R(E)` +0.3 bp (SE 0.9 at 32 seeds), the explicit term's own response -0.13 bp; W4-13 between 15 and 30 $bn; W4-20 30.0 % and 40.80 %; W4-32 against the E4b values on 2001-2064 (policy 2.448, unemployment 4.781, core PCE 2.035, `y_sd` 1.128 (**0.028 above the lower edge of its band: a first-run miss on `y_sd` is possible and is decided by rule (4)**), corporate share 10.198, policy below 1.0 in two seeds, below 0.5 in none).

**Selection (4-2).** One lever group of the round budget each: `world_fo_trend_cn` {-80, 0} (S4-h); `world_fo_vix_resp` {1.05, 0.5, 0} (used once, with the trend at 0, if the S4-h re-run fails; the VIX term as the level mean is [assumed], D44); W4-11, W4-12 and W4-04: none (sourced, by construction).

**Stop rules (4-2).** S4-a as above (also I5, the pair identity, the ledger and `hcheck`). S4-e as above (W4-27 above 5 %). S4-h: W4-32 (4-2 prefix) fails on the pooled estimate with `world_fo_trend_cn` -80: re-run once with 0 (the baseline term premium otherwise settles at +19 bp after month 100); if it passes, ship 0 and record the drift as a limitation; if it fails again, one further round with `world_fo_vix_resp` 0.5; if that fails, ship `world_link_finance` off. S4-j: W4-20 (E) fails after 2 repairs: ship off. S4-l: W4-14 fails on 32 seeds and again on the pooled 48 (Q1 outside [-0.48, -0.18] pp, or `abs(R(L) - R(E))` above 2 bp): ship `world_link_finance` off. S4-m: W4-04 (10y half), W4-11 or W4-12 fails after 2 repairs (they are by construction: a miss is a wiring or timing defect), or W4-33 or W4-34 fails after 2 repairs: ship off.

Each hook gets its WIP commit with I1 first (1.4), squashed before review.

#### Sub-round 4-3 (4c retaliation, 4d positions)

**Rev 10 scope (S4-R24).** 4-3 starts from `4240b34` (the `cc33f15` code, the list-A' head of `b8-world-s4b`, plus list B's revision-9 spec only; worktree `b8-world-s4c`, spec rev 10 committed on top); the code of 4-1b (list B) is merged by hand later (S4-R27) with `world_link_dollar` and `world_link_finance` **off**; profile `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s4c.json` = `b8-world-s4a.json` plus `world_retaliation` true and `world_positions` true. E: W4-21, W4-23, W4-27, W4-32 (4-3 prefix), W4-37, W4-38. Reported: W4-24b, W4-25 (a), the niip and current-account moments of W4-21. **Expected (pre-registered in section 4 and in the pre-flight note (not published) before any 4-3 run):** W4-24 exports -2.390 (hand; probe -2.428, SE 0.003) in [-5, -2]; imports -1.2 (probe -1.200, SE 0.042; the share-only hand value is -1.02 and the b7 aggregate adds -0.16) in [-1.7, -0.8]; W4-24b goods exports to China -28.58 % (probe -28.96, data -26), imports under +29 points -26.0 % (probe -25.2, data -30); W4-25 (a) the ABM's own dollar +0.05 % at month 3 and +0.77 % at month 12 under `tarwar` (bound 8); W4-22 -8.662 %; W4-23 residual 4e-14.

**Selection (4-3).** One lever group, at most 2 rounds (S4-k): `world_ret_eps_lr` {2.0, 3.0, 4.0} and `world_ret_speed` {0.05, 0.10, 0.20} for W4-24 (exports). The hand exponent at 12 updates is `1 + (eps_lr - 1)(1 - (1 - speed)^12)`: 1.46 / 1.92 / 2.38 (speed 0.05), 1.72 / **2.44** / 3.15 (0.10), 1.93 / 2.86 / 3.79 (0.20) for `eps_lr` 2 / 3 / 4, and the band needs 2.0 or more (the probe adds -0.04): the pre-registered point passes with a margin of 0.43 on the hand value, so S4-k can fire only through a wiring defect. W4-24 (imports), W4-21, W4-22, W4-23: no lever. The levers are chosen on seeds 1101-1148 and the row is judged once on 1149-1164.

**Stop rules (4-3).** S4-a as above (also I7, the retaliation and positions pair identities, the ledger and `hcheck`): ship nothing of the sub-round. S4-e as above (W4-27 above 5 %). S4-k: W4-24 (exports) not reachable inside the two grids after 2 rounds: ship `world_retaliation` off. **S4-r** (new): W4-23 (a residual above 1e-9 of monthly GDP in any month of any cell), W4-21 or W4-22 fails after 2 repairs: ship `world_positions` off. **S4-s** (new): W4-37 (retaliation neutrality, zero base state, pair identity) fails after 2 repairs: ship `world_retaliation` off; W4-38 (positions base, pair identity, hook inertness) fails after 2 repairs: ship `world_positions` off. A stop rule ships the named switch off with its best parameters in `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s4c.json`; the sub-round is still reported; one switch failing does not block the other.

### Stage 5: validation and the default decision (`b8-world-s5`, base: the reviewed head of the merged stage 4, `b8-world-s4e` @ `d5aa81d`)

**Scope (rev 11, D55).** Stage 5 decides each world link on its own rows (the ladder of 3.0), ships the links that pass as profiles, names the default set for the joint switch of D61 and hands the site what it can use (the website's source folder brief). It writes no model code except the twelve centring constants of D57, the `Tariff` check of D60 and the harness; it does not change `Config::default` (D61). The oil link is measured only through the early gates of D59.

**Work.** (0) *Step 0:* the 3.0 setup on `d5aa81d`; fingerprints (I1 and the nine recorded profile md5s); re-measure the D57 constants on seeds 1-12. (1) *Code:* `world_import_gap_offset` (config key, validation, one subtraction in `TradeState::pre_bloc`, 3 tests); `Tariff` needs the block and the trade link on `/api/compare` (server, 1 test); the profiles of D58 (+1 cli test); the harness: `scripts/world_runs.py` (long runs and event runs), `scripts/world_s5.py` (`gap-offset`, `shifts`, `w504`, `w407`, `w210`, `tariff-eq`, `binom`, `fo-drift`), `scripts/world_events.py` (S4-R45), the `Tariff` and bloc-policy scenario codes of `world_check.py`, the exact binomial print in `joint_gates.py --stage4`. (2) *Identities* I1, I2, I4, I5, I6, I7, I8 on the new binary. (3) *Rung T, then D, then F* (rows below), each rung's G rows decided by the confirmatory rule; a failed rung ships its link off (S5-c, S5-d). (4) *Cumulative rows* W5-01 to W5-03 for T, D and F against `L0`; W5-04 presets; W5-06 to W5-08 (reported and E rows); the lean arm `Tl` / `Wl` and the finance-only cell `Fn` (reported). (5) *Cost* W5-05 in a quiet window (PREFLIGHT 9). (6) *Oil early gates* (D59). (7) *Sensitivities, reported:* `world_fo_trend_cn` 0 on the finance rung (32 default seeds); `world_oil_profile` `supply_heavy` and `world_uae_in_opec` false only if the oil link passes its early gates; projection persistences 0.90 / 0.97 on W1-31 (reported). (8) *Docs and web:* SCENARIOS rows for the cards of section 5, CALIBRATION `## b8-world S5`, API.md (`Tariff` 400, the profile keys, the constants), README, DESIGN 19.7. The web brief (the website brief (not published)) is a separate deliverable. (9) *Merge proof* (W5-09) with the web line in a scratch clone. (10) The **record** and the **decision**: each link's class (ship / profile / off), the default set, and the questions left for the joint switch.

**Rung T gates (cell T against W'; trade, retaliation, positions).** *E:* I1, I2, I4, I7 (T against `b8-world-s4a.json` carrying the same constants: every non-world key bit-identical on every record, so the retaliation and positions switches add nothing), I8 on the D rung, determinism at RAYON 1 and 8, `hcheck`, ledger and `world::check_identities` in debug; W2-11, W2-12, W2-17, W4-23, W4-34 (n/a), W4-37, W4-38. *G:* W1-31 v2; W2-06 (T1-T9 at 48 seeds, `tariff_check.py`); W2-07; W2-13 / W4-32 (trade prefix: G1-G4, the G2 counts and paired spike guard, W2-15 dX/GDP and dM/GDP within 0.3 pp); Tier A TA1-TA7. *F:* W2-01 (an F row, the D13 lever is in the profile), W2-02, W2-03, W2-04, W2-09, W4-22, W4-24. *R:* W2-05, W2-08, W2-10 (restated), W2-16, W4-21, W4-24b, W4-25 (a), W5-06. The presets of section 5 that need only the trade link (ECB, BoJ, PBoC, US-China tariff war, tariff war with retaliation, machinery and electronics) are read in cell T.
**Rung D gates (cell D against T; dollar with the M-F keys).** *E:* I6, I8, W4-29 (both clauses and the D49 reading), W4-35, W4-36, W4-38 on the dollar branch (positions rows in dollar mode), the pair identities. *G:* W4-32 (4-1b prefix: D against T, G4 with the S4-R26 effect floor, W2-15 dX and dM only), W4-03, W4-26 (P1-P5 on the seed-mean IRF, P7 paired against T), W4-28 (pins 2301-2316, confirmatory 2317-2332), W2-06 repeated in cell D (every T-row, T8 read on the dollar link's `dollar_index`). *F:* W4-01, W4-02, W4-04 (dollar half), W4-05, W4-06, W4-07 and W4-08 (v2, pins), W4-09, W4-15, W4-16. *R:* W4-17, W4-19, W4-25 (b), W4-31, W4-39, the cumulative moments, W5-07. Presets: China slowdown, global risk-off (dollar rows), tariff war (W4-25 b).
**Rung F gates (cell F against D; finance).** *E:* I5, W4-20, W4-33, W4-34. *G:* W4-14 (32 seeds 1401-1432, pooled with the extension by rule), W4-32 (cumulative prefix: F against D, seeds 6101-6164 and pooled 6165-6228 by rule). *F:* W4-04 (10y half), W4-11, W4-12 (both the trade-only value -72.98 and the dollar-mode value -60.3). *R:* W4-10 (S4-R21 form), W4-13, the `tp_fo_bp` path, W5-07. Presets: reserve-manager sell-off. The sensitivity cell `trend 0` is reported.
**Cumulative rows.** *W5-01 (G):* G1-G4 and Tier A for T, D and F against `L0` and against the matched cell. *W5-02 (G):* G5 entry/exit, G6 scorecard severity change <= +2 against `L0` (T; D and F are reported and carry their own class), G7 `irf_check.py --stage final`, G8 `hcheck` 0 failures. *W5-03 (G):* determinism and the fingerprint table (brief section 7). *W5-04 (G):* presets (section 5) by their "Needs". *W5-05 (G):* cost (PREFLIGHT 9).

**Class rule.** A link is **off** when an E row of its rung fails after the allowed repair, or a G row fails after the confirmatory rule by 2 SE or more of the pooled estimate; **profile** when its rows pass but D55 names it a profile (dollar, finance), or when a G row misses by less than 2 SE of the pooled estimate (the near-miss rule: such a miss is rejected by no conventional test at the seed count the rule allows, and the cost of calling it a fail is the loss of the feature, while the cost of calling it a profile is only that the default-set proposal is withheld); **ship** otherwise. The near-miss rule does not apply to E rows or to counts. The default set {block, trade, retaliation, positions} is proposed for the joint switch only if rung T passes, W5-01 and W5-02 pass for T, and W5-03 and W5-05 hold. F rows that fail are recorded and decide nothing by themselves (D30), except that a failed F row of the dollar rung withdraws the dollar presets (S5-f).

**Tests (+11).** `import_gap_offset_empty_is_identity`, `import_gap_offset_subtracts_after_the_clamp`, `config_validation_import_gap_offset` (core, 3); `tariff_without_trade_link_is_400` (server, 1); `stage5_profiles_load_and_validate` (cli, 1); `profiles_threads_equal` (the three profiles, md5 at RAYON 1 and 8), `legacy_b8off_reproduces_the_default_fingerprint`, `presets_parse_as_shocks`, `docs_examples_run`, `web_build` (npm), `preset_bands_smoke` (tiny) (6, as before; the first two replace `world_on_default_threads_equal` and `legacy_profile_equals_world_off`).

**Stop rules (stage 5).** **S5-a:** any identity (I1, I2, I4, I5, I6, I7, I8, the pair identities, the ledger, `hcheck`) fails after 2 repairs: the ladder stops at that rung; the rungs below it stand. **S5-b:** W1-31 v2 fails in cell T with the D57 constants: the trade link ships on `b8-world-s5a-nc.json` (`world_us_import_channel` false) if W1-31 and every other rung-T row pass there on the same seeds (run on demand), and the presets that read a partner's output gap are withdrawn; otherwise S5-c. **S5-c:** W2-06 fails in cell T after the confirmatory rule (pooled 1401-1448 + 1449-1464), or W2-13 / W4-32 (trade prefix) fails on the pooled 128, by 2 SE or more: the trade link ships off (S2-b stands) and so does everything that needs it; by less than 2 SE: `profile` (near-miss rule). **S5-d:** W4-32 (rung prefix), W4-03, W4-26, W4-28 or the b7 set in cell D (rung D), W4-14 or W4-32 (rung F) fails after the confirmatory rule by 2 SE or more: that rung's link ships off (by less: `profile`). **S5-e:** a *valid* cost block (PREFLIGHT 9) above 5 % at RAYON 1 or 8: the top link of that profile ships off; an invalid block leaves the row open and the profile a profile. **S5-f:** a failed F row of the dollar rung (W4-01, W4-02, W4-05..W4-09, W4-15, W4-16): recorded, the dollar presets are withdrawn; no stop. **S5-g:** a preset that fails W5-04 is withdrawn from the site unless the failing row is one of its link's gating rows; no stop. **S5-h:** W5-02 scorecard above +2 for T: the default set is not proposed. **S5-i** (alarm, not a stop): the nominal drift of `ln D` moves the executed official flow by more than 2 $bn a month between cells F and `Fn` (the finance link without the dollar link, 6101-6132), or `tp_fo_bp` of cell F exceeds the finance-only cell `Fn` by more than 10 bp at record 239 or 6 bp at record 119: recorded for the owner (the trend of `ln D - ln fx` is reported, not an alarm). **S5-m:** the merge proof fails after 2 repairs: the web line does not receive the profiles. **S5-o:** an early gate of the oil link fails (D59): the oil link ships off.

**Files.** `crates/econsim-core/src/{config.rs, world/trade.rs}` (D57), `crates/econsim-server/src/main.rs` (D60), `scripts/profiles/{b8-world-s5a, -s5b, -s5c, -s5a-nc, -s5a-lean, -s5c-nd, -wabm, -wabm-mf, -wl}.json`, `scripts/profiles/legacy_b8off.json`, `scripts/world_check.py`, `scripts/world_runs.py`, `scripts/world_s5.py`, `scripts/joint_gates.py`, `scripts/world_events.py`, `docs/{DESIGN, CALIBRATION, API, SCENARIOS}.md`, the earlier README. The web files (`domain/types.ts`, `domain/shocks.ts`, `domain/request.ts`, `domain/scenarios.ts`, `domain/caveats.ts`, a world profile constant and the by-country panels) are the web brief's, in the web line, not in this branch.

**Commits.** (1) `world: stage 5 step 0 and the import-gap centring constants (S2-R7), Tariff check; defaults byte-identical` (2) `scripts: stage-5 profiles, legacy_b8off, harness commands` (3) `docs: world block S5 record, SCENARIOS rows, API` (4) merge proof report. The record states the class of every link in its first paragraph.

#### Sub-round 5-T2 (the trade repair, rev 12)

**Scope.** The stage-5 trade rung failed one G row, W2-15 dM/GDP (-0.433 pp, SE 0.032, 128 registered seeds, against +-0.3: S5-c, S5-R15), with every other rung-T row passing. The review found one mechanism (D62): the block's import price is flat against PT while the legacy ABM's falls 0.38 % a year, and the D13 import elasticities turn the difference into a drift of the import share. 5-T2 repairs exactly that, adds the uniform-duty fold that closes W5-06 (D63), re-measures the D57 constants (the import-demand term sees the repaired Q/Y), and runs rung T once on fresh seeds (D64). If T2 ships, rungs D and F run as in stage 5 on the same fresh seeds. It changes no band, no lever and no other model code.

**Work.** (0) *Step 0:* D64 (3). (1) *Code:* `world_import_rel_drift` (config key and validation, the factor in `TradeState::post_bloc_ref`), the uniform-duty fold in the same function, the profiles `b8-world-t2a/b/c.json` (+ `-t2a-nc.json`, `-t2c-nd.json`), 5 core tests, 1 cli test; defaults byte-identical (I1, I10). (2) *Identities* I1, I2, I4, I5, I7, I9, I10 and the pair identities on the new binary. (3) *Rung T on 4401-4528 and the events, then D and F.* (4) *Records:* CALIBRATION `## b8-world 5-T2` (the first paragraph states the class of the trade link), DESIGN 19.8, API.md (the key), README (test counts), SCENARIOS (the tariff cards if T ships). The class rule, the near-miss rule and stages D and F are those of stage 5.

**Rung T2 gates (cell T2 against W').** *E:* I1, I2, I4, I7 (T2 against `b8-world-s4a.json` carrying the same drift and constants: every non-world key bit-identical), I10, W2-11, W2-12, W2-17, W4-23, W4-37, W4-38, **W5-06 (bit identity of `Tariff{null, null, p}` and `ImportTariff{p}`)**, determinism at RAYON 1 and 8, `hcheck`, ledger and `check_identities` in debug. *G:* W1-31 v2 (with the new constants), W2-06 (T1-T9 at 48 seeds), W2-07, W2-13 / W4-32 (trade prefix: G1-G4, G2 counts and paired spike guard, W2-15 dX/GDP and dM/GDP within 0.3 pp), Tier A TA1-TA7. *F:* W2-01, W2-02, W2-03, W2-04, W2-09, W4-22, W4-24. *R:* W2-05, W2-08, W2-10, W2-16, **W2-18**, W4-21, W4-24b, W4-25 (a). The presets that need only the trade link are read in cell T2.

**Stop rules (5-T2).** **S5T-a:** any identity (I1, I2, I4, I5, I7, I9, I10, W5-06, the pair identities, the ledger, `hcheck`) fails after 2 repairs: the sub-round stops; stage 5's classes stand. **S5T-b:** the step-0 re-measure of g or of a constant is outside 2 SE of the D64 value: stop and ask (nothing is changed by the implementer). "
          "**S5T-c:** W2-15 (dX/GDP or dM/GDP), W2-13 / W4-32 or W2-06 fails on the pooled 128 seeds by 2 SE or more: the trade link ships off for good (no second repair round); by less than 2 SE: profile (S5-R12). **S5T-d:** a rung-T G row that passed in stage 5 (W1-31 v2, G1-G4 and the spike guard, W2-06, W2-07, TA1-TA7) fails in T2: the repair is rejected and the stage-5 outcome stands; g and the constants are not re-tuned. ## 4. Validation targets

`K`: **G** gate: out-of-sample, counted as evidence, judged on the stated mean with jackknife SE (a row within 1 SE of a bound is re-run at 48 seeds); **F** fit check: must pass, but the parameter was set or searched on this statistic, or the value is by construction, so it is **not** evidence of validity (D30); **R** reported only; **E** engineering identity or consistency. Gate reports show F and G rows in separate tables and summary counts quote G only: **27 of the 116 rows are G, 43 are F, 20 R and 26 E** (rev 11 adds W4-39 (R), W5-06 and W5-07 (R) and W5-08 and W5-09 (E); rev 10 had 111 rows: 107 at `cc33f15`, list B added W4-35 and W4-36, 4-3 W4-37 and W4-38). "Expected" is the pre-registered value from the reference (Python, the lead modeller's reference tooling (published as `scripts/world/`)) or from a hand calculation (marked), or the master value measured for this spec on 3 seeds x 5000 households (`$W/spec_work/runs/`). Horizons are months after the shock unless stated. Bloc groups: AE5 = EA, JP, UK, CA, AU; EME5 = CN, IN, KR, BR, MX; FLO4 = IN, KR, BR, MX.

**US gates that must not regress (numbering used by this spec; HANDOFF 4 lists the content).** G1 hard bands (32 seeds, months 12-239: policy rate 1.6-3.6, u 4.3-5.5, core PCE 1.7-2.3, HP GDP cycle sd 1.1-1.9); G2 per-seed (policy < 1.0 in at most 2 seeds and < 0.5 in at most 1; no one-month |dlnGDP| > 3%; sfc 0; non-finite 0); G3 corporate profit share 9-11%; G4 cycle not-worse set (exp_dur_m, hk_rate, u_updown, u_ac1, du12_skew, tot_lt25, u<2.5 sign test; Z >= -2.0); G5 firm entry and exit >= 7%/yr; G6 scorecard (208-id severity not worse beyond noise); G7 IRF gates of `irf_check.py` (P1-P7, O1-O4, H1-H5, T1-T4 term-premium, Q1-Q2, X1, F1); G8 `hcheck` zero failures plus engineering (no warnings, tests, determinism at 1 and 8 threads, debug ledger). [assumed numbering of G5-G8; the content is HANDOFF 4.]
