A preset whose needed link does not ship is withdrawn, not failed. Pre-flight reading of the ECB preset in the integrated model (16 seeds): EA gap trough -0.56 at m16 and EA inflation -0.46 pp pass; US GDP trough -0.14 % (band +-0.10) and Mexico's peak gap 0.18 (band 0.15) fail, because the reference values came from the isolated blocs; BoJ: JP gap trough -0.42 at month 17 and inflation -0.32 pp pass, the US GDP trough (-0.10, SE 0.08) is reported (D56 ix); PBoC: CN gap +0.83 and inflation +0.50 pass, KR +0.17, OPEC+ +0.16, AU inside [0, 0.4], US exports +0.02 reported; the ECB's US GDP trough row is reported for the same reason, so Mexico's peak gap is the one gating row it fails.

---

## 6. Engineering rules

1. **Determinism.** The world code is serial; reductions iterate fixed arrays in fixed order; the only parallel code is `oil-check`'s sample loop (independent samples keyed by index). Shared tables are immutable `Arc`s built through a `OnceLock`. Every random draw comes from `agent_rng(seed, tick, stream, index)` and is taken whether or not its scale is zero. No wall-clock or hash-order dependence. The output must be identical at `RAYON_NUM_THREADS` 1 and 8 (both with the block off and on).
2. **Ledger.** All money moves through `ledger::pay`, `bond_transfer` or `monetary::trade_with*`. The memo positions and the bilateral decomposition never touch balances. `world::check_identities` runs in debug builds after `ledger::check_consistency` and panics with the tick on failure; `sfc_world_error` is exported in `Metrics.world`.
3. **Legacy identity.** Every hook is `if let Some(wb) = w.world.as_deref()` (and the link flag) around a *new* branch; the else branch is the literal old expression (same operations in the same order; the model is sensitive to one ulp). Do not inline or refactor existing helpers (the b7 review found default md5 changes from inlining). Every new function called from an existing hot function is `#[inline(never)]`; I1 is run after each hook and each hook has its own WIP commit (section 1.4); if I1 changes with no logic change, `#[inline(never)]` is the first remedy. New fields on existing structs are `#[serde(default)]`; `Metrics.world` is skipped when `None`.
4. **Performance budget.** The block adds under 5% at 5,000 households at RAYON 1 and 8; target under 2%. Estimated: bloc step 12 x ~5k multiply-adds (K = 240 kernels), forcing paths 12 x ~4k flops, trade 108 origin cells with `exp`/`ln`, oil ~200 flops, finance and positions ~1k: about 0.1 ms per month (1%) against 9.3 ms (RAYON 1) / 8.2 ms (RAYON 8) measured on 3 master runs with the `profile` bin. Measure the **total** ms/month, world on against world off, best of 3 at RAYON 1 and 8 (`world_check.py cost`, `profile 5000 48`): the `world` and `exog` phase rows alone miss `settle` and the hooks in `goods` and `curve`. `scripts/finite_size_benchmark.py` is a firm-size sampler, not a speed tool; `scripts/bench_speed.sh` gives months per second at 20k/50k/100k.
5. **Tests.** Stage counts: S1 +37 (one more ignored), S2 +28, S3 +25, S4: 4-1 +25 plus the list-A fix round (10 core, 1 server, 2 cli), 4-2 +22 plus list A' (8 core, 1 server, 2 cli), 4-3 +28 core, +1 server and +3 cli (rev 10), 4-4 +4, S5 +11 (rev 11: three core, one server, one cli, six script and web tests) on top of the base count at `M3` (read from `cargo test --release -p econsim-core`; the README shows 256 on 0242ab9) [verify@M3]. Each stage reports the new total.
6. **Docs.** `docs/DESIGN.md` new section 19 and the tick-order list (section 3), `docs/CALIBRATION.md` one section per stage plus the Config ledger (`scripts/config_ledger.py`), `docs/SCENARIOS.md` (stage 5), `docs/API.md` (shocks, `world` keys), the earlier README (feature, run instructions, test count, numbers).
7. **Per-commit checks.** (1) `cargo build --release` no warnings; `cargo test --release -p econsim-core`. (2) I1 (md5 at RAYON 1 and 8), also after each hook (WIP commits, section 1.4). (3) `python3 -B scripts/world/build_world_params.py --check` and `solve_blocs.py --check`. (4) `cargo build -p econsim-cli`, debug `run 2000 24 1` with the stage profile (ledger and world identities), then `cargo clean --profile dev`. (5) `hcheck 4 20000 240` zero failures (block off, and on from stage 2). (6) I2/I4 (32 seeds, at most 8 processes, `RAYON_NUM_THREADS=1`, `python3 -B`). (7) `profile 5000 48`. (8) The stage's gates. (9) Stage 5: `npm run build` (link the website's `node_modules` in, remove it and `.next` afterwards). Delete the 20000x240 runs once summarised; `pgrep` must show none of your servers; kill only PIDs you started (never `pkill -f`).
8. **Report format** (as b7): head commit, summary (mechanism, final parameters, defaults), identity results with md5s, acceptance tables with SEs, selection and stop log, checks, SCENARIOS text, open issues.

---

## 7. Open items (not resolved)

1. **Rates and currencies.** AE5 policy easing at two years (-0.94) stays 3x the Miranda-Agrippino-Rey point estimate; the foreign GDP trough is at months 20-26 against 14-16 in the data (the I&N path has reverted); China's response depends on a regime parameter (D25). Stage 4 may re-open `k_us`/`lambda_rp`.
2. **Fear-of-floating `g_z` 0.25** has no verified source [assumed]; it makes the floaters' rates rise (sign) and trims their depreciation by about 1 point.
3. **Sector detail of tariffs.** Partner-sector duties enter b7 as one weighted scalar; the retail wedge, elastic shares and absorption are uniform across covered sectors.
4. **Import composition.** The ABM's firm imports are about 27% of imports against 62% of goods imports that are inputs or capital goods (trade REPORT section 6); household vs firm import shares are a master calibration issue. Master's import elasticities 0.2/0.6 (D13).
5. **Dollar invoicing of non-US trade.** The 0.6% fall in rest-of-world trade volumes per 1% dollar rise (Gopinath et al.) is not modelled; US re-import content (4.9%) is not deducted.
6. **Oil.** Products (diesel, jet), natural gas and the `GasolineMargin` pump-price shock are not modelled; ABM Energy demand and the market's US demand are separate (W3-18); the shale regime default is between boom and discipline; Brent/WTI spread is reported only.
7. **Composition.** OPEC+ membership (UAE flag covers GDP and oil only; trade and TIC keep the UAE; Malaysia/Brunei are in RoW for trade and in OPEC+ for GDP, oil and finance); EA-20 (Bulgaria ignored); RoW is a residual with about $1.05tn of economies lacking WB data.
8. **Finance.** Official Treasury holdings by bloc are an allocation (unverified); the equity cap-rate arithmetic for `erp_fin_loading`; memo positions use reinvested earnings and no cash flows; foreign equity returns are `b_j x US return` plus noise; US-originated risk-off needs an explicit shock (the dollar's haven status is regime dependent).
9. **Not modelled.** Bloc fiscal policy and sovereign premia; pegs breaking; neutral-rate and potential-growth shocks; the ELB inside the dynamics; equilibrium real-exchange-rate random walks; foreign yield curves beyond the memo 10y; capital controls beyond `uip_w`.
10. **Evidence quality.** The 2026 inputs (UAE exit, Hormuz, tariff regime changes, several policy rates) are secondary in the research; the Iacoviello-Navarro paths are figure reads; trade V09 (dollar-priced pass-through 0.14) and the dollar-priced state (0.47 long run) measure different things.
11. **After joint calibration 3.** Names to re-read before stage 1: `tariff_step`, `TariffState`, `GoodsState.tariff_month`, `firm_stage` signature and the line numbers of H8/H9 (`goods.rs` 614, 1049, 1056, 1359-1581; `firms.rs` 995; `metrics.rs` 1729), `fx_mp_premium`, `fx_rate_knee`, `qe_duration_weight`, `pin_path_on`, `cipi_excludes_nonstorable`; test count; default md5; `docs/SCENARIOS_MEASURED_*`. Stage 4 re-reads the merged `fx.rs` (D33).
12. **UIP uses the nominal US policy deviation**, not the real rate (inherited from the Iacoviello-Navarro setup): a US inflation shock (oil, tariff) with a Taylor-rule response is treated as a real tightening by the blocs.
13. **Expected path of the offset (D28).** The offset decaying at 0.97 a month in `ex[k]` is my assumption; the alternative (a constant shift of the whole path) leaves the blocs expecting a rise to neutral for ever and defeats the centring.
14. **Trade block and master's real dollar.** With the dollar link off, `z~_j = c_t + cross-section` and `c_t = 100[ln(fx_t/fx_0) + ln(PT_t/PT_0) - ln(p_world,t/p_world,0)]` is master's own real dollar relative to the re-captured base. On the 32 master S6 runs it has a mean over months 12-239 of **+2.87%** (sd across seeds 8.0, SE 1.42) and a trend of **+0.15 %/yr** (sd 0.82); the CPI-based `real_dollar_index` metric, a different series, gives +13.3% and +1.17 %/yr. The integrated trade shares inherit `c_t` by design; W2-15 gates the cross-section `z~_j - c_t` and compares X/GDP and M/GDP with master, and W2-16 only reports `c_t`. With the dollar link on, the real dollar has no drift and the baseline trade shares move away from master's (checked by G1-G4 in stage 4).
15. **Double counting.** The b7 tariff-elastic import share and origin substitution do not double count; imports from the tariffed origin have no independent check beyond W2-07.
16. **Evidence labels.** Only 27 of 104 target rows are out-of-sample gates (D30); the bloc block's validity rests mainly on W1-21, W1-22, W1-27, W2-01, W2-07, W3-07..W3-11, W4-03, W4-14, W4-18, W4-26, W4-28 and W5-*.
17. **Baseline dollar transient (rev 7).** With the dollar link on, the ABM's dollar follows the blocs' UIP on the ABM's own rate path, which drifts down by about 1 pp over the first 100 reported months (the start-up drift; the 24-month server warm-up removes most of it for `compare`). The weighted bloc `z` is +14.9 % at the base and unwinds by month 60, so the base-relative `ln D` falls 7.6 % by month 24 and 13 % by month 60 against 2.1 % and 4.5 % for the ABM's own dollar (W4-31). It cancels in every pair; W4-32 judges its effect on the ABM baseline. If W4-32 fails because of it, the remedy to design is the removal of the unforced response from the base state (a deterministic, shock-independent path computed once at the base), not a splice. **Rev 9: resolved by D47** (W4-32 did fail on it): the path is the blocs' conditional forecast under the inputs they expect at the base, not the path of zero inputs, which the review's twin used and which broke W4-29; see 2.6.
18. **Model RoW holdings are 1.5 times the data.** RoW holds 42.8 % of GDP (34-66 % across seeds) and 37.6 % of the face debt at the base month against 28.4 % and 29.4 % in the data (30 % at creation). The official block is therefore expressed in real-world-equivalent units (D39); the quantity effects of an `OfficialTreasury` shock on the ABM bond market are proportional to the tranche, not to GDP.
19. **The CN secular flow is a baseline term-premium drift.** `world_fo_trend_cn` -80 $bn a year adds +6.7 bp to the 10y term premium at month 12, +21 bp at month 60 and +49 bp at month 240 (hand calculation, 2.7). Lever {-80, 0} in sub-round 4-2 (S4-h).
20. **Memo positions cover 80 % of the IIP assets and 82 % of its liabilities** (D40); the model's own NIIP/GDP includes the two ledger stocks at the ABM's scale.
21. **A `Dollar` shock in dollar mode is partly unwound by the blocs' UIP** (realised +8.6 % at impact and +6.4 % at month 12 for +10 %, 2.6); the preset text must say so in stage 5.
22. **Assumed in rev 7:** the blocs see the broad addend `a_t` (common), not the bilateral `a_{j,t}`; `qpsi` is projected at `fx_psi_rho`; `OfficialTreasury` spreads `bn` evenly over `months`. The comment at `types.rs:613` ("Foreign currency per dollar is 1/fx") is wrong: `fx` is foreign currency per dollar (`goods.rs:112`, `import_pass_through`).
23. **The CN trend exhausts the CN tranche (rev 8, D44).** `world_fo_trend_cn` -80 $bn a year sold from a CN tranche of 585 $bn rw (growing 3.75 % a year with the memo) ends at month 102 (93-111 over 64 seeds); the per-bloc cap stops the flow, the baseline term premium the block adds rises to about +27 bp at month 100 and settles near +19 bp (stock 1.46 % of GDP). The rev-7 figure (+49 bp at month 240) was an uncapped extrapolation with negative holdings. Lever {-80, 0} (S4-h) unchanged.
24. **RoW's holdings in the ABM keep growing (rev 8).** In the matched cell RoW's bond market value is 105-120 % of GDP and 66-75 % of the face debt at month 239 (37-65 % of GDP at the base); the memo tranche grows with trend GDP only, so its share of RoW's holdings falls from 40.8 % to 18-21 %. Memo semantics: the term-premium effect is on the tranche, not on RoW's holdings.
25. **The VIX term is a level (rev 8, D44).** The research gives $1.05bn a month per VIX point and "zero beyond six months"; the spec implements `v_vix mean_6(F - Fbar)`, so a persistent VIX gives a persistent flow (autocorrelation 0.97, sd $7.3bn a month, 12-month cumulative sd $73bn, 60-month $207bn, about 3 bp and 8 bp of 10y term premium). [assumed]; lever {1.05, 0.5, 0}.
26. **Counterparties (rev 8, D45).** Official purchases are sold by the banks and the Fund, official sales go to the banks; the Fund's own rebalance and `rebalance_row` can re-trade the same bonds within the month. The memo tranche and the term premium use the executed official flow only.
27. **Chaos between the arms of L and E (rev 8).** Once the term premium reaches the ABM the two arms of a pair diverge: the paired sd of a 10y response difference is 5.2 bp at h = 12 and 8.2 bp at h = 18 (`qe05`); per seed, the paired sd of the G1 statistics is 0.125 (`y_sd`), 0.17 (core PCE), 0.47 (policy), 0.38 (unemployment). The power statements of 3.0 (rev 8) rest on them.
28. **The remaining baseline shift of the redesigned link (rev 9).** L4d against E4a on seeds 1-64: core +0.108, policy +0.146, unemployment -0.056, zero-bound months -11.8. The forecast-error state has no drift, while the ABM's own real dollar `c_t` drifts +2 to +3 points over 20 years and the ABM's start-up transient moves the blocs below their forecast by about 1 point in years 1-5; the G rows pass with room (core 2.05 against 2.3), but the link is not baseline-neutral to the second decimal. The review's zero-input twin was closer on core (+0.043) only by cancelling a +2.4 to +3.2 point excess of the real dollar in years 1-5 against -3.3 later, and it paid for it in continuity (D47).
29. **Nominal drift of `ln D` (rev 9).** The nominal broad dollar carries the inflation differential of the blocs' price levels against US tradables (+14 points per 100 months above the ABM's own dollar, W4-31). The ABM does not read it in dollar mode with the trade link on; the official block reads its monthly change (the baseline flow `eta x 100 d ln D` changes by about -$6bn a year against the -$80bn CN trend), positions (4-3) read the bilateral rates. The combined run after list B checks it; making the price part forecast-relative too would remove it at the price of a nominal dollar that falls with US prices alone.
30. **W4-07 and W4-08 at their floors (rev 9).** On the ABM's less hot baseline and with the bound, the dollar's month-24 response per pp of policy gap is 2.1 and 1.8 on seeds 1-16 (4-1: 3.62 and 2.93); both are F rows, none of the pre-authorised levers moves them upward. The statistic reads a decayed response (the paired policy gap is 0 by month 12 and moves in 25 bp steps) and is noisy; recorded, not a stop.
31. **The bound is one-sided (rev 9, D48).** Like M-F it is the identity below the knee, so a deep easing (`T` far below zero, a zero-bound episode) is not bounded; the baseline already contains such months and the matched-cell comparison covers them. A symmetric bound is a stage-5 option.
32. **No tariff end date (rev 10).** `Tariff` components are permanent and non-negative (D34), so the retaliation duty never falls: the ratchet-down branch (`mu_down` 0.1) and the lag as a delay-then-hold are exercised through the state function only. A "tariff for N months" or "rollback" scenario needs a duration field (default permanent) on `Tariff`; stage 5 designs it with the web builder.
33. **Memo positions: constant yields and a baseline drift (rev 10).** The memo yields (3.8 % assets, 2.55 % liabilities) do not follow the model's interest rates, so the primary-income balance does not move in a rate scenario (the research has slopes of 0.26 and 0.39 on the 10y yield); the model's own `niip_pct_gdp` drifts from -75 % at the first reported month to -122 % at month 240 (current account -5 % of GDP: the ABM's RoW keeps accumulating US assets), so absolute NIIP paths are reported with their paired responses only.
34. **Retaliation reaches the blocs only through US imports (rev 10).** The blocs see the US import-demand gap (D27) but not their own export loss to the US, and a retaliator's own duty does not touch its output; blocs without a research entry (JP, UK, KR, BR, AU, OPEC+) and MX (kappa 0) never retaliate; the response is on duty-inclusive values with no exporter absorption (Cavallo et al.: 54 % for agriculture).
35. **Dollar-link-on positions and the cumulative prefix (rev 10).** The branch of `PositionsState::update` that uses the dollar module's bilateral rates has a unit test and no gate; the cumulative prefix (retaliation, positions, finance, dollar) and W4-25 (b) are measured after list B.
36. **"Today's US economy" and the world block exclude each other (rev 11, D60).** The web line's `validate_config` answers 400 to `start_state: us_now` with `world_block_enabled` true (the Today's-start rulings note (not published) 4: steering is untested with the block); the site hides world features from that start. Sub-round 5-U would make them compatible: the steered 24-month window stepped with the blocs running, the realised tariff rate of the start window against D24, bases captured at the end of burn-in against a window that moves the economy away from them. **Closed for the trade link (rev 13, D65; the Today's-start design note (not published; its content is summarised in CALIBRATION, `world-usnow`)):** the trade block runs on Today's start from a dated reference installed at the end of the window. The dollar, finance and oil links on Today's start stay refused and remain open under this item.
37. **The coupled import-demand term sits on its +-10 clamp in 7-40 % of months (rev 11, D57).** Only its mean can be centred; the per-seed level (sd 2-3.7 pp across seeds) and the clamped response to a shock remain. Paired responses are unaffected to first order; the baseline output gap of a foreign bloc in a single seed is not a result.
38. **A tariff on a product group is one weighted duty (rev 11; item 3 sharpened).** For a 25 % duty on Durables alone the sector prices of Durables, Nondurables and Tech rise +0.68, +0.53 and +0.58 % at month 12: the retail wedge, the elastic shares and the absorption are uniform across covered sectors, so the model cannot answer "what happens to the price of parts". A sector-specific wedge is a stage-6 design.
39. **The Jensen term of the oil market (rev 11, S3-R10/R13).** The integrated market's cross-seed sd of ln price is 0.378 against the legacy 0.425; against the cached benchmark the link-on arm is -1.96 % (SE 0.87). Accepted and reported; the offset 8.355 is right in log terms (mean ln shift +0.0003).
40. **The Fund sleeve (4-4) is not built (rev 11).** `world_fund_foreign_equity` and `world_spillback_off` do not exist; W4-18 is not run and the spillback share is unmeasured.
41. **The realism program and the joint switch (rev 11, D61).** Realism F5 (the dollar as a jump in `exchange_rate`) is inert when the dollar link replaces `exchange_rate`; F4 (the rule's look-through) and F1 (rate perception) act on the b7 tariff set and the P rows. The world ladder uses most of the G1 core-PCE headroom (J4 1.79; T 1.89; D 2.04; F 1.99 against a top of 2.3).
42. **No tariff end date, design (rev 11, item 32 continued).** `months: Option<u32>` on `Tariff`, default permanent; the component is removed that many months after its shock (D34 amended); the retaliation state function already has the ratchet-down branch and its tests; W4-37 needs one more pair test. Sub-round 5-T.
43. **The financial-factor keys are ABM keys (rev 11, D58).** `fin_factor_sigma` 0.30, `fin_jump_intensity` 0.06 and `erp_fin_loading` 0.0028 are zero in the J4 default and are carried by every world profile; with the block off they move one baseline quantity, the equity price, by -2.6 % (32 seeds, `W'` against `Wl`); the policy (-0.13 pp), 10-year (-0.07 pp) and bank-capital (-0.4 points) shifts of the profiles belong to the import elasticities 0.4 / 1.0. The default set does not read the financial-factor keys; the lean arm `Tl` measures the default set without them (pre-flight: equity price -0.4 % against `L0`, every quantity inside noise).
44. **The legacy import price drifts against domestic goods prices and the data do not (rev 12, D62).** In J4 the ABM's import price falls against PT at 0.38 %/yr (world goods inflation 0.5 %/yr against 1.22 %/yr for the ABM's tradables), so imports / GDP rise 0.04 pp a year; the BLS ex-fuel import price index over the core goods CPI has no trend (+0.02 %/yr, 2005-2026; over the all-items CPI -1.46 %/yr), and US imports / GDP have no trend since 2005 (BEA: 15.3 % in 2015, 14.0 % in 2024). The 5-T2 repair restores the legacy drift for continuity; whether J4's `world_goods_inflation` or the ABM's tradables inflation should change is a realism-program question (it moves every absolute import-share level) and is not decided here.
45. **The D57 constants depend on the import price path (rev 12).** The mean of the coupled import-demand term is a property of the ABM's Q/Y trend against its 60-month benchmark: with the drift of D62 it rises by 0.35-0.86 pp per bloc. Any later change of the import price path (a realism change of `world_goods_inflation`, the dollar link's baseline) needs the constants re-measured (`gap-offset`, seeds 1-12, trigger 2 SE) before W1-31 is read.
46. **Band provenance of the trade-share rows (rev 12).** W2-15's 0.7 pp (review problem 12) and 0.3 pp (stage-2 review F8) have no derivation in any source; the BEA series (NIPA, `bea_nipa_trade_extract`) has an annual imports / GDP sd of 0.71 pp (2015-2025) and the J4 baseline an across-seed sd of the 228-month mean of 0.89 pp. A band that is meant to catch a baseline drift should be derived from the ABM's own seed-to-seed sd and from what the downstream rows tolerate (G1 moves 0.04-0.19 pp for the 0.4 pp). The orchestrator's ruling S5-R22 stands until a derivation is registered before a run.

---

## Notes to the orchestrator

1. **Rulings applied (D-R1..D-R10 and the revision-3 rulings), one line each.** D-R1: `ius = 100 (i - i_inf) - world_ius_offset` with a fixed constant measured once (-0.40, SE 0.09; D28; `ex[k]` shifted by the offset decaying at 0.97 a month [assumed]); W1-31 stays G and is scored on the holdout seeds 13-32; W2-15 gates `z~_j - c_t` and W2-16 reports `c_t`. D-R2: K = 240 plus a tail term (D29), because K = 240 alone fails the 0.05 test (JP 1.39); S1-d reduces K for the S equation only. D-R3: no extra activity noise; the prototype activity only in `oil-check`; W3-15 expected `oil_lvl` 0.269, a first-run miss by 0.001, then the D5 levers. D-R4: 43 rows F, 26 G, 16 R, 16 E of 101 (D30). D-R5: `UsCredibility{tp_bp, months, dollar_pct}` (D31). D-R6: monthly official-flow rule, W4-13 report-only (D32). D-R7: M-F and M-X bind; exact `psi_step` call from b7-transmission 6faf872; b7-transmission is part of `M3` with a fallback sentence (D33). D-R8: `ImportTariff` set-level, `cov` per channel (D34). D-R9: elasticity fallback in the profile (D13). D-R10: one AR(1), lever grid, 6-month W4-02 row R (D35). Revision-3 rulings: M3 content (D33); W2-15 and `c_t`; W4-07, W4-08, W4-15 F; W3-07 and W3-14 stay G and leave the Selection; W1-31 holdout; Hormuz row R; D36 (burn-in, bases, `fx_base`).
2. **Where I deviated from, or interpreted, a ruling.** (a) D-R3 words the isolated oil rows as "data bands, G"; D-R4 relabels in-sample rows F. Oil REPORT 7.5 shows that steps 1-3 of the oil calibration fit exactly the W3-01..W3-06 bands and moments, so they are F (they must still pass). (b) D-R1 gives the realised-input formula only; a constant shift of the whole expected path would leave the blocs expecting a rise to neutral for ever, so `ex[k]` carries the offset decaying at the path's own rate. (c) The measured offset is -0.40, not -0.6. (d) D-R7's M-F bound is applied to the nominal deviation `ius`, not to a real-rate differential (same function, different argument; W4-28 checks it). (e) W4-04 is F under D-R5; W4-06 is F (its only lever is the noise added to hit it); W1-28 is R with expected 0.106 (the earlier 0.032 was wrong). (f) **D-R10 calls the W4-02 impact and 12-month rows G; they are F** (D-R4 logic: gamma comes from the same regressions as the targets) (D35). (g) The Hormuz preset row is R, not gated (its literature range, Brent +45..65%, lies above the prototype's +40%). (h) W3-09 and W4-03 are kept G but are borderline (D30). (i) The fallback for D33 is written so that stage 1 does not depend on whether `M3` includes b7-transmission.
3. **Research and spec defects found.** From revision 1 to 2: the old goldens mixed settings; `world_ref.py` had no `gates` or `--inputs`; the old W1-28 expected value was wrong; trade hand values used theta-bar 0.94 (the data give 0.893); the official-flow rule overshot six-fold and carried an unsourced Japan factor; `m_jk` orientation; the `z~` price-level definition drifted. From revision 2 to 3: `w109` network reference and the three stage-1 test data sets were missing from the goldens; the stress replay mixed extra shocks into the statistics of the gates; shocks scheduled for later were anticipated by the reference from month 0 (now active from `t0` only, and the Dollar path follows `ShockState` exactly); `demand_scale` in the reference omitted the tail and could miss the peak for long half-lives; S1-d as first written could not pass its own test; W2-15 gated master's real dollar. From the first review: E9 sign (D11), oil `eta0` columns (D12), trade `retaliation.recommended_rule.lag_months.IN = 12` not in the report text, `elasticities.us_exports.monthly_adjustment_speed_lambda` 0.07 against the text's 41% at 12 months (0.04 used), oil K9 against K10 at m24, finance `xi` against blocs `k_us`.
4. **Validation I ran** (scratch and reference only; no Rust built or edited, the main checkout stayed on master and clean): in the lead modeller's tree and in a scratch copy of the repo layout built by `scripts/world/make_layout.py` (placement table of 1.1): `selftest` OK, `gates` (every F and G row passes), `kcheck` (identical in both trees), `goldens --check` (stage1, irf_unit, pf_ar1_EA, kref, inputs_clean, inputs_stress: max difference 0), `trade_hand.py` (byte-identical golden), `oil_activity.py` runs; replay round trip 1e-10 for both replay files; `w109` 3e-8 and 1e-6; real ABM series centre to |z| <= 1.32 on the holdout seeds 13-32. The earlier baselines (3 seeds, 5000 households; default md5 `3215ea02...`; 104 months/s) stand.
5. **Unsure assumptions (mark when reviewing).** The offset decay in `ex[k]` (D28); constant continuation as the tail (D29); the tranche growth with trend GDP (2.7); D17 projections (0.93/0.95/0.98); D18 Okun gap; `world_opec_va_share` 0.20; `world_theta_f_ratio` 0.5; `world_china_risk_pts_per_pct` 6; export FX long run 0.30; DCP non-USD speed 0.9; `zeta` noise sizes (1.5 / 2.0 / 0.3 / 0.3); the W4-28 bands and the numeric preset bands marked [assumed]; sleeve parameters; the cost estimate 1% (to be measured); the 2.4 retaliation elasticity; the K-FO conversion; the memo positions' FX share for bank claims (0.3); the validation ranges of 1.9 (my choices where the existing shocks give no analogue); the S1-d recipe (S equation only, recomputed tail) passes narrowly (0.042).
6. **Dependencies.** Stage 1 must start from the merged joint-3 master (`M3`); if joint 3 changes `goods.rs` hook anchors, re-read section 1.4 before coding; the stage-1 profile and step 0 need the frozen `M3` binaries (1.9). Stage 4's first step re-reads the merged `fx.rs` (D33). The block touches `exog.rs`, `monetary.rs`, `goods.rs`, `firms.rs` (one read site), `fx.rs` (none changed), `metrics.rs`, `shocks.rs`, `types.rs`, `config.rs`, `rng.rs`, `sim.rs`, `init.rs`, `testkit.rs`, `lib.rs` and `crates/econsim-cli` (`main.rs`, `bin/profile.rs`); keep other branches off these anchors during the stages. H15 (`common.rs`) was deleted.
7. **Companion files** (the lead modeller's working tree; only the published parts are in this repository): `targets.json` (101 rows of section 4, published as `scripts/world/targets.json`), `pstar_blocs.json` (published as `scripts/world/pstar.json`), the `golden_*` files and `oil_activity_results.json` (published under `crates/econsim-core/src/world/data/golden/`), and the 18 reference tools of the placement table (published under `scripts/world/`); `param_map.json`, `config_keys.json`, the target generators, the tuning scripts, the scripts that produced revisions 2 and 3, and the texts of revisions 1 and 2 are not published.

8. **Revision 7 (stage-4 pre-flight): what was found and which rulings are needed.** `fx.rs` and the b7-transmission premium step are unchanged in substance, so the rev-6 premium block was right as to `q`; what was wrong or missing is around it. Found against `ccdcc48` (evidence and file:line references in the stage 4 pre-flight note (not published)): (a) `UsInputs` carries one addend with one decay and `bloc.rs` has no `fex` term, so the rev-6 text could not be implemented as written (2.6 now specifies `dollar_proj` and the two phases); (b) the dollar base must be captured at the end of the last burn-in month, not in the stage-1 `Bases` (1.6b); the weighted bloc `z` is +14.9 % at that month, so a level-based dollar would have jumped by 15 %; (c) the official block's units (D39) and the baseline drift of the CN trend flow (+49 bp on the 10y after 20 years); (d) W4-05's P(VIX rise >= 10) fails at the rev-6 jump intensity (0.024 against [0.03, 0.06]); (e) W4-21's NIIP/GDP (-68.9) cannot be produced by the nine stocks (-59.8); W4-20's 40.8 % needs a normalisation; W4-14 used the wrong QE scenario and master value (qe05, -0.34); W4-12 and W4-22 expected values restated; W4-28 reference cells measured; (f) W4-26 row P7 is a coin flip for master itself and is judged paired; (g) the J4 defaults: M-X and M-P on, M-F off. Rulings needed from the orchestrator, with my recommendation: (1) jump intensity 0.06 in the stage-4 profile (recommended: yes, before any run; the alternative is to demote the P row of W4-05 to R); (2) base-relative dollar with W4-31 reported and W4-32 deciding (recommended: yes; the remedy of open item 17 is designed only if W4-32 fails); (3) real-world-equivalent units for the official block, D39 (recommended: yes); (4) W4-20, W4-21, W4-22 restated as in section 4 (recommended: yes); (5) the blocs see the common broad addend (recommended: yes, [assumed]); (6) four sub-rounds, 4-1 alone first (recommended; merging 4-1 and 4-2 saves one review but joins the `exchange_rate` skip with the `update_curve` and ledger hooks); (7) `world_fo_trend_cn` stays -80 with the lever {-80, 0} (recommended); (8) W4-26 P7 paired (recommended: yes); (9) the port block and CPU budget of the 4-1 dispatch (proposed: at most 4 processes); (10) the offsets stay at -0.43 / -0.90 unless a pre-registered trigger fires (Stage 4 Step 0 (b); recommended: yes).

9. **Revision 8 (sub-round 4-2 pre-flight): what was found and which rulings are needed.** Evidence and file:line references in the stage 4-2 pre-flight note (not published); the interface was re-derived against the list-A head and a compiled prototype (`probes/proto_4_2_sketch.patch`, 7 tests) reproduces the hand values on 12-seed cells. Found: (a) the spec hook of `rebalance_row` leaks `1/13` of every sale and its `dep < 0` branch ignores the extra deposits (D45); (b) purchases through `trade_with_banks` execute 5-45 % in worlds where the banks hold no bonds (4 of 12 seeds), which also distorts the baseline VIX and dollar purchases (D45); (c) `trend_gdp_month` is a stochastic path, not 3.7 % a year, and the tranche must be kept as a deviation to make `tp_FO` exactly zero in the baseline (D44); (d) the CN trend exhausts the CN tranche at month 102, so the +49 bp drift of rev 7 is +19 bp after month 100 with the cap (D44); (e) the tranche capture must be added to the `base_month` branch of `settle`, where the burn-in-end capture is open-coded, not only to `capture_bases` (a world with a burn-in would otherwise capture at its first reported step); (f) the credibility term-premium part must be registered in `after_exog` to reach the shock month's curve, and its tenor shape is open (D46); (g) W4-12 in the ABM's own dollar is -72.98, not -60.3; W4-11 and the fo400 preset are restated by month; (h) W4-14 needs 32 seeds; (i) the "month 0 is continuous" test of the flow is reworded (the first reported month carries the ABM's own dollar step). Rulings needed from the orchestrator, with my recommendation: (1) the per-bloc cap (D44; recommended: yes; the alternative is negative holdings and +49 bp); (2) purchases from the banks then the Fund (D45; recommended: yes); (3) the `rebalance_row` book adjustment (D45; recommended: yes); (4) the `TP_SLOPE`-loaded credibility term (D46; recommended: yes; the literal flat 22 bp would also lift the 3m bill and the 2y rate); (5) the VIX term as the level mean (recommended: keep, [assumed], lever exists); (6) W4-14 on 32 seeds with the mechanism clause (recommended: yes); (7) the seed sets of 3.0 (rev 8) (recommended: yes; list B shares 2001-2064 and the matched cell only); (8) W4-32 reported F when the S4-h lever fires (recommended: yes, the D13/D5 form); (9) the dollar-mode rows deferred as listed in D43 (recommended: yes); (10) two new E rows W4-33 and W4-34 and +22 tests instead of +13 (recommended: yes); (11) `f_vix` is not filled by the finance link (the injections and `f_vix` stay 4-1 functions of the dollar link; recommended: yes).

10. **Revision 10 (sub-round 4-3 pre-flight): what was found and which rulings are needed.** Evidence and file:line references in the stage 4-3 pre-flight note (not published); the interface was re-derived against `cc33f15` and a compiled sketch (`probes/proto_4_3_sketch.patch`, 28 tests) reproduces the hand values on 16-seed event cells and holds the identity to 4e-14 over 240 months. Numbering: this revision sits on list B's revision 9 (4240b34 = the `cc33f15` code plus that spec) and takes D51-D54, stop rules S4-r and S4-s, identity I7, open items 32-35 and rows W4-37 and W4-38 (S4-R34); the code of the two sub-rounds is merged by hand (S4-R27). Found: (a) the research lag formula `max_{k<=lag}` gives no delay for a permanent duty (D51); (b) the W4-24 export statistic must be scored on goods (82 % of the model's exports to China against 74.8 % in 2024; D53), and the expected W4-24b import figure is -26.0 % (probe -25.2), not -30; (c) the NIIP identity is not exact without the ledger's coupon, deposit-interest and auction flows (a negative 3m yield breaks it by 2e-5 of GDP; D52, hooks H19-H21); (d) the positions have no bilateral nominal rates with the dollar link off, and the scale `k` is better taken from the trend GDP; (e) neither switch changes a default run, so the 4-3 prefix of W4-32 is an identity (D54); (f) W4-21's primary income band mixes memo and ledger stocks (restated), and the model's own NIIP is -75 % with a drift to -122 %; (g) the tariff-war preset sits at three W5-04 edges; (h) the section 4 sentence counted 106 rows and 19 E (107 and 20 at `cc33f15`). Rulings needed from the orchestrator, with my recommendation: (1) the lag is a delay (D51; recommended: yes; no gated row depends on it); (2) no tariff end date in 4-3 (open item 32; recommended: defer to stage 5); (3) goods-only retaliation statistics and the metric `exports_goods_bn` (D53; recommended: yes); (4) trend-GDP scale for the memo stocks (D52; recommended: yes); (5) the ABM's broad dollar for the valuation with the dollar link off (D52; recommended: yes); (6) hooks H19-H22 (recommended: yes); (7) the 4-3 prefix of W4-32 as an identity and no new G row (D54; recommended: yes); (8) two new E rows W4-37 and W4-38, stop rules S4-r and S4-s, +28 core, +1 server and +3 cli tests (recommended: yes); (9) the restated W4-21, W4-24b and preset expectations (recommended: yes); (10) the seeds of S4-R25 stand although two smoke runs touched 3001 and 3002 (recommended: keep; the alternative shifts the default set to 3003-3066); (11) a 1-month default lag for the blocs without a research entry (recommended: yes; only RoW matters); (12) the memo yields stay constant (open item 33; recommended: yes); (13) the noise sd 0.02 on `WORLD_FX` index 16 + j (recommended: yes); (14) W4-25 (b) and the dollar-on positions branch wait for list B (recommended: yes).
11. **Revision 11 (stage-5 pre-flight): what was found and which rulings are needed.** Evidence in the stage 5 pre-flight note (not published). (a) Two of the four proposed seed sets were not fresh (6001-6032: 4-2 finance probes; 1301-1364: inside the W3-14 set); sets 6101-6228, 1401-1464, 2301-2332, 10001-10800 and 20001-24000 are registered instead. (b) The default is decided per link on a cumulative ladder; the default set is {block, trade, retaliation, positions}; dollar and finance are profiles; oil is off; the flip itself is one joint switch with the realism program (D55, D61). (c) Re-specified before any run (D56): W1-31 scoring, W2-06 at 48 seeds, W2-10, W3-14 v2, W4-07/W4-08 on `pin7`, the W4-26 convention, W5-04 needs and the exports row, and a near-miss rule (D55). (d) Rulings needed: the seeds (Q1), the W2-15 dM/GDP coin flip (Q2), the default set (Q3), the exports definition (Q4), `pin7` (Q5), TA5 (Q6), the sub-rounds 5-T and 5-U (Q7, Q9), the cost window (Q10), the joint switch (Q11), the near-miss rule (Q12), the lean arm (Q13).

## Revision 4 change log (stage-2 fix round, 2026-10-12; rulings S2-R1..S2-R6)

Source: the stage 2 review note (not published) (independent review of stage 2, head 83b9f40) and `docs/world/DECISIONS.md` (S2-R1..S2-R6). Scope: the trade link. The link ships off by default (S2-R6).

| # | Change |
|---|---|
| 1 | D36 and section 1.6: the trade bases are captured at the **end of the last burn-in month** from the ABM's own values (not the first reported month); the stage-1 bases keep the first reported step. A world without burn-in captures at its first step. |
| 2 | H6 and D36: the ABM's `fx_base` overwrite and the `trade_import_z` reset are **deleted** (F1, F5). The import level `imp_level` is the base-month legacy import level (`goods.import_rel`), so the terms ratio is continuous. |
| 3 | Section 2.5: the export level scale `kappa_s` (the base-month legacy exports over the block's base formula) and the export formula `X = kappa_s x0 ...`; W2-12 restated for the shares by destination within a sector. |
| 4 | Section 2.5: the dynamic terms (dominant-currency prices, `uf`, export FX term) read `zrel = z~ - z~_base` and Energy's competitor term reads `ln world_f - ln world_f0`, so they start at the base-month levels; `z~` itself is reported unchanged. Reason: with the cross-section in levels the first reported month moved the import index by up to 0.4 % (measured). |
| 5 | Section 2.3 and D27 (S2-R5, F3): the coupled import-demand term uses ln(Q/Y) against a slow benchmark: the mean of the first 12 observations, then an EWMA with a 60-month half-life (`BENCH_HALF_LIFE`). q0 and y0 are removed. |
| 6 | Section 2.5 (F4): real imports and exports are deflated by the post_bloc PT ratio (stored in the state). |
| 7 | Section 3 (Stage 2) measurement: the dollar scenarios start at reported month 0 (the month-1 workaround `dollar10m1` is removed); the link-on rows are paired against the matched link-off cell E (S2-R2); W2-01 and W2-02 are judged on the realised real-dollar step (S2-R6: band x mean c_t over the window / 9.53). |
| 8 | Rows: W2-12 restated (see 3); W2-13 paired against E, G2 per S2-R3 (64 seeds: policy limits x2; paired spike count), du12_skew reported not gated (S2-R4); W2-15 band 0.3 pp (F8) and paired against E; new row W2-17 (E, continuity of months 0-2, S2-R2). |
| 9 | Tests (section 3, Tests): `terms_ratio_neutral_at_reported_month_0` inverted to `terms_ratio_continuous_at_reported_month_0`; `bases_recaptured_at_tick_reset` replaced by `bases_captured_at_last_burn_in_month`; new `link_first_month_is_continuous`, `import_demand_benchmark_is_warm_up_mean_then_slow_ewma`, `deflator_is_post_bloc_pt_ratio`; the link-on step tests run on burn-in worlds (F6). Hand-value tests unchanged. |
| 10 | Tooling: `scripts/world_check.py` `--base-port` (F7), `continuity` subcommand, W2-15 band, realised-step bands; `scripts/joint_gates.py --stage2`. |
| 11 | Deviations and interpretations, for the lead: (a) the base month is the last burn-in month, not the first reported month: anchoring at the first reported month would put a month-0 dollar shock inside the anchor and double count it; (b) the cross-section and Energy terms are read relative to the base (item 4); (c) the link stays off by default. |

## Revision 5 change log (stage-3 implementation, 2026-10-12; ruling S3-R1)

Source: `docs/world/DECISIONS.md` (S3-R1, accepted provisionally) and the stage 3 progress note (not published). Scope: the oil link (stage 3).
The link ships off by default; the profile `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s3.json` turns it on for measurement.

| # | Change |
|---|---|
| 1 | Section 2.4, "Interface to the ABM": the decaying splice `G_t = kappa rho^(t+1)` replaces the literal month-0 interface (deviation, the reasons are in the paragraph). `rho` = `oil_ar` by default. |
| 2 | Section 1.5 config: `world_oil_splice_rho` (default -1 = follow `oil_ar`; 1 and 0 are the sensitivities of the stage-3 result). |
| 3 | Section 1.2 `WorldBlock.oil` and section 1.6 metrics: the stage-3 metric group is `p_bar_pct` (market price, offset not removed), `brent_usd`, `oil_supply_pct`, `oil_demand_pct`, `opec_out_mbd`, `us_prod_mbd`, `stocks`, `spare_pct`, and the diagnostic `oil_dev_pct` (the ABM deviation A_t). |
| 4 | Section 2.4 `OPEC+ regime market_share` (not specified in the research prototype): the floor rule is off and OPEC+ output rises by `supply.opec.market_share_unwind_rate` (0.37 mb/d per month) until spare capacity (assumption; no gate row depends on it). |
| 5 | Section 2.4 UAE switch (D7): with `world_uae_in_opec` false the OPEC+ liquids divisor is the UAE-out liquids (EIA 53-1 UAE 4.24 mb/d removed) and the spare capacity is reduced by `world_opec_uae_spare_share` (0.30, [assumed]). |
| 6 | Section 2.4 `OilSupply` path: ramp over `ramp_months`, hold `hold_months` at the full loss, restore linearly over `recover_months` (Hormuz 12, 1, 3, 6 gives 12, 12, 12, 10, 8, 6, 4, 2, 0: research K7). Test `supply_path_matches_research_hormuz_path`. |
| 7 | Section 3 stage 3 measurement: the isolated oil rows use the research check table as bands (`data/world/research/oil/data/oil_model_irf_check.csv`, copied into the repo; the golden `bands`). The ABM oil scenarios of the measurement cells are scheduled at reported month 12 (review, ZLB note). |
| 8 | Section 2.4 random draws: the jump count is an exact Poisson draw (the research thresholds at two jumps); the difference is inside the bands (K2-K5 re-run in `oil-check`). |
| 9 | Section 2.4 `OilState::step` is the aggregate of the prototype when run with one region; the integrated run uses thirteen regions with D12 coefficients (`eta0_i = eta0 phi_i / phibar`). |

## Revision 6 change log (stage-3 fix round, 2026-10-12; rulings S3-R2..S3-R9)

Source: `docs/world/DECISIONS.md` (S3-R2..S3-R9) and the stage 3 fix-round progress note (not published); the measured results are in CALIBRATION, "Fix round". Scope: the stage-3 fix list (the stage 3 review note (not published) section 7). The link ships off; the shipped profile `scripts/profiles/b8-world-s3.json` keeps it off and carries the two pre-authorised levers.

| # | Change |
|---|---|
| 1 | Section 2.4 splice (S3-R4, fix step 2): the base-month gap is taken from the baseline `oil_ar` path, so `kappa = L_b - (p_b - offset)` no longer contains the user shock. In a matched pair the month-0 gap cancels (test `splice_kappa_cancels_without_burn_in_month0_shock`). |
| 2 | Section 2.4 `world_oil_feedback` (S3-R2, fix step 3): default true. False gives the exact legacy path, with no bloc feedback into the market (test `exact_path_pair_difference_is_the_shock_without_feedback`). The shocked price is lower with feedback (test `feedback_lowers_the_shocked_price`). W3-10 is gated with feedback false; W3-10r (feedback on) is reported. |
| 3 | Section 1.5 config (fix steps 7 and 10): `world_oil_shock_mask` (default `IDO`; the W3-19 variance-share cells), `world_oil_qty_offset` (-6.627, rule 6), and `world_oil_feedback`. The key `world_oil_sigI_mult` becomes `world_oil_sig_i_mult`: one spelling for the field and the JSON key. The old spelling is rejected by the CLI overlay (`ECONSIM_CFG`, which panics on an unknown key; checked 2026-10-12); the server JSON path was not checked and `Config` has no `deny_unknown_fields`. |
| 4 | Section 1.6 metrics (fix step 7, W3-18): `oil_us_demand_pct`, the market's US-region demand deviation in % of Q (`#[serde(default)]`, so older dumps still read). |
| 5 | Section 4 validation (fix step 10): `validate_world_oil` rejects an unknown `world_oil_profile` and an unknown `world_opec_regime`, and the shock mask accepts only I, D and O. The server answers 400 (checked live). |
| 6 | Section 2.4 OPEC+ quantity channel (S3-R8 option b, fix step 9): `Q_t = q_t - world_oil_qty_offset + G^q_t`, with `kappa_q` taken from the base-month quantity. The offset applies only when `stochastic_oil` is on (measured -6.627 on seeds 1-200; check -7.631 on seeds 201-400). Tests `splice_qty_kappa_cancels_in_matched_pair`, `splice_qty_month_zero_is_continuous`, `qty_offset_only_when_stochastic`. |
| 7 | Section 1.5 shipped profile (fix step 5): `world_oil_exact_path` is removed from the shipped profile (the exact path is a test interface); `world_oil_sig_i_mult` 1.1 and `world_oil_rho_q` 0.995 (the two levers); link off. |
| 8 | Section 3, W3-11: the m24 band is [-1.1, -0.5] (sign corrected in rev 6; the master value is -0.61). The 48-seed result is in CALIBRATION: CPI m12 0.909 (SE 0.058) against [1.0, 1.4], outside the band. Recorded, not re-decided here. |
| 9 | Section 3, W3-14 (S3-R3, fix step 12): the statistic is the ratio of cross-seed means over months 12-239 against the matched link-off cell, with a delta-method SE. Decision on fresh seeds 1001-1400 (1000 households, 240 months, shipped levers): -4.89 % (SE 1.63), FAIL against +-3 %. The 20000-household paired check on seeds 1001-1032: -13.70 % (SE 5.47), FAIL. S3-d stands; the offset 8.355 is unchanged. |
| 10 | Section 3, W3-15 is reported on both series (ABM `oil_lvl` and the market-only `oil_lvl`), on 128 seeds of 1000 households with the shipped levers: 0.2844 and 0.2712 against the floor 0.27. |
| 11 | Stop rules: S3-c withdrawn (the W3-11 failure was a horizon convention error; the W3-10 h12 failure is the structural copy-A feedback, which is gated away by `world_oil_feedback` false). S3-d re-decided (item 9). |
| 12 | Section 2.4 exact-path feedback numbers: the feedback is 2 % of the shock at 3 months, 16-18 % at 12 months and 46 % at 24 months for OilPrice +50 %. The exact-path decision is an open item for stage 5. |
| 13 | Section 2.4 interface, OPEC+ quantity channel paragraph (item 6). |
| 14 | `docs/API.md`: the stage-3 config list uses `world_oil_sig_i_mult`; the fix-round keys are added. |
